# 六爻 · 事业 · 成否

*Liu Yao (six-line coin divination) · Career and office · Will it come: seeking a post, a commission, a recommendation; easy or hard*

86 条。原文逐字核对过 `functions/_lib/doctrine/books/` 下的文本,行号是那份整理本的行号,不是原刻本的页码。「规则(整理)」是本库的归纳,引用时引原文。

## LY.CAREER.BSQS.02 通玄妙论·绝处逢生·占官：父母绝处逢生，文书虽有阻节，终有贵人主张，其事必成

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:求官时遇到父母爻绝处逢生（它落在日辰的绝地，又得动爻来生）：文书上虽然有阻滞卡节，终究有贵人替你做主，事情必成。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** In divining for office, if the Resource line meets life in the place of extinction (it falls where the Day extinguishes it but a moving line gives it life): though the documents meet delays and hold-ups, a noble will in the end take charge and the matter will surely succeed.

**《卜筮全书·通玄妙论·二、绝处逢生》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 2806 行 · *Bushi Quanshu (Complete Book of Divination), "Tongxuan Miaolun, no. 2: life from the place of extinction", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 2806*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 且如申日占卦，遇用爻属木，木见申则绝，木爻无用矣。 | 比如申日占卦，用爻属木，木到申上就是绝地，这个木爻就没有用了。 | Suppose a divination on a Shen day in which the Subject line belongs to Wood: Wood meets extinction at Shen, and the Wood line is of no use. |
| 若得水爻发动来相生，木爻仍复有用。 | 如果有水爻发动来生它，木爻又有了用处。 | If a Water line moves to give it life, the Wood line is useful again. |
| 譬人当困穷之际，得遇贵人扶持，必有寒谷回春之象，此乃是绝处逢生也。 | 就像人在穷困的时候，遇到贵人扶持，一定有寒谷回春的景象，这就叫绝处逢生。 | It is like a person in hardship who meets a noble and is helped: surely there is the look of spring returning to a cold valley; this is what is meant by life from the place of extinction. |

**《卜筮全书·通玄妙论·二、绝处逢生》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 2810 行 · *Bushi Quanshu (Complete Book of Divination), "Tongxuan Miaolun, no. 2: life from the place of extinction", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 2810*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 占官： | 占求官： | In divining for office: |
| 遇父母绝处逢生， | 遇到父母爻绝处逢生， | if the Resource line finds life in extinction, |
| 文书虽有阻节， | 文书上虽然有阻滞卡节， | though the documents meet delays and hold-ups, |
| 终有贵人主张， | 终究有贵人替你做主， | a noble will in the end take charge, |
| 其事必成。 | 这件事一定成。 | the matter will surely succeed. |

> **按**:这一段后面接着讲占讼、出行（那两件放在官非、出行域）。「绝处逢生」的做法见第一个引文：用爻落在绝地，有动爻来生才算。
>
> **Note:** The passage goes on to lawsuits and travel, which belong to the lawsuit and travel domains. How "life in extinction" works is in the first quotation: the Subject line falls in a place of extinction and a moving line must give it life.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《断易天机》:同文 第 56 行:且如申日占，卦遇用爻属木，木见申则绝，木爻无用矣;异文(相似 0.97) 第 56 行:如人当困穷之际，得遇贵人扶持，必有寒谷回春之象，此乃是绝处逢生也
- 《火珠林》:同文 第 821 行:且如申日占卦，遇用爻属木，木见申则绝，木爻无用矣;同文 第 821 行:若得水爻发动来相生，木爻仍复有用;异文(相似 0.97) 第 821 行:譬人当困穷之际，得遇贵人扶持，必有寒谷回春之象，此乃绝处逢生也
- 《断易天机》:同文 第 59 行:遇父母绝处逢生;同文 第 59 行:文书虽有阻节;同文 第 59 行:终有贵人主张
- 《火珠林》:同文 第 823 行:遇父母绝处逢生;同文 第 823 行:文书虽有阻节;同文 第 823 行:终有贵人主张

## LY.CAREER.BSQS.03 通玄妙论·合处逢冲·占官遇官鬼文书暗冲尚有反复变动；谋事遇之必因人阻滞将成而有变

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:合处逢冲（卦本来相合，却被日辰冲了）：求官时官鬼、文书被暗中冲着，事里还有反复和变动；谋事遇到这样，一定因为别人而受阻，眼看要成又有变。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** A clash at a place of combination (a hexagram of combination that is clashed by the Day): in divining for office, if the Pressure line and the documents are clashed in secret, the matter still has reversals and changes; in planning a matter, meeting this means being held up by someone, and just as it is about to succeed there is a change.

**《卜筮全书·通玄妙论·三、合处逢冲》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 2817 行 · *Bushi Quanshu (Complete Book of Divination), "Tongxuan Miaolun, no. 3: a clash at a place of combination", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 2817*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 且如占得雷地豫卦。 | 比如占到雷地豫卦。 | Suppose one gets the hexagram Thunder over Earth, Yu. |
| 世应相生，六爻相合，吉无不利，乃事事可成之象也。 | 世应相生，六爻相合，吉利没有不顺的，是事事都能成的卦象。 | The Self and Other give each other life and the six lines combine; all is auspicious and nothing unfavourable, the sign of every matter being achievable. |
| 却在子日占得，子冲应上午、害世上未，此乃合处逢冲也。 | 却是在子日占到的，子冲应爻上的午、害世爻上的未，这就是合处逢冲。 | But if it is got on a Zi day, Zi clashes the Wu on the Other line and harms the Wei on the Self line: this is a clash at a place of combination. |

**《卜筮全书·通玄妙论·三、合处逢冲》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 2818 行 · *Bushi Quanshu (Complete Book of Divination), "Tongxuan Miaolun, no. 3: a clash at a place of combination", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 2818*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 占官遇官鬼文书暗冲， | 占求官时遇到官鬼和文书被暗中冲着， | In divining for office, when the Pressure line and the documents are clashed in secret, |
| 其中尚有反复变动。 | 里面还有反复和变动。 | there are still reversals and changes within it. |

**《卜筮全书·通玄妙论·三、合处逢冲》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 2819 行 · *Bushi Quanshu (Complete Book of Divination), "Tongxuan Miaolun, no. 3: a clash at a place of combination", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 2819*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 谋事遇之，必因人阻滞，将成而有变。 | 谋事遇到这种情形，一定因为别人而受阻滞，快成的时候会有变化。 | In planning a matter, meeting this means being held up by someone, and just as it is about to succeed there is a change. |

> **按**:同一段还讲占婚、占财，以及占讼与病「喜遇合处逢冲」（合则事必成，逢冲灾必散），那几件放在别的问题域。「官鬼文书暗冲」是官鬼和文书（父母）这两爻被日辰暗冲，书里没有展开，译文照字面。
>
> **Note:** The same passage goes on to marriage and wealth, and says that lawsuits and illness are glad to meet a clash at a place of combination (combination means the matter surely succeeds, a clash means the calamity disperses); those go to other domains. "The Pressure line and the documents clashed in secret" means the Pressure and Resource lines are clashed by the Day without moving; the book does not expand on it and the translation follows the words.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《断易天机》:同文 第 68 行:且如占得雷地豫卦;同文 第 68 行:世应相生，六爻相合，吉无不利，乃事事可成之象也;同文 第 68 行:却在子日占得，子冲应上午、害世上未，此乃合处逢冲也
- 《火珠林》:同文 第 828 行:且如占得雷地豫卦;同文 第 828 行:世应相生，六爻相合，吉无不利，乃事事可成之象也;同文 第 828 行:却在子日占得，子冲应上午、害世上未，此乃合处逢冲也
- 《火珠林》:同文 第 828 行:占官遇官鬼文书暗冲;同文 第 828 行:其中尚有反复变动
- 《断易天机》:异文(相似 0.93) 第 72 行:谋事遇之，必因人阻滞，事将成而有变
- 《火珠林》:同文 第 828 行:谋事遇之，必因人阻滞，将成而有变

## LY.CAREER.BSQS.08 断易总论·财官若遇生扶谋望并无阻滞；用官忌子孙持世；子孙兴发求官应举枉劳心

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:求官时，财爻、官鬼如果得到生扶，谋划的事没有阻滞；卦身和世爻没有救助，做的事一定彼此背离。用官鬼做用神的，忌子孙持世；子孙兴旺发动，求官、应举都是白费心。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** In seeking office, if Wealth and Pressure are given life and support, plans meet no obstruction; if the Body and the Self have no rescue or help, what one does will surely run to discord. When Pressure is the Subject, Output holding the Self is feared; if Output flourishes and moves, seeking office or taking the examinations is a waste of effort.

**《卜筮全书·通玄妙论·八、断易总论》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 2880 行 · *Bushi Quanshu (Complete Book of Divination), "Tongxuan Miaolun, no. 8: general discussion of Duanyi", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 2880*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 用官忌子孙持世， | 用官鬼做用神的，忌子孙持世； | When Pressure is the Subject, Output holding the Self is feared; |
| 求财忌兄弟临身。 | 求财的，忌兄弟临身。 | in seeking wealth, Peer on the Body is feared. |

**《卜筮全书·通玄妙论·八、断易总论》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 2881 行 · *Bushi Quanshu (Complete Book of Divination), "Tongxuan Miaolun, no. 8: general discussion of Duanyi", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 2881*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 财官若遇生扶， | 财爻、官鬼如果得到生扶， | If Wealth and Pressure are given life and support, |
| 谋望并无阻滞。 | 谋划和指望的事没有阻滞。 | plans and hopes meet no obstruction. |
| 身世若无救助， | 卦身和世爻如果没有救助， | If the Body and the Self have no rescue or help, |
| 所为必主参商。 | 所做的事一定彼此背离、合不到一起。 | what one does will surely run to discord and not come together. |

**《卜筮全书·通玄妙论·八、断易总论》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 2881 行 · *Bushi Quanshu (Complete Book of Divination), "Tongxuan Miaolun, no. 8: general discussion of Duanyi", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 2881*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 子孙兴发， | 子孙兴旺发动， | If Output flourishes and moves, |
| 求官应举枉劳心。 | 求官、应举都是白费心思。 | seeking office or taking the examinations is a waste of effort. |

> **按**:「财官若遇生扶」里的财是妻财、官是官鬼（原文写作「财官」）；「参商」是参星和商星，此出彼没，比喻彼此背离。同一行里「兄弟动摇，买婢求财空费力」是求财，放在求财域。「应举」是考试，这一条同时属考试域。「用官忌子孙持世」是说官鬼做用神时怕子孙持世，因为子孙克官鬼。
>
> **Note:** "Wealth and Pressure" are the Wealth and Pressure lines (the text writes them as "wealth-official"); "Shen and Shang" are two stars that never appear together, a figure for people at odds. In the same line, "if Peer moves, buying a maid or seeking wealth is wasted effort" concerns seeking wealth and is in the wealth domain. "Taking the examinations" is also an examination matter, so this entry belongs to the examination domain as well. "When Pressure is the Subject, Output holding the Self is feared" is because Output controls Pressure.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《增删卜易》:异文(相似 0.86) 第 8488 行:用。最忌子孙持世
- 《断易天机》:同文 第 871 行:用官忌子孙持世
- 《火珠林》:同文 第 866 行:用官忌子孙持世;同文 第 866 行:求财忌兄弟临身
- 《火珠林》:同文 第 866 行:财官若遇生扶;同文 第 866 行:谋望并无阻滞;同文 第 866 行:身世若无救助;同文 第 866 行:所为必主参商
- 《火珠林》:同文 第 866 行:求官应举枉劳心

相关:LY.CAREER.ZSZ.06

## LY.CAREER.BSQS.12 断易通玄赋·父母爻兴旺，为官至侯王（旧注：印旺主大贵）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:父母爻兴旺，做官能做到侯王；旧注说，印（父母）旺，主有大贵。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If the Resource line flourishes, one who holds office rises to marquis or prince; the old note says that when the Seal (Resource) is strong, great nobility is indicated.

**《卜筮全书·断易通玄赋》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 2997 行 · *Bushi Quanshu (Complete Book of Divination), "The Duanyi Tongxuan Fu", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 2997*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 父母爻兴旺，为官至侯王。 | 父母爻兴旺，做官能做到侯王。 | If the Resource line flourishes, one who holds office rises to marquis or prince. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 2998 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 2998*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 印旺主大贵。 | 印旺，主有大贵。 | When the Seal is strong, great nobility is indicated. |

> **按**:「印」是印绶，也就是父母爻（文书、印信），官印旺就是父母旺。同一联前半「占宅青龙旺，豪富冠一乡」是占宅，放在家宅。后面的虎易评注说这两句旧注太简略，那是今人的话，不收。
>
> **Note:** The "Seal" is the seal of office, that is, the Resource line (documents and seals); a strong seal means a strong Resource line. The first half of the couplet, "in divining the house, a strong Azure Dragon makes one rich above all in the village", is about the house and goes to the house domain. The later remark by Hu Yi that these notes are too brief is modern commentary and is not quoted.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 187 行:父母爻兴旺，为官至侯王
- 《断易天机》:同文 第 2822 行:父母爻兴旺，为官至侯王
- 《火珠林》:同文 第 924 行:父母爻兴旺，为官至侯王

## LY.CAREER.CA.06 申酉辰戌持世为吉；当官遇禄好施为（申酉持世兼得禄最吉）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:申、酉、辰、戌的爻持世是吉；做官又遇上禄，正好施展；其中申、酉持世又得禄，最吉。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The branches Shen, You, Chen and Xu holding the Self are auspicious; taking office and meeting the Emolument gives room to act; Shen or You holding the Self and getting the Emolument is the most auspicious.

**《阐奥歌章·仕宦章》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 3743 行 · *Chan'ao Gezhang (Songs Expounding the Mysteries), "Verse on holding office", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 3743*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 申酉辰戌持世吉， | 申、酉、辰、戌持世是吉， | Shen, You, Chen and Xu holding the Self is auspicious; |
| 任官遇禄好施为。 | 做官又遇到禄，好施展作为。 | taking office and meeting the Emolument, there is room to act well. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 3744 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 3744*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 申酉持世及得禄，最吉也。 | 申、酉持世，再得到禄，最吉。 | Shen or You holding the Self and also getting the Emolument is the most auspicious. |

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《断易天机》:同文 第 2585 行:申酉辰戌持世吉;同文 第 2585 行:任官遇禄好施为
- 《火珠林》:同文 第 1220 行:申酉辰戌持世吉;同文 第 1220 行:任官遇禄好施为

相关:LY.CAREER.DYTJ.02

## LY.CAREER.CA.07 官鬼临身世、卦爻旺相才是得官的真兆；驿马空亡徒费力，卦遭胎没枉劳神

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:官鬼落在卦身或世爻上、卦里各爻旺相，才是真的得官，可喜；驿马落空亡，白费力气；卦遇胎、没，白白劳神。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Pressure standing on the Body or the Self, with the lines of the hexagram thriving, is the true sign of getting the office and is cause for joy; the Post Horse Void means effort spent for nothing; a hexagram meeting Embryo or Sunk means wearying the mind in vain.

**《阐奥歌章·仕宦章》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 3745 行 · *Chan'ao Gezhang (Songs Expounding the Mysteries), "Verse on holding office", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 3745*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 鬼临身世得官真， | 官鬼落在身、世上，得到官才是真的； | Pressure on the Body or the Self, and the office is truly gained; |
| 卦爻旺相为欢欣。 | 卦里的爻旺相，是欢喜的事。 | the lines of the hexagram strong and thriving, there is cause for joy. |
| 驿马空亡徒费力， | 驿马空亡，白费力气； | the Post Horse Void, effort is spent for nothing; |
| 卦遭胎没枉劳神。 | 卦遇胎、没，白白劳神。 | the hexagram meeting Embryo or Sunk, the mind is wearied in vain. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 3746 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 3746*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 若卦遇胎没，定枉劳神。 | 若是卦遇到胎、没，必定白白劳神。 | If the hexagram meets Embryo or Sunk, it surely means wearing the mind in vain. |
| 立春后巽胎离没，若得家人卦之类是也。 | 立春之后，巽是胎、离是没，如果得到家人卦这一类，就是这种情形。 | After the Beginning of Spring, Xun is Embryo and Li is Sunk; getting a hexagram such as Jiaren is a case of it. |

> **按**:胎、没是八卦随节气轮转的八个状态（旺、相、胎、没、死、囚、休、废）里的两个，这里的旧注只举了立春后的一例。大易断例卜筮元龟目说「胎没二卦，春巽离，夏坤兑，秋乾坎，冬艮震」，春天是巽、离，和旧注的例子相合。
>
> **Note:** Embryo and Sunk are two of the eight states through which the trigrams turn with the solar terms (thriving, mutual, embryo, sunk, dead, trapped, resting, abandoned); the note here gives only one example, after the Beginning of Spring. The Dayi Duanli says 'the two trigrams of embryo and sunk: in spring Xun and Li, in summer Kun and Dui, in autumn Qian and Kan, in winter Gen and Zhen', which agrees with the note's example for spring.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《断易天机》:同文 第 2585 行:鬼临身世得官真;异文(相似 0.86) 第 2585 行:卦爻旺相甚欢欣;同文 第 2585 行:驿马空亡徒费力;同文 第 2585 行:卦遭胎没枉劳神

## LY.CAREER.CA.13 问文官武职，求官必须用唐符、国印：按太岁年支取——寅申年巳亥、巳亥年寅申、子午年卯酉、卯酉年子午、辰戌年丑未、丑未年辰戌

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:不论文官武职，求官都要看唐符、国印。按年支取：寅、申年，巳是唐符、亥是国印（旧注）；巳、亥年取寅、申；子、午年取卯、酉；卯、酉年取子、午；辰、戌年取丑、未；丑、未年取辰、戌。旧注说「以下类推」，这里按此读成每一对里前一支是唐符、后一支是国印。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Whether it is a civil or a military post, in seeking office one must look to Tangfu and the State Seal. By the year branch: in the years Yin and Shen, Si is the Tangfu and Hai the State Seal (the note); in Si and Hai years take Yin and Shen; in Zi and Wu years, Mao and You; in Mao and You years, Zi and Wu; in Chen and Xu years, Chou and Wei; in Chou and Wei years, Chen and Xu. The note says the rest follow by analogy, which is read here as the first branch of each pair being the Tangfu and the second the State Seal.

**《阐奥歌章·仕宦章》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 3763 行 · *Chan'ao Gezhang (Songs Expounding the Mysteries), "Verse on holding office", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 3763*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 若问文官并武职， | 若是问文官和武职， | If the question is a civil post or a military one, |
| 唐符国印求端的。 | 唐符、国印是求得确实答案的地方。 | Tangfu and the State Seal are where the sure answer is found. |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 3763 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 3763*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 唐符国印，求官必须用之。 | 唐符、国印，求官必须用上。 | Tangfu and the State Seal: in seeking office they must be used. |

**《阐奥歌章·仕宦章》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 3764 行 · *Chan'ao Gezhang (Songs Expounding the Mysteries), "Verse on holding office", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 3764*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 寅申之年巳亥为， | 寅、申年，取巳、亥。 | In the years Yin and Shen, it is Si and Hai; |

**旧注(《卜筮全书》所载,注者不详)** — 《卜筮大全》第 3765 行 · *Old commentary (printed in the Bushi Quanshu; commentator unknown); Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 3765*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 寅申之年，巳为唐符，亥为国印，以下类推。 | 寅、申年，巳是唐符，亥是国印，以下依此类推。 | In the years Yin and Shen, Si is the Tangfu and Hai the State Seal; the rest follow by analogy. |

**《阐奥歌章·仕宦章》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 3766 行 · *Chan'ao Gezhang (Songs Expounding the Mysteries), "Verse on holding office", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 3766*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 巳亥之年寅申觅。 | 巳、亥年，到寅、申里找。 | In the years Si and Hai, look among Yin and Shen. |
| 子午之年卯酉求， | 子、午年，到卯、酉里求； | In the years Zi and Wu, seek among Mao and You; |
| 卯酉之年子午出。 | 卯、酉年，子、午出现。 | in the years Mao and You, Zi and Wu appear. |
| 辰戌之年丑未方， | 辰、戌年，在丑、未的方位； | In the years Chen and Xu, it is in the place of Chou and Wei; |
| 丑未之年在辰戌。 | 丑、未年，在辰、戌。 | in the years Chou and Wei, it is in Chen and Xu. |

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《断易天机》:同文 第 2585 行:若问文官并武职;异文(相似 0.86) 第 2585 行:唐苻国印求端的
- 《火珠林》:同文 第 1232 行:若问文官并武职;同文 第 1232 行:唐符国印求端的
- 《断易天机》:同文 第 2585 行:寅申之年巳亥为
- 《火珠林》:同文 第 1233 行:寅申之年巳亥为
- 《断易天机》:同文 第 2585 行:巳亥之年寅申觅;同文 第 2585 行:子午之年卯酉求;同文 第 2585 行:卯酉之年子午出;同文 第 2585 行:辰戌之年丑未方;同文 第 2585 行:丑未之年在辰戌
- 《火珠林》:同文 第 1236 行:巳亥之年寅申觅;同文 第 1236 行:子午之年卯酉求;同文 第 1236 行:卯酉之年子午出;同文 第 1236 行:辰戌之年丑未方;同文 第 1236 行:丑未之年在辰戌

## LY.CAREER.CA.27 僧道占：财旺宜收布施、鬼交偏好谒官员；财空注疏徒劳力、鬼旺营谋枉费钱

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:财旺，正适合收布施；官鬼发动，偏适合去谒见官员；财落空，注解经疏是白费力气；官鬼旺，营谋是白费钱。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Wealth thriving is just right for receiving alms; Pressure moving is especially good for calling on officials; Wealth void, annotating the scriptures is wasted labour; Pressure strong, scheming is money wasted.

**《阐奥歌章·僧道章》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 3835 行 · *Chan'ao Gezhang (Songs Expounding the Mysteries), "Verse on monks and Daoists", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 3835*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 财旺正宜收布施， | 财旺，正适合收取布施； | Wealth thriving is just right for receiving alms; |
| 鬼交偏好谒官员。 | 官鬼发动，偏适合去谒见官员。 | Pressure moving is especially good for calling on officials. |

**《阐奥歌章·僧道章》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 3835 行 · *Chan'ao Gezhang (Songs Expounding the Mysteries), "Verse on monks and Daoists", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 3835*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 财空注疏徒劳力， | 财落空，注解经疏是白费力气； | Wealth void, annotating the scriptures is wasted labour; |
| 鬼旺营谋枉费钱。 | 官鬼旺，谋求营运是白白费钱。 | Pressure strong, scheming and managing is money wasted. |

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《周易尚占》:同文 第 677 行:财空注疏徒劳力;同文 第 677 行:鬼旺营谋枉费钱
- 《火珠林》:同文 第 1278 行:财空注疏徒劳力;同文 第 1278 行:鬼旺营谋枉费钱

## LY.CAREER.CA.32 僧道占成道之象：世空身旺为地行仙、身空不动头头是道、应旺无魔处处是禅；应偶世奇生合、神清身静寿绵绵

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:世空、身旺，是地上的行仙；身空而不动，事事都是道；应旺而没有魔，处处都是禅；应落偶位、世落奇位，又生又合，精神清明、身体安静，寿命绵长。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The Self void and the Body strong makes an earth-wandering immortal; the Body void and still, every single thing is the Way; the Other strong with no demon, Chan is everywhere; the Other on an even place and the Self on an odd one, generating and combining, the spirit clear and the body at peace, life is long.

**《阐奥歌章·僧道章》原文** — 《卜筮大全》第 3839 行 · *Chan'ao Gezhang (Songs Expounding the Mysteries), "Verse on monks and Daoists", base text; Bushi Daquan (= Bushi Quanshu), line 3839*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 世空身旺地行仙。 | 世空、身旺，是地上的行仙； | the Self void and the Body strong, an earth-wandering immortal; |
| 身空不动头头道， | 身空而不动，事事都是道； | the Body void and still, every single thing is the Way; |
| 应旺无魔处处禅。 | 应旺而没有魔，处处都是禅； | the Other strong with no demon, Chan is everywhere; |
| 应偶世奇生又合， | 应落偶位、世落奇位，又生又合， | the Other on an even place and the Self on an odd one, generating and combining, |
| 神清身静寿绵绵。 | 精神清明、身体安静，寿命绵长。 | the spirit clear and the body at peace, life is long. |

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《周易尚占》:同文 第 680 行:世空身旺地行仙;同文 第 680 行:身空不动头头道;同文 第 680 行:应旺无魔处处禅;异文(相似 0.86) 第 681 行:应偶世持生又合;同文 第 681 行:神清身静寿绵绵
- 《火珠林》:同文 第 1278 行:世空身旺地行仙;同文 第 1278 行:身空不动头头道;同文 第 1278 行:应旺无魔处处禅;同文 第 1278 行:应偶世奇生又合;同文 第 1278 行:神清身静寿绵绵

相关:LY.CAREER.YM.101、LY.CAREER.YM.92

## LY.CAREER.DYTJ.03 丘寺丞易鉴歌（二）：鬼临身世、卦爻旺相得官真而欢欣；驿马空亡徒费力、卦遭胎没枉劳神；旺爻克应不良、应入墓忧死亡；世爻受克遭贬谪、若克他人倍俸粮

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:官鬼落在身、世上，卦里各爻旺相，得官是真，欢喜；驿马落空亡，白费力气；卦遇胎、没，白白劳神。旺相的爻克应，不好；应入墓的时候，要担心死亡；世爻被克，会遭贬谪；如果世爻去克别人，俸禄加倍。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Pressure on the Body or the Self with the lines of the hexagram strong and thriving, the office is truly gained and there is joy; the Post Horse Void, effort is spent for nothing; the hexagram meeting Embryo or Sunk, the mind is wearied in vain. A strong line controlling the Other is not good; when the Other enters its tomb, worry about death; the Self line being controlled, one is demoted and exiled; if the Self controls others, the salary is doubled.

**《断易天机·占仕宦第十一》原文** — 《断易天机》第 2585 行 · *Duanyi Tianji (Heavenly Secrets of Judging the Changes), "Chapter 11: Divining official careers", base text; Duanyi Tianji, line 2585*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 鬼临身世得官真， | 官鬼落在身、世上，得到官才是真的； | Pressure on the Body or the Self, and the office is truly gained; |
| 卦爻旺相甚欢欣， | 卦里各爻旺相，十分欢欣； | the lines of the hexagram strong and thriving, great joy; |
| 驿马空亡徒费力， | 驿马空亡，白费力气； | the Post Horse Void, effort is spent for nothing; |
| 卦遭胎没枉劳神（2）。 | 卦遇胎、没，白白劳神。 | the hexagram meeting Embryo or Sunk, the mind is wearied in vain. |

**《断易天机》注(注者不详)** — 《断易天机》第 2589 行 · *Duanyi Tianji commentary (commentator unknown); Duanyi Tianji, line 2589*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| （2）若卦遇胎没，定枉劳神。 | （2）如果卦遇到胎、没，必定白白劳神。 | (2) If the hexagram meets Embryo or Sunk, it surely means wearing the mind in vain. |
| 如立春后巽胎离没，得家人卦是也。 | 比如立春之后，巽是胎、离是没，得到家人卦就是这种情形。 | For example after the Beginning of Spring, Xun is Embryo and Li is Sunk, and getting the hexagram Jiaren is such a case. |

**《断易天机·占仕宦第十一》原文** — 《断易天机》第 2585 行 · *Duanyi Tianji (Heavenly Secrets of Judging the Changes), "Chapter 11: Divining official careers", base text; Duanyi Tianji, line 2585*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 旺爻克应是不良， | 旺相的爻克应，是不好的； | A strong line controlling the Other is not good; |
| 应入墓时忧死亡， | 应入墓的时候，要担心死亡； | when the Other enters its tomb, worry about death; |
| 世爻受克遭贬谪， | 世爻被克，会遭贬谪； | the Self line being controlled, one is demoted and exiled; |
| 若克他人倍俸粮。 | 如果克了别人，俸禄加倍。 | if it controls others, the salary is doubled. |

> **按**:卜筮大全本作「卦爻旺相为欢欣」「若克他爻倍俸粮」，断易天机本作「卦爻旺相甚欢欣」「若克他人倍俸粮」，意思没有差别。
>
> **Note:** The Bushi Daquan text reads 'the lines strong, there is joy' and 'if it controls another line, the salary is doubled'; the Duanyi Tianji text reads 'the lines strong, great joy' and 'if it controls others, the salary is doubled'; the sense is the same.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮大全》:同文 第 3745 行:鬼临身世得官真;异文(相似 0.86) 第 3745 行:卦爻旺相为欢欣;同文 第 3745 行:驿马空亡徒费力;异文(相似 0.88) 第 3745 行:卦遭胎没枉劳神
- 《火珠林》:同文 第 1222 行:鬼临身世得官真;同文 第 1222 行:驿马空亡徒费力;异文(相似 0.88) 第 1222 行:卦遭胎没枉劳神
- 《卜筮大全》:同文 第 3747 行:旺爻克应是不良;同文 第 3747 行:应入墓时忧死亡;同文 第 3747 行:世爻受克遭贬谪;异文(相似 0.86) 第 3747 行:若克他爻倍俸粮
- 《火珠林》:同文 第 1223 行:旺爻克应是不良;同文 第 1223 行:应入墓时忧死亡;同文 第 1224 行:世爻受克遭贬谪;异文(相似 0.86) 第 1224 行:若克他爻倍俸粮

相关:LY.CAREER.CA.07、LY.CAREER.CA.08

## LY.CAREER.DYTJ.11 卜筮元龟·求官总断：抑身扶世求官得，天驿相生允不刻，更逢鬼旺讵相疑（例：二月坤之升卦）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:求官，压住身、扶助世就能得官；天驿互相生扶，允许不用拖延；再逢官鬼旺相，哪里还有怀疑——例如二月占得坤之升卦。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** In seeking office, with the Body held down and the Self supported the office is gained; with the Heavenly Post mutually generating, it is promised there will be no delay; and with Pressure also strong how could there be doubt — as for example in the second month the hexagram Kun changing to Sheng.

**《断易天机·占仕宦第十一》原文** — 《断易天机》第 2668 行 · *Duanyi Tianji (Heavenly Secrets of Judging the Changes), "Chapter 11: Divining official careers", base text; Duanyi Tianji, line 2668*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 抑身扶世求官得， | 压住身、扶助世，求官就能得到； | with the Body held down and the Self supported, the office is gained; |
| 天驿相生允不刻， | 天驿互相生扶，允许不用拖延； | with the Heavenly Post mutually generating, it is promised there will be no delay; |
| 更逢鬼旺讵相疑， | 再逢官鬼旺相，哪里还有怀疑； | and meeting a strong Pressure besides, how could there be doubt; |
| 二月坤之升卦是。 | 二月占得坤之升卦就是这样。 | in the second month the hexagram Kun changing to Sheng is such a case. |

> **按**:「抑身扶世」没说是哪一爻压住身、哪一爻扶世；「天驿」没有解释，可能指天喜、驿马，这里照字面译成 Heavenly Post；「允不刻」的「不刻」也没有解释，这里读作「不拖延」，不确定。「卜筮元龟」是断易天机转引的书名。
>
> **Note:** The text does not say which line holds down the Body or supports the Self; 'tianyi' is not explained and may mean the Joy star and the Post Horse, and it is rendered literally as Heavenly Post; the 'bu ke' of 'it is promised not to be ...' is not explained either and is read here as 'no delay', which is uncertain. 'Bushi Yuangui' is the name of a book quoted by the Duanyi Tianji.

相关:LY.CAREER.YY.07、LY.CAREER.HJC.05

---

## LY.CAREER.DYTJ.24 洞林秘诀·求官（二）：鬼爻旺相无刑克、遇扶持则高显得；杀神与世动克官则凶、虚费身心求不获

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:官鬼爻旺相、没有刑克，如果再遇扶持，就能得高位显达；杀神和世发动来克官，是凶，白费身心、求不到。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** With the Pressure line strong and not punished or controlled, if it also meets support, high and distinguished rank is gained; a baleful spirit and the Self moving to control Pressure is bad, and body and mind are spent in vain and it is not obtained.

**《断易天机·占仕宦第十一》原文** — 《断易天机》第 2720 行 · *Duanyi Tianji (Heavenly Secrets of Judging the Changes), "Chapter 11: Divining official careers", base text; Duanyi Tianji, line 2720*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 鬼爻旺相无刑克， | 官鬼爻旺相、没有刑克， | With the Pressure line strong and not punished or controlled, |
| 若遇扶持高显得， | 如果再遇扶持，就能得高位显达； | if it also meets support, high and distinguished rank is gained; |
| 杀神世动克官凶， | 杀神和世发动来克官，是凶； | a baleful spirit and the Self moving to control Pressure is bad; |
| 虚费身心求不获。 | 白费身心，求不到。 | body and mind are spent in vain and it is not obtained. |

相关:LY.CAREER.TXF.01

## LY.CAREER.DYTJ.30 火珠林·求官：要官鬼有气旺相，印绶（父母）不带空亡；忌子孙发动，并忌父母与官鬼落空

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:求官，官鬼要有气、旺相，印绶（父母爻）不带空亡；忌讳子孙发动，也忌父母和官鬼落空。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** In seeking office, Pressure must have qi and be strong and the seal and ribbon (the Resource line) must not carry Void; Output moving is to be avoided, and so are Resource and Pressure falling void.

**《断易天机·占仕宦第十一》原文** — 《断易天机》第 2731 行 · *Duanyi Tianji (Heavenly Secrets of Judging the Changes), "Chapter 11: Divining official careers", base text; Duanyi Tianji, line 2731*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 要官鬼有气旺相， | 官鬼要有气、旺相， | Pressure must have qi and be strong, |
| 并印绶不带空亡， | 并且印绶不带空亡； | and the seal and ribbon must not carry Void; |
| 印绶者父母爻也。 | 印绶，就是父母爻。 | the seal and ribbon are the Resource line. |
| 忌子孙发动， | 忌讳子孙发动， | Output moving is to be avoided, |
| 并父母与官鬼落空也。 | 以及父母和官鬼落空。 | as well as Resource and Pressure falling void. |

> **按**:「火珠林」是断易天机转引的书名，这一条在火珠林里的相同意思见 HZL.01、HZL.02。
>
> **Note:** 'Huozhulin' is the name of a book quoted by the Duanyi Tianji; the same sense in the Huozhulin itself is in HZL.01 and HZL.02.

相关:LY.CAREER.HZL.01、LY.CAREER.HZL.02、LY.CAREER.HJC.05

## LY.CAREER.DYTJ.48 杜氏占求事歌：求事先看内卦，内刚柔克外所求通；内爻无气哪能共、世应空亡身心不同；内外相生俱有气事皆成就荷天公

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:谋事，先看内卦：是阳卦还是阴卦；内卦克外卦，所求就通，外卦克内卦，难成；内卦的爻无气，即使外卦旺相，也不能共事、所求终不遂心；世、应空亡，身与心相违，与人谋事也难称意；内外卦相生、又都有气，事情都成就，像得到天公的庇佑。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** In planning a matter, first look at the inner trigram, whether it is a yang trigram or a yin one; if the inner controls the outer, what is sought goes through, and if the outer controls the inner it is hard to accomplish; if the lines of the inner trigram have no qi, then even if the outer is strong one cannot work together and what is sought is in the end not as one wishes; with the Self and the Other Void, body and heart are at odds, and in planning a matter with others it is hard to get what one wants; with inner and outer generating each other and both with qi, all matters are accomplished, as if blessed by Heaven.

**《断易天机·占求事第十三》原文** — 《断易天机》第 2329 行 · *Duanyi Tianji (Heavenly Secrets of Judging the Changes), "Chapter 13: Divining for an undertaking", base text; Duanyi Tianji, line 2329*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 求事先看内卦中（1）， | 求事先看内卦里的情形（1）； | In seeking a matter, first look into the inner trigram (1); |
| 刚柔克外所求通（2）， | 内卦的刚柔去克外卦，所求就通（2）； | if its hardness or softness controls the outer, what is sought goes through (2); |
| 内爻无气哪能共（3）， | 内卦的爻无气，哪里能共事（3）； | if the inner lines have no qi, how can one work together (3); |
| 空处身心亦不同（4）。 | 落空的地方，身与心也不一致（4）。 | in a void place, body and heart too are not as one (4). |
| 内外相生俱有气（5）， | 内外相生，又都有气（5）， | inner and outer generating each other and both with qi (5), |
| 事皆成就荷天公（6）。 | 事情都成就，承蒙天公庇佑（6）。 | all matters are accomplished, thanks to the blessing of Heaven (6). |

**《断易天机》注(注者不详)** — 《断易天机》第 2332 行 · *Duanyi Tianji commentary (commentator unknown); Duanyi Tianji, line 2332*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| （1）求事当先看内卦，是阳卦还是阴卦。。 | （1）求事应当先看内卦，是阳卦还是阴卦。 | (1) In seeking a matter one should first look at the inner trigram, whether it is a yang trigram or a yin one. |

**《断易天机》注(注者不详)** — 《断易天机》第 2333 行 · *Duanyi Tianji commentary (commentator unknown); Duanyi Tianji, line 2333*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| （2）若内卦克外卦，则所求可成。 | （2）如果内卦克外卦，所求就能成； | (2) If the inner trigram controls the outer, what is sought can be accomplished; |
| 若外卦克内卦，则主难成。 | 如果外卦克内卦，就主难成。 | if the outer trigram controls the inner, it means hard to accomplish. |

**《断易天机》注(注者不详)** — 《断易天机》第 2334 行 · *Duanyi Tianji commentary (commentator unknown); Duanyi Tianji, line 2334*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| （3）若内卦无气，即使外卦旺相，所求终不能遂心。 | （3）如果内卦无气，即使外卦旺相，所求终究不能遂心。 | (3) If the inner trigram has no qi, then even if the outer trigram is strong, what is sought cannot in the end be as one wishes. |

**《断易天机》注(注者不详)** — 《断易天机》第 2335 行 · *Duanyi Tianji commentary (commentator unknown); Duanyi Tianji, line 2335*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| （4）若世应空亡，主身与心违，与人谋事，也难如心称意。 | （4）如果世、应空亡，主身与心相违，与人谋事，也难如心称意。 | (4) If the Self and the Other are Void, body and heart are at odds, and in planning a matter with others it is hard to get what one wants. |

**《断易天机》注(注者不详)** — 《断易天机》第 2336 行 · *Duanyi Tianji commentary (commentator unknown); Duanyi Tianji, line 2336*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| （5）内外卦相生，有气则吉利。 | （5）内外卦相生，有气就吉利。 | (5) Inner and outer trigrams generating each other with qi is auspicious. |
| 如风水涣卦之类是也。 | 如风水涣卦这一类就是。 | The hexagram Huan (wind over water) and the like are of this kind. |

**《断易天机》注(注者不详)** — 《断易天机》第 2337 行 · *Duanyi Tianji commentary (commentator unknown); Duanyi Tianji, line 2337*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| （6）谋事成就，如得天公庇佑。 | （6）谋事成就，好像得到天公的庇佑。 | (6) A planned matter is accomplished as if blessed by Heaven. |

> **按**:「求事」指谋求办成一件事，不限于求官。「杜氏」是这首歌的作者，断易天机没有交代是谁。
>
> **Note:** 'Seeking a matter' means working to get a thing done and is not limited to seeking office. 'Master Du' is named as the author of the song, and the Duanyi Tianji does not say who he is.

相关:LY.CAREER.DYTJ.44

## LY.CAREER.DYTJ.49 经验云·出行谒贵及寻人：兄鬼交重不可亲，子旺财明须吉利，父兴既去又回程

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:出行、谒见贵人以及寻人：兄、鬼交重发动，不可亲近；子孙旺、财爻明，一定吉利；父母兴动，即使去了也会又回来。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** For travelling, calling on a noble, or looking for someone: with Peer and Pressure moving heavily one cannot get close; with Output strong and Wealth clear it is surely auspicious; with Resource rising, one who has gone will come back again.

**《断易天机·占求事第十三》原文** — 《断易天机》第 2344 行 · *Duanyi Tianji (Heavenly Secrets of Judging the Changes), "Chapter 13: Divining for an undertaking", base text; Duanyi Tianji, line 2344*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 出行谒贵及寻人， | 出行、谒见贵人以及寻人， | For travelling, calling on a noble, or looking for someone, |
| 兄鬼交重不可亲， | 兄、鬼交重发动，不可亲近； | with Peer and Pressure moving heavily, one cannot get close; |
| 子旺财明须吉利， | 子孙旺、财爻明，一定吉利； | with Output strong and Wealth clear, it is surely auspicious; |
| 父兴既去又回程。 | 父母兴动，即使去了也会又回来。 | with Resource rising, one who has gone will come back again. |

> **按**:「经验云」是断易天机引来的一条经验语，没有交代出处。
>
> **Note:** 'Jingyan yun' (experience says) is an empirical saying cited in the Duanyi Tianji without a source.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮大全》:同文 第 3884 行:出行谒贵及寻人;同文 第 3884 行:兄鬼交重不可亲;异文(相似 0.86) 第 3865 行:子旺财明须得利;同文 第 3884 行:父兴既去又回程
- 《火珠林》:同文 第 1305 行:出行谒贵及寻人;同文 第 1305 行:兄鬼交重不可亲;异文(相似 0.86) 第 1294 行:子旺财明须得利;同文 第 1305 行:父兴既去又回程

相关:LY.CAREER.HZL.03、LY.CAREER.HZL.04

## LY.CAREER.EL.83 官员荐奖：推举得官父旺动稳望高迁奖劳、若财鬼衰空虚图给赏；推荐者若得官父两全便为佳兆二中欠一未得稳成，奖励者虽用官爻又宜财旺、财鬼如空定无奖勤给赏之美

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:推举时，官爻、父爻旺相发动，稳稳地盼得高升、奖励功劳；如果财爻、官鬼衰弱落空，想谋求赏赐也是白费。具体说：推荐的，官爻、父爻两样齐全才是好兆头，两样缺一，就不能稳稳办成；论奖励的，虽然用官爻，还适宜财爻旺相；财爻、官鬼如果落空，就一定没有奖励勤劳、给予赏赐的美事。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** For a recommendation, if the star of office and Resource are thriving and moving, one can securely hope for high promotion and an award for service; if Wealth and Pressure are weak or Void, to plan for a reward is vain. In detail: for a recommendation, to have both the star of office and Resource is a good omen, and if one of the two is lacking it is not securely accomplished; for an award, though the Pressure line is the one used, Wealth should also be thriving, and if Wealth and Pressure are Void there will surely be no fine thing like an award for diligence and the granting of a reward.

**《易林补遗·官员荐奖章第三十三》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 818 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 33: Recommendation and commendation of officials", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 818*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 推举得官父旺动，稳望高迁奖劳。 | 推举时得官爻、父爻旺相发动，稳稳地盼得高升、奖励功劳； | For a recommendation, if the star of office and Resource are thriving and moving, one can securely hope for high promotion and an award for service; |
| 若财鬼衰空虚图给赏。 | 如果财爻、官鬼衰弱落空，想谋求赏赐也是白费。 | if Wealth and Pressure are weak or Void, to plan for a reward is vain. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 819–821 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, lines 819–821*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 推荐者若得官父两全，便为佳兆。 | 推荐的，如果官爻、父爻两样齐全，就是好兆头。 | For a recommendation, if both the star of office and Resource are present, it is a good omen. |
| 二中欠一，未得稳成。 | 两样当中缺一样，就不能稳稳办成。 | If one of the two is lacking, it is not securely accomplished. |
| 奖动者虽用官爻，又宜财旺。 | 论奖励的，虽然用官爻，还适宜财爻旺相。 | For an award, though the Pressure line is the one used, Wealth should also be thriving. |
| 财鬼如空，定无奖勤给赏之美也。 | 财爻、官鬼如果落空，一定没有奖励勤劳、给予赏赐的美事。 | If Wealth and Pressure are Void, there will surely be no fine thing like an award for diligence and the granting of a reward. |

> **按**:「奖动者」疑是「奖勤者」（后一句有「奖勤给赏」），译作「论奖励的」。「官父」是官爻和父母爻，「财鬼」是财爻和官鬼。「高迁」是高升。小标说推荐看官文、旌奖看财鬼，这一条把两样合起来说：推荐要官父齐，奖励要财官俱旺。
>
> **Note:** 'Jiang dong zhe' is presumably 'jiang qin zhe', for the next sentence has 'award for diligence and granting of reward', and it is translated 'for an award'. 'Guanfu' is the Pressure line and the Resource line, 'caigui' is Wealth and Pressure. 'Gaoqian' is high promotion. The subtitle said that recommendation looks at the star of office and the document and commendation at Wealth and Pressure, and this entry puts the two together: a recommendation needs both the star of office and Resource, and an award needs Wealth and the star of office both thriving.

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.82、LY.CAREER.EL.84、LY.CAREER.HJC.05、LY.CAREER.HJC.18

---

## LY.CAREER.EL.86 官员荐奖：欲求坊扁方光辉必藉官爻之旺相，世空绝望、子动难谋、财动还须嘱托、鬼空不必祈求；凡求牌坊扁额光辉门闾先看官爻次推世象二者若有一空始终绝望，子孙动亦不能求，财动生官谋为遂意

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:想求得牌坊、匾额来光耀门庭，必须凭官爻旺相。世爻落空就绝望，子孙发动就难谋求，财爻发动还要托人嘱托，官鬼落空就不必祈求了。凡是求牌坊、匾额来光耀门庭的，先看官爻，再推世爻；这两个只要有一个落空，始终没有指望；子孙发动，也求不到；财爻发动去生官爻，谋求就遂心如意。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** To seek an honorific archway or plaque that will bring glory to one's door, one must rely on the Pressure line being thriving. If the Self is Void there is no hope, if Output moves it is hard to arrange, if Wealth moves one must still entrust the matter to someone, and if Pressure is Void there is no need to pray for it. In every seeking of an archway or plaque to bring glory to one's gate, look first at the Pressure line and then reckon the Self image; if either of the two is Void, there is no hope to the end; if Output moves, it cannot be obtained either; if Wealth moves to generate the star of office, the scheming turns out as one wishes.

**《易林补遗·官员荐奖章第三十三》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 826 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 33: Recommendation and commendation of officials", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 826*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 欲求坊扁方光辉，必藉官爻之旺相。 | 想求得牌坊、匾额才光耀门庭，必须凭官爻旺相。 | To seek an archway or plaque that will bring glory to one's door, one must rely on the Pressure line being thriving. |
| 世空绝望。 | 世爻落空，就绝望了。 | If the Self is Void there is no hope. |
| 子动难谋， | 子孙发动，就难以谋求； | If Output moves it is hard to arrange; |
| 财动还须嘱托， | 财爻发动，还要托人嘱托； | if Wealth moves one must still entrust the matter to someone; |
| 鬼空不必祈求， | 官鬼落空，就不必祈求了。 | if Pressure is Void there is no need to pray for it. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 827 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 827*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 凡求牌坊扁额光辉门闾，先看官爻，次推世象，二者若有一空，始终绝望。 | 凡是求牌坊、匾额来光耀门庭的，先看官爻，再推世爻；这两个如果有一个落空，始终没有指望。 | In every seeking of an archway or plaque to bring glory to one's gate, look first at the Pressure line and then reckon the Self image; if either of the two is Void, there is no hope to the end. |
| 子孙动，亦不能求。 | 子孙发动，也求不到。 | If Output moves, it cannot be obtained either. |
| 财动生官，谋为遂意。 | 财爻发动去生官爻，谋求就遂心如意。 | If Wealth moves to generate the star of office, the scheming turns out as one wishes. |

> **按**:「坊扁」是牌坊和匾额，「光辉门闾」是光耀门庭；官府给牌坊匾额是一种表彰，所以看官爻。「嘱托」是托人关照。诀里「财动还须嘱托」和解里「财动生官，谋为遂意」并不冲突：财动生官是成事的路子，但事要有人去办。
>
> **Note:** 'Fangbian' are the honorific archway and the plaque, and 'guanghui menlü' is bringing glory to one's gate; an archway or plaque given by the authorities is a kind of commendation, so the Pressure line is looked at. 'Zhutuo' is entrusting a matter to someone. The verse's 'if Wealth moves one must still entrust the matter to someone' and the explanation's 'if Wealth moves to generate the star of office, the scheming turns out as one wishes' do not clash: Wealth moving to generate the star of office is the road by which the matter is done, but someone must be sent to see to it.

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.85、LY.CAREER.EL.87、LY.CAREER.HJC.02

---

## LY.CAREER.EL.87 官员荐奖：六合则心怀喜悦、六冲则面染忧愁；如求扎帖又看文书反嫌财动，父鬼财空允能给发，不拘扁贴皆忌六冲

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:六合的卦，心里喜悦；六冲的卦，脸上带着忧愁。如果求扎帖（官府的帖文），又要看文书爻，反而嫌财爻发动；父爻、官鬼在而财爻落空，就准许发给；不论匾额还是帖文，都忌讳六冲。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** With a six-combine the heart is glad; with a six-clash the face is clouded with sorrow. If one seeks an official warrant, look also at the document line, and on the contrary dislike Wealth moving; with Resource and Pressure present and Wealth Void, it is allowed and can be issued; whether plaque or warrant, a six-clash is feared in all.

**《易林补遗·官员荐奖章第三十三》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 826 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 33: Recommendation and commendation of officials", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 826*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 六合则心怀喜悦， | 卦是六合，心里喜悦； | With a six-combine the heart is glad; |
| 六冲则面染忧愁。 | 卦是六冲，脸上带着忧愁。 | with a six-clash the face is clouded with sorrow. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 827 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 827*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 如求扎帖，又看文书，反嫌财动。 | 如果求扎帖（官府的帖文），又要看文书爻，反而嫌财爻发动。 | If one seeks an official warrant, look also at the document line, and on the contrary dislike Wealth moving. |
| 父鬼财空，允能给发。 | 父爻、官鬼在而财爻落空，就准许发给。 | With Resource and Pressure present and Wealth Void, it is allowed and can be issued. |
| 不拘扁贴皆忌六冲。 | 不论匾额还是帖文，都忌讳六冲。 | Whether plaque or warrant, a six-clash is feared in all. |

> **按**:「扎帖」是官府发给的帖文（札帖），求的是一张批准的文书，所以看文书爻；文书爻怕财克，所以反嫌财动。「父鬼财空」书里连写，按上一句「反嫌财动」读作父、鬼在而财空；若读成三样都空，就与「允能给发」不合。「扁贴」是匾额和帖文。
>
> **Note:** 'Zhatie' is a written warrant issued by the authorities (a 'zhatie' of the yamen); what is sought is an approving document, so the document line is looked at, and the document line fears being controlled by Wealth, which is why Wealth moving is disliked. 'Fu gui cai kong' is written run together in the book and is read, after the previous sentence's 'on the contrary dislike Wealth moving', as Resource and Pressure present and Wealth Void; if it were read as all three Void it would not agree with 'it is allowed and can be issued'. 'Bian tie' is the plaque and the warrant.

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.86、LY.CAREER.EL.88、LY.CAREER.HJC.40

## LY.CAREER.EL.90 上书奏对：进拜表章兼上本，父旺官兴御笔标，亦忌六冲财子动，次愁印绶化财爻，更嫌鬼象之为福，父绝官空莫进朝；拜表上本启奏皆同，若得父旺官兴定蒙准奏，若犯六冲、财摇子动、父变妻财、官之福、父绝官空一节者莫奏朝廷，纵奏徒费心机难迎圣意

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:递表章、上奏本，父爻旺相、官爻兴旺，就会得到御笔批准。也忌讳六冲、财爻和子孙发动；其次怕印绶（父母）变成财爻，更嫌官鬼变成福神（子孙）；父爻绝、官爻空，就不要进朝。拜表、上本、启奏都是同一个取法：父旺官兴，一定蒙准奏；如果遇到六冲，或者财摇子动、或者父变妻财、或者官变福神、或者父绝官空，这几条里只要犯了一条，就不要奏朝廷；即使奏了，也白费心机，难以合君王的心意。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** In presenting a memorial and submitting a petition, if Resource is thriving and the star of office flourishes, the imperial brush will mark it for approval. Also fear a six-clash and Wealth and Output moving; next worry the seal line changing into the Wealth line; still more dislike the Pressure image changing into Output; with Resource extinct and the star of office Void, do not go to court. Presenting a memorial, submitting a petition and memorialising all take the same method: if Resource is thriving and the star of office flourishes, the memorial will surely be approved; if it meets a six-clash, or Wealth sways and Output moves, or Resource changes into Wealth, or the star of office changes into Output, or Resource is extinct and the star of office Void, then if even one of these points is violated, do not memorialise the court; even if one does, it is a waste of effort and hardly meets the sovereign's wish.

**《易林补遗·上书咎奏章第三十四》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 835 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 34: Submitting letters and memorials", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 835*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 进拜表章兼上本，父旺官兴御笔标， | 递送表章、上呈奏本，父爻旺相、官爻兴旺，就会得到御笔批准； | In presenting a memorial and submitting a petition, if Resource is thriving and the star of office flourishes, the imperial brush will mark it for approval; |
| 亦忌六冲财子动，次愁印绶化财爻， | 也忌讳六冲、财爻和子孙发动，其次怕印绶（父母）变成财爻； | also fear a six-clash and Wealth and Output moving, and next worry the seal line changing into the Wealth line; |
| 更嫌鬼象之为福，父绝官空莫进朝。 | 更嫌官鬼变成福神（子孙）；父爻绝、官爻空，就不要进朝。 | still more dislike the Pressure image changing into Output; with Resource extinct and the star of office Void, do not go to court. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 836 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 836*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 拜表上本启奏皆同，若得父旺官兴，定蒙准奏。 | 拜表、上本、启奏都是一样的取法；如果父爻旺相、官爻兴旺，一定蒙准奏。 | Presenting a memorial, submitting a petition and memorialising are all the same; if Resource is thriving and the star of office flourishes, the memorial will surely be approved. |
| 若值六冲，或财摇子动、或父变妻财、或官之福、或父绝官空，以上数端，如犯一节者，莫奏朝廷，纵奏，徒费心机，难迎圣意。 | 如果遇到六冲，或者财摇子动、或者父变妻财、或者官变福神、或者父绝官空，这几条里如果犯了一条，就不要奏朝廷；即使奏了，也白费心机，难以合君王的心意。 | If it meets a six-clash, or Wealth sways and Output moves, or Resource changes into Wealth, or the star of office changes into Output, or Resource is extinct and the star of office Void, then if even one of these points is violated, do not memorialise the court; even if one does, it is a waste of effort and hardly meets the sovereign's wish. |

> **按**:「表章」「奏本」都是呈给君王的文书；「御笔标」是皇帝用笔批示。「印绶」是父母爻（文书、印）。「官之福」是官变成福神（子孙）。这一条与增删卜易上书（ZS.28 起）取同一类象：父母（文书）旺、官旺为吉；财动克文书、子动伤官为凶。
>
> **Note:** 'Biaozhang' and 'zouben' are both documents presented to the sovereign; 'yubi biao' is the emperor marking approval with his brush. 'Yinshou', the seal line, is the Resource line (the document and the seal). 'Guan zhi fu' is the star of office changing into the fortune spirit (Output). This entry takes the same kind of images as the Zengshan Buyi on submitting letters (from ZS.28): Resource (the document) thriving and the star of office thriving are auspicious; Wealth moving to control the document and Output moving to hurt the star of office are ominous.

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.89、LY.CAREER.HJC.26、LY.CAREER.ZS.28、LY.CAREER.ZS.29、LY.CAREER.ZS.30、LY.CAREER.ZS.31

## LY.CAREER.EL.93 朝天面旨：逢冲莫去朝天子、遇合应来奏帝皇，无鬼不能沾雨露、世空岂得受恩光；六冲之象切莫朝天，六合之爻理宜面圣，爻虽攒合卦中无鬼或值空乡渥恩莫望，卦纵有官世居陷地还嗟运蹇岂沐天恩

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:逢到冲，不要去朝见天子；遇到合，就该来奏对皇帝；没有官鬼，就沾不到雨露（恩泽）；世爻落空，怎能蒙受恩光？具体说：六冲的卦象，千万不要去朝见天子；六合的爻象，按理适宜面见圣上；爻象虽然聚合，但卦中没有官鬼，或者落在空乡，就不要指望丰厚的恩典；卦里即使有官爻，世爻落在陷地，也只能叹运气艰难，怎么谈得上沐浴天恩？

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Meeting a clash, do not go to court the Son of Heaven; meeting a combination, it is fitting to come and memorialise the emperor; with no Pressure one cannot share in the rain and dew of favour; with the Self Void how can one receive the glow of grace? In detail: with a six-clash image, on no account go to court; with a six-combine line, it is proper to meet the sage sovereign; even if the lines gather in combination, if the hexagram has no Pressure or it falls on empty ground, do not hope for rich favour; even if the hexagram has a star of office, if the Self sits in a pit one can still only lament one's hard fortune, and how could one bathe in heaven's favour?

**《易林补遗·朝天面旨章第三十五》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 848 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 35: An audience with the sovereign", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 848*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 逢冲莫去朝天子，遇合应来奏帝皇， | 逢到冲，不要去朝见天子；遇到合，就该来奏对皇帝； | Meeting a clash, do not go to court the Son of Heaven; meeting a combination, it is fitting to come and memorialise the emperor; |
| 无鬼不能沾雨露，世空岂得受恩光。 | 没有官鬼，就沾不到雨露（恩泽）；世爻落空，怎能蒙受恩光？ | with no Pressure one cannot share in the rain and dew of favour; with the Self Void how can one receive the glow of grace? |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 849 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 849*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 六冲之象，切莫朝天。 | 六冲的卦象，千万不要去朝见天子。 | With a six-clash image, on no account go to court. |
| 六合之爻，理宜面圣。 | 六合的爻象，按理适宜面见圣上。 | With a six-combine line, it is proper to meet the sage sovereign. |
| 爻虽攒合，卦中无鬼或值空乡，渥恩莫望。 | 爻象虽然聚合，但卦中没有官鬼，或者落在空乡，就不要指望丰厚的恩典。 | Even if the lines gather in combination, if the hexagram has no Pressure or it falls on empty ground, do not hope for rich favour. |
| 卦纵有官，世居陷地，还嗟运蹇，岂沐天恩。 | 卦里即使有官爻，世爻落在陷地，也只能叹运气艰难，怎么谈得上沐浴天恩？ | Even if the hexagram has a star of office, if the Self sits in a pit one can still only lament one's hard fortune, and how could one bathe in heaven's favour? |

> **按**:「六冲」「六合」指卦的六冲、六合（见术语表）。「攒合」是聚合，「空乡」是空亡之地，「渥恩」是优厚的恩典。「世居陷地」的「陷」与前面仕宦升迁「临陷临空」同，是落入空、墓、绝一类凶地，书里没有细说。这一条与黄金策仕宦「奏陈谏诤，那堪太岁刑冲」（HJC.26）取同一类忌：冲克是朝见君王的大忌。
>
> **Note:** 'Six-clash' and 'six-combine' are the hexagram patterns (see the glossary). 'Cuanhe' is gathering in combination, 'kongxiang' is the ground of Void, and 'wo'en' is rich favour. The 'xian' of 'shi ju xian di' is the same as in 'lin xian lin kong' in the earlier chapter on official promotion, a fall into a bad place such as Void, tomb or extinction, and the book does not go into it. This entry takes the same taboo as 'for memorials and remonstrance, how can one bear Tai Sui's punishment and clash' in the Huangjin Ce on official careers (HJC.26): clash and control are the great taboo for seeing the sovereign.

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.92、LY.CAREER.EL.94、LY.CAREER.HJC.26、LY.CAREER.EL.80

## LY.CAREER.EL.97 文书消息：以文书为主、官鬼为凭；官印文书宜父动，民修票约忌财兴，财临身世徒书契，用不空亡准此情；凡论文书票约取父母为用爻，旺则有成空则无用，财爻为动或值世身空劳纸笔枉费神思

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:论文书、消息，以文书爻为主，以官鬼为凭据。官府的印信文书，适宜父爻发动；民间立的票据契约，忌讳财爻兴旺；财爻临在身爻、世爻上，书契就白写；用爻不落空亡，就按这个情形断。凡是论文书、票据、契约，取父母爻为用爻：旺相就有成，落空就无用；财爻发动，或者世爻、身爻落空，就白费纸笔、枉费心思。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** In judging documents and tidings, take the document as the chief and Pressure as the ground. For official seals and documents it is fitting that Resource move; for bills and contracts drawn up by the people, avoid Wealth flourishing; if Wealth sits on the Body or the Self, the writing of the deed is in vain; if the Subject is not Void, hold to this situation. In judging documents, bills and contracts, take Resource as the Subject line: if thriving it succeeds, if Void it is useless; if the Wealth line moves, or the Self or Body is Void, the paper and brush are toiled for in vain and thought is wasted.

**《易林补遗·文书消息章第三十七》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 863 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 37: Documents and tidings", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 863*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 以文书为主，官鬼为凭。 | 以文书为主要看的，以官鬼为凭据。 | Take the document as the chief to be looked at, and Pressure as the ground. |

**《易林补遗·文书消息章第三十七》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 864 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 37: Documents and tidings", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 864*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 官印文书宜父动，民修票约忌财兴， | 官府的印信文书，适宜父爻发动；民间立的票据契约，忌讳财爻兴旺； | For official seals and documents it is fitting that Resource move; for bills and contracts drawn up by the people, avoid Wealth flourishing; |
| 财临身世徒书契。 | 财爻临在身爻、世爻上，书契就白写了。 | if Wealth sits on the Body or the Self, the writing of the deed is in vain. |
| 周不空亡准此情。 | 用爻不落空亡，就按这个情形断。 | If the Subject is not Void, hold to this situation. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 865 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 865*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 凡论文书票约，取父母为用爻，旺则有成，空则无用，财爻为动或值世身空，劳纸笔枉费神思。 | 凡是论文书、票据、契约，取父母爻为用爻：旺相就有成，落空就无用；财爻发动，或者世爻、身爻落空，就白费纸笔、枉费心思。 | In judging documents, bills and contracts, take Resource as the Subject line: if thriving it succeeds, if Void it is useless; if the Wealth line moves, or the Self or Body is Void, the paper and brush are toiled for in vain and thought is wasted. |

> **按**:「票约」是票据和契约，「书契」是写下来的契约文书，「官印文书」是官府盖印的文书。「周不空亡准此情」的「周」疑是「用」（用神，这里是父母爻），译作「用爻不落空亡」。这一条与易隐文书占「文书占·公私之辨与成否」（YY.71）取同一个关系：都以父母为用，公家的文书看官旺，私下的契约看财怕克父。
>
> **Note:** 'Piaoyue' are bills and contracts, 'shuqi' are the written contract documents, and 'guanyin wenshu' are documents sealed by the authorities. The 'zhou' of 'zhou bu kongwang zhun ci qing' is presumably 'yong', the Subject (here the Resource line), and it is translated 'if the Subject is not Void'. This entry takes the same relation as 'public and private divinations and success' in the Yi Yin divination on documents (YY.71): both take Resource as the Subject, and for the document of the state one looks at the star of office thriving, for a private contract one fears Wealth controlling Resource.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《易隐》:异文(相似 0.89) 第 2563 行:以文书为主，官鬼为

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.98、LY.CAREER.YY.71、LY.CAREER.YY.72、LY.CAREER.HJC.05、LY.CAREER.ZS.28、LY.CAREER.ZS.30

## LY.CAREER.EL.98 文书消息：鬼是元神空不就、财为忌客动难成，父之冲克真为假、母变生扶却信凭；卦无官鬼或落空亡皆主不就，妻爻发动契必难成，财动官亦动反能成契，父母若被日月冲破或化绝化空此契不能见信于人，父母若遇生扶或变为有气此书纵假而可为真

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:官鬼是元神，落空就办不成；财是忌神，发动就难成；父爻被冲克，真的也成了假的；父母爻变出生扶它的爻，就有信凭。具体说：卦里没有官鬼，或者官鬼落空亡，都主办不成；妻爻发动，契约必定难成；财动官也动，反而能成契约；父母爻如果被日月冲破，或者化绝、化空，这份契约不能取信于人；父母爻如果遇到生扶，或者变成有气，这份文书即使是假的也可以当真。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Pressure is the Support, and if Void it does not succeed; Wealth is the Adversary, and if it moves it is hard to accomplish; if Resource is clashed or controlled, the genuine turns false; if Resource changes and is generated and supported, it gains credence. In detail: if the hexagram has no Pressure or it falls on Void, all mean it will not succeed; if the wife line, Wealth, moves, the contract will surely be hard to complete; if Wealth moves and the star of office also moves, on the contrary the contract can be completed; if Resource is clashed or broken by the Day or Month, or changes into extinction or Void, this contract cannot be believed by others; if Resource meets generation and support, or changes into something with qi, then even if this document is false it can be taken as true.

**《易林补遗·文书消息章第三十七》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 866 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 37: Documents and tidings", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 866*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 鬼是元神空不就，财为忌客动难成， | 官鬼是元神，落空就办不成；财是忌神，发动就难成； | Pressure is the Support, and if Void it does not succeed; Wealth is the Adversary, and if it moves it is hard to accomplish; |
| 父之冲克真为假，母变生扶却信凭。 | 父爻被冲克，真的也成了假的；父母爻变出生扶它的爻，反而有信凭。 | if Resource is clashed or controlled, the genuine turns false; if Resource changes and is generated and supported, it gains credence. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 867–869 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, lines 867–869*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 卦无官鬼或落空亡，皆主不就。 | 卦里没有官鬼，或者官鬼落空亡，都主办不成。 | If the hexagram has no Pressure or it falls on Void, all mean it will not succeed. |
| 妻爻发动，契必难成。 | 妻爻（财爻）发动，契约必定难成。 | If the wife line, Wealth, moves, the contract will surely be hard to complete. |
| 财动官亦动，反能成契。 | 财爻发动、官爻也发动，反而能成契约。 | If Wealth moves and the star of office also moves, on the contrary the contract can be completed. |
| 父母若被日月冲破、或化绝化空，此契不能见信于人。 | 父母爻如果被日月冲破，或者化绝、化空，这份契约不能取信于人。 | If Resource is clashed or broken by the Day or Month, or changes into extinction or Void, this contract cannot be believed by others. |
| 父母若遇生扶、或变为有气，此书纵假而可为真也。 | 父母爻如果遇到生扶，或者变成有气，这份文书即使是假的也可以当作真的。 | If Resource meets generation and support, or changes into something with qi, then even if this document is false it can be taken as true. |

> **按**:「元神」是生用神的爻，「忌客」按文意是忌神（见术语表）：这里用神是父母（文书），官鬼生父母所以是元神，财克父母所以是忌神。「母」是父母爻。末两句「父之冲克真为假，母变生扶却信凭」「此书纵假而可为真」书里就是这样说的：文书取不取信于人，看父母爻的旺衰生克；这里照字面译，不替它加解释。
>
> **Note:** 'Yuanshen', the Support, is the line that generates the Subject, and 'jike', the Adversary guest, is by the sense the Adversary (see the glossary): here the Subject is Resource (the document), so Pressure, which generates Resource, is the Support, and Wealth, which controls Resource, is the Adversary. 'Mu', mother, is the Resource line. The last two statements, 'if Resource is clashed or controlled, the genuine turns false; if Resource changes and is generated and supported, it gains credence' and 'even if this document is false it can be taken as true', are exactly what the book says: whether a document is believed depends on the strength, generation and control of the Resource line; it is translated literally here, with no explanation added.

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.97、LY.CAREER.YY.71、LY.CAREER.YY.72、LY.CAREER.ZS.28、LY.CAREER.ZS.30

## LY.CAREER.EL.104 谋望成事：六冲爻象难谐就、卦内无官事必差、世应若逢日辰合任君不愿也堪夸；六冲之卦事决不成，合处逢冲成后复退，鬼若空亡及不上卦皆不就，世应二爻自相会合或得日辰合世合应纵若心中不欲也得允成

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:六冲的爻象难以谐成，卦里没有官爻，事一定出差错；世应如果逢日辰来合，任凭你不愿意也值得夸赞。六冲的卦，事一定不成；合的地方遇到冲，成了之后还会退回；官鬼如果空亡或者不上卦，都办不成；世、应二爻自相会合，或者得到日辰来合世、合应，即使心里不想，也能得到允准办成。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** A six-clash image is hard to bring to harmony, and with no star of office in the hexagram the affair is sure to go wrong; if the Self and the Other meet a combination from the Day, even if you do not wish it, it is worth praising. With a six-clash hexagram the affair will surely not succeed; if where there is a combination it meets a clash, after it succeeds it falls back again; if Pressure is Void or not in the hexagram, in neither case does it succeed; if the Self and the Other lines combine with each other, or the Day combines with the Self or the Other, even if one does not wish it in one's heart, it will be granted and accomplished.

**《易林补遗·谋望成事章第三十九》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 886 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 39: Scheming and accomplishing affairs", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 886*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 六冲爻象难谐就，卦内无官事必差， | 六冲的爻象难以谐成，卦内没有官爻，事一定出差错； | A six-clash image is hard to bring to harmony, and with no star of office in the hexagram the affair is sure to go wrong; |
| 世应若逢日辰合，任君不愿也堪夸。 | 世应如果逢上日辰来合，任凭你不愿意也值得夸赞。 | if the Self and the Other meet a combination from the Day, even if you do not wish it, it is worth praising. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 887 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 887*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 六冲之卦，事决不成。 | 六冲的卦，事一定不成。 | With a six-clash hexagram the affair will surely not succeed. |
| 合处逢冲，成后复退。 | 合的地方遇到冲，成了之后还会退回。 | If where there is a combination it meets a clash, after it succeeds it falls back again. |
| 鬼若空亡及不上卦，皆不就也。 | 官鬼如果空亡或者不上卦，都办不成。 | If Pressure is Void or not in the hexagram, in neither case does it succeed. |
| 世应二爻自相会合，或得日辰合世合应，纵若心中不欲，也得允成。 | 世、应二爻自相会合，或者得到日辰来合世、合应，即使心里不想，也能得到允准办成。 | If the Self and the Other lines combine with each other, or the Day combines with the Self or the Other, even if one does not wish it in one's heart, it will be granted and accomplished. |

> **按**:「合处逢冲，成后复退」与求馆章「如合处逢冲或变出六冲，虽见成就，其年定主不终」（EL.53）取同一个关系。「任君不愿」「心中不欲」书里没有说是谁心里不愿，这里按字面译。
>
> **Note:** 'Where there is a combination it meets a clash, after it succeeds it falls back' takes the same relation as 'if where there is a combination it meets a clash, or it changes out a six-clash, then even if it seems settled that year it will not last' in the chapter on seeking a school post (EL.53). The book does not say whose heart it is that is unwilling in 'even if you do not wish it' and 'even if one does not wish it in one's heart', and it is translated literally.

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.103、LY.CAREER.EL.105、LY.CAREER.EL.53、LY.CAREER.YY.84

## LY.CAREER.EL.105 谋望成事：陛官迁职兼兴讼官鬼交重名倍加、觅利还须财旺相、求书必得父光华，兄爻独发般般忌、子象兴隆事事佳；官中谋望所断如前复喜官爻旺相独忌子摇，谋利息财忌空亡、斡文书不宜财动卦如无父终不能成，诸般谋事各嫌兄弟交重惟子动无不为佳功名独忌

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:升官迁职、兴讼，官鬼发动，名声更添；求利，还要财爻旺相；求书信，必须父爻有光彩；兄爻独自发动，样样都忌；子孙兴旺，事事都好。在官府里谋求的，断法同前，又喜官爻旺相，唯独忌讳子孙发动。谋求利息钱财，忌讳财空亡；如果是办理文书，不适宜财爻发动；卦里如果没有父爻，终究不能成。各种谋事，都嫌兄弟发动；只有子孙发动没有不好的，唯独功名一项忌讳。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** For promotion and transfer in office, and for starting a lawsuit, if Pressure moves one's name is doubled; for seeking profit, Wealth must also be thriving; for seeking a letter, Resource must be bright; if the Peer line moves alone, everything is taboo; if Output flourishes, everything is good. For scheming in official business the judgment is as before, and one is again glad for the Pressure line to be thriving and alone fears Output moving. In scheming for interest and wealth, Void is to be avoided for Wealth; if one is managing a document, Wealth moving is not fitting; and if the hexagram has no Resource it cannot in the end succeed. In every kind of scheming, Peer moving is disliked; only Output moving is never anything but good, and for fame and office alone is it a taboo.

**《易林补遗·谋望成事章第三十九》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 888 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 39: Scheming and accomplishing affairs", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 888*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 陛官迁职兼兴讼，官鬼交重名倍加， | 升官迁职、兴讼，官鬼发动，名声更添； | For promotion and transfer in office, and for starting a lawsuit, if Pressure moves one's name is doubled; |
| 觅利还须财旺相，求书必得父光华。 | 求利，还要财爻旺相；求书信，必须父爻有光彩。 | for seeking profit, Wealth must also be thriving; for seeking a letter, Resource must be bright. |
| 兄爻独发般般忌，子象兴隆事事佳。 | 兄爻独自发动，样样都忌；子孙兴旺，事事都好。 | if the Peer line moves alone, everything is taboo; if Output flourishes, everything is good. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 890 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 890*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 官中谋望所断如前，复喜官爻旺相，独忌子摇。 | 在官府里谋求的，断法同前，又喜官爻旺相，唯独忌讳子孙发动。 | For scheming in official business the judgment is as before, and one is again glad for the Pressure line to be thriving and alone fears Output moving. |
| 如谋利息财忌空亡，若斡文书，不宜财动。 | 如果是谋求利息钱财，财爻忌空亡；如果是办理文书，不适宜财爻发动。 | In scheming for interest and wealth, Void is to be avoided for Wealth; if one is managing a document, Wealth moving is not fitting. |
| 卦如无父，终不能成。 | 卦里如果没有父爻，终究不能成。 | If the hexagram has no Resource, it cannot in the end succeed. |
| 诸般谋事，各嫌兄弟交重，惟子动无不为佳，功名独忌。 | 各种谋事，都嫌兄弟发动；只有子孙发动没有不好的，唯独功名一项忌讳。 | In every kind of scheming, Peer moving is disliked; only Output moving is never anything but good, and for fame and office alone is it a taboo. |

> **按**:「陛官迁职」的「陛」是「升」（见仕宦升迁章）。「斡文书」的「斡」是转、办理。「父光华」是父爻有光彩，即旺相不受伤。子孙发动是各种谋事的吉兆，唯独功名、官职一项忌讳——这与前面仕宦升迁章「官职升迁子莫刚」（EL.55）是一回事：官职功名最忌子孙。
>
> **Note:** The 'bi' of 'bi guan qian zhi' is 'sheng', to rise (see the chapter on official promotion). The 'wo' of 'wo wenshu' is to turn and to manage. 'Fu guanghua' is Resource having lustre, that is thriving and unhurt. Output moving is an auspicious sign for every kind of scheming, and only for fame and office is it a taboo, which is the same thing as 'for promotion in office Output must not be strong' in the earlier chapter on official promotion (EL.55): office and fame most fear Output.

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.104、LY.CAREER.EL.55、LY.CAREER.YY.77、LY.CAREER.YY.85

## LY.CAREER.EL.131 用人藏否：以用爻为主、应象为凭；凡用他人应上寻、家亲各有用爻分，日冲月破空亡者临在斯爻永不亨；用人行事、请人代劳、招人作生涯、托付图利，用外人以应爻为主，占亲戚以用象为凭；用爻遇日辰冲月建破或落旬空皆主不成纵成无力又不久远切莫用之

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:用人，以用爻为主，以应象为凭据。凡是用别人，就在应爻上找；亲属各有各的用爻划分；日冲、月破、空亡落在这个爻上，就永远不亨通。凡是用人办事，或者请人代自己劳作，或者招人做生意，或者托付别人图利益，如果用的是外人，以应爻为主；如果是亲戚，以用象为凭据。用爻如果遇到日辰冲、月建破，或者落旬空，都主不成；即使成了，也没有力量，又不长久，千万不要用这个人。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** In employing a man, take the Subject line as the chief and the Other image as the ground. In employing an outsider, look to the Other; for one's own kin, each has a Subject line to itself; if the Day's clash, Month-break or Void sits on this line, it never prospers. Whenever one employs a man to act, or asks a man to labour in one's place, or takes on a man for a business, or entrusts something to another for gain, if one employs an outsider the Other line is the chief; if it is a relative, the Subject image is the ground. If the Subject line meets the Day's clash, the Month's break, or falls on the ten-day Void, in every case it does not succeed, and even if it succeeds it has no strength and does not last, so by no means employ him.

**《易林补遗·用人藏否章第八十三》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 1627 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 83: Whether the man one employs is good or bad", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 1627*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 以用爻为主，应象为凭。 | 以用爻为主要看的，以应象为凭据。 | Take the Subject line as the chief to be looked at, and the Other image as the ground. |

**《易林补遗·用人藏否章第八十三》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 1628 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 83: Whether the man one employs is good or bad", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 1628*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 凡用他人应上寻，家亲各有用爻分， | 凡是用别人，就在应爻上找；亲属各有各的用爻划分； | In employing an outsider, look to the Other; for one's own kin, each has a Subject line to itself; |
| 日冲月破空亡者，临在斯爻永不亨。 | 日冲、月破、空亡落在这个爻上，就永远不亨通。 | if the Day's clash, Month-break or Void sits on this line, it never prospers. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 1629 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 1629*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 凡若用人行事、或请人代己之劳、或招人而作生涯、或托付而图利益，如用他人，以应爻为主。 | 凡是用人办事，或者请人代自己劳作，或者招人做生意，或者托付别人图利益，如果用的是外人，以应爻为主。 | Whenever one employs a man to act, or asks a man to labour in one's place, or takes on a man for a business, or entrusts something to another for gain, if one employs an outsider the Other line is the chief. |
| 若占亲戚，以用象为凭。 | 如果是占亲戚，就以用象为凭据。 | If it is about a relative, the Subject image is the ground. |
| 用爻若遇日辰冲、月建破、或落旬空者，皆主不成，纵成无力又不久远，切莫用之。 | 用爻如果遇到日辰冲、月建破，或者落旬空，都主不成；即使成了，也没有力量，又不长久，千万不要用这个人。 | If the Subject line meets the Day's clash, the Month's break, or falls on the ten-day Void, in every case it does not succeed, and even if it succeeds it has no strength and does not last, so by no means employ him. |

> **按**:「用人」是雇用、托付别人办事。「藏否」就是「臧否」，好坏。「家亲各有用爻分」是用亲属时按六亲各取那一爻。「斯爻」是这一爻。这一章看的是被用的这个人：外人取应爻，亲属取他那个六亲的爻。
>
> **Note:** 'Yong ren' is employing and entrusting a man with an affair. 'Zangpi' is written 'cang fou' and means good and bad. 'Jiaqin ge you yongyao fen' is that in employing a relative, each of the six kin takes its own line. 'Siyao' is this line. This chapter looks at the man who is employed: for an outsider, the Other line; for a relative, the line of his own kin.

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.132、LY.CAREER.EL.134

---

## LY.CAREER.EL.132 用人藏否：用遇绝乡终不美、世遭用克却无情、六冲反目非宾主、用变刑伤力必轻；用爻临绝又无生助者此人定主无能，用来克世者实怀欺主之心彼非仗义之人，卜六冲之卦终成反目，用爻虽有气而变克制之乡或墓绝人必先勤后惰事当有始无终

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:用爻遇到绝乡，终究不好；世爻被用爻所克，却是无情；六冲就反目、不成宾主；用爻变出刑伤，力量一定轻。用爻临绝又没有生扶，这个人一定没有能耐；用爻来克世，实在怀着欺骗主人的心，他不是仗义的人；卜得六冲的卦，终究反目，怎么能和谐？卦上用爻虽然有气，但变成克制它的地方、或者变入墓、绝，这个人必定先勤后惰，事情当是有始无终。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If the Subject meets a place of extinction it is in the end not good; if the Self is controlled by the Subject, it is without feeling; with a six-clash they fall out and are not host and guest; if the Subject changes into punishment and injury, its strength is surely slight. If the Subject line is in extinction and has no generation or support, the man is surely incapable; if the Subject comes to control the Self, he truly harbours a mind to cheat his master and is not a man of loyalty and justice; if a six-clash hexagram is cast, they end by falling out, and how can there be harmony? Even if the Subject line in the hexagram has qi, if it changes into a place that controls it or into tomb or extinction, the man will surely be diligent at first and idle later, and the affair will have a beginning but no end.

**《易林补遗·用人藏否章第八十三》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 1630 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 83: Whether the man one employs is good or bad", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 1630*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 用遇绝乡终不美，世遭用克却无情， | 用爻遇到绝乡，终究不好；世爻被用爻所克，却是无情； | If the Subject meets a place of extinction it is in the end not good; if the Self is controlled by the Subject, it is without feeling; |
| 六冲反目非宾主，用变刑伤力必轻。 | 六冲就反目、不成宾主；用爻变出刑伤，力量一定轻。 | with a six-clash they fall out and are not host and guest; if the Subject changes into punishment and injury, its strength is surely slight. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 1631–1633 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, lines 1631–1633*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 倘若用爻临绝又无生助者，此人定主无能。 | 如果用爻临绝、又没有生助，这个人一定没有能耐。 | If the Subject line is in extinction and has no generation or support, the man is surely incapable. |
| 用来克世者，实怀欺主之心，彼非仗义之人。 | 用爻来克世，实在怀着欺骗主人的心，他不是仗义的人。 | If the Subject comes to control the Self, he truly harbours a mind to cheat his master and is not a man of loyalty and justice. |
| 若卜六冲之卦，终成反目，岂得和谐？ | 如果卜得六冲的卦，终究反目，怎么能和谐？ | If a six-clash hexagram is cast, they end by falling out, and how can there be harmony? |
| 卦上用爻虽然有气，变成克制之乡或之墓绝，人必先勤后惰，事当有始无终。 | 卦上用爻虽然有气，但变成克制它的地方、或者变入墓、绝，这个人必定先勤后惰，事情当是有始无终。 | Even if the Subject line in the hexagram has qi, if it changes into a place that controls it or into tomb or extinction, the man will surely be diligent at first and idle later, and the affair will have a beginning but no end. |

> **按**:「绝乡」是绝地。「世遭用克却无情」是世爻被用爻所克。「仗义之人」是讲义气、可靠的人。「之墓绝」的「之」是变入。这一条与前一条（EL.131）合起来，是用人的两面：用爻不被冲破空绝是可用，用爻临绝、克世、六冲、变克都是不可用。
>
> **Note:** 'Juexiang' is a place of extinction. 'Shi zao yong ke que wuqing' is the Self line controlled by the Subject line. 'Zhangyi zhi ren' is a man of loyalty and justice, one who can be relied on. The 'zhi' of 'zhi mu jue' is changing into. This entry and the previous one (EL.131) together are the two sides of employing a man: a Subject line that is not clashed, broken, Void or extinct can be employed, while a Subject line in extinction, controlling the Self, in a six-clash or changing into control cannot.

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.131、LY.CAREER.EL.133

---

## LY.CAREER.EL.133 用人藏否：以上七般发不犯方言和悦又多能，若还世得他扶助必受渠恩事有成；应爻并用爻不遇日冲月破空亡死绝又不克世又不变出冲伤、本卦之卦皆非六冲，便曰宾主相投用之有力，用爻若来生世合世者赖以维持周旋吾事

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:以上七样都不犯，才可说他和悦又多能；如果世爻又得他的扶助，必定受他的恩，事情成功。应爻和用爻，不遇日冲、月破、空亡、死绝，又不克世，又不变出冲伤，本卦和之卦都不是六冲，就叫做宾主相投，用他有力；用爻如果来生世、合世，就靠他维持，替我周旋办事。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If none of these seven things is violated, one may say he is amiable and very capable; if the Self also gets his support, one will surely receive his kindness and the affair will succeed. If the Other line and the Subject line do not meet the Day's clash, Month-break, Void, death or extinction, and do not control the Self, and do not change out a clash or injury, and neither the original nor the changed hexagram is a six-clash, it is called host and guest in accord, and the man is strong to employ; if the Subject line comes to generate or combine with the Self, one relies on him to hold things up and he manages my affairs for me.

**《易林补遗·用人藏否章第八十三》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 1634 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 83: Whether the man one employs is good or bad", base text; Yilin Buyi, line 1634*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 以上七般发不犯，方言和悦又多能， | 以上七样都不犯，才可说他和悦又多能； | If none of these seven things is violated, one may say he is amiable and very capable; |
| 若还世得他扶助，必受渠恩事有成。 | 如果世爻又得他的扶助，必定受他的恩，事情成功。 | if the Self also gets his support, one will surely receive his kindness and the affair will succeed. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 1635 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 1635*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 应爻并用爻，不遇日冲、月破、空亡、死绝、又不克世、又不变出冲伤，本卦兴之卦皆非六冲便曰宾主相投，用之有力。 | 应爻和用爻，不遇日冲、月破、空亡、死绝，又不克世，又不变出冲伤，本卦和之卦都不是六冲，就叫做宾主相投，用他有力。 | If the Other line and the Subject line do not meet the Day's clash, Month-break, Void, death or extinction, and do not control the Self, and do not change out a clash or injury, and neither the original nor the changed hexagram is a six-clash, it is called host and guest in accord, and the man is strong to employ. |
| 用爻若来生世合世者，赖以难持，周旋吾事。 | 用爻如果来生世、合世，就靠他维持，替我周旋办事。 | If the Subject line comes to generate or combine with the Self, one relies on him to hold things up and he manages my affairs for me. |

> **按**:「七般」按解里列出的是：日冲、月破、空亡、死绝、克世、变出冲伤、六冲。「本卦兴之卦」疑是「本卦与之卦」，「赖以难持」疑是「赖以维持」，按文意译。「周旋」是替我张罗办事。「宾主」在这里是用人的人和被用的人。
>
> **Note:** The 'seven things' are, by what the explanation lists: the Day's clash, Month-break, Void, death or extinction, controlling the Self, changing out a clash or injury, and a six-clash. The 'xing' of 'ben gua xing zhi gua' is presumably 'yu', and 'ben gua yu zhi gua' is 'the original hexagram and the changed hexagram'; 'lai yi nan chi' is presumably 'lai yi weichi', relying on him to hold up; they are translated by the sense. 'Zhouxuan' is bustling about on my behalf. 'Host and guest' here are the man who employs and the man who is employed.

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.132、LY.CAREER.EL.134

## LY.CAREER.EL.144 平生学艺：无鬼必无头无绪、游魂必游去游回，世犯旬空自有更张之意、应遭月破师无传授之心，蹇遇六冲彼我无情难习业、幸逢六合师徒相得却成功；一应徒师习业不宜卦内无官鬼，世为己应为师皆不可值旬空月破又不宜六冲，世应不空又遇六合定主师徒相得事必成功；欲择明师未知曲直当占易卦便见虚真

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:没有官鬼，必定没有头绪；游魂，必定游去游回。世爻犯旬空，自己有改弦更张的意思；应爻遭月破，师父没有传授的心。不幸遇到六冲，彼此无情，难以学业；幸而逢六合，师徒相得，反倒成功。凡是徒弟向师父学业，不适宜卦内没有官鬼；如果逢空，事情没有头绪；卦值游魂，往返不定；又看世爻是自己、应爻是师父，都不可值旬空、月破，又不适宜卦值六冲；世应如果不空亡，又遇到爻逢六合，一定主师徒相得、事必成功。想选择明师，不知道曲直好坏，应当占一个易卦，就能看到真假。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** With no Pressure there is surely no clue to the matter, and a Wandering Soul surely means going off and returning; if the Self falls on the ten-day Void one is inclined to change one's course, and if the Other meets Month-break the teacher has no heart to pass on his teaching; meeting a six-clash is unlucky, for the two have no feeling for each other and it is hard to learn the trade; luckily meeting a six-combine, master and pupil suit each other and succeed. For all pupils learning under masters, it is not fitting for the hexagram to have no Pressure line in it; if it meets Void, the matter has no clue; if the hexagram falls on a Wandering Soul, one goes back and forth and nothing is settled; look also at the Self as oneself and the Other as the teacher, neither of which may fall on the ten-day Void or Month-break, and it is also unfitting for the hexagram to be a six-clash; if the Self and the Other are not Void and the lines also meet a six-combine, master and pupil will surely suit each other and the matter will surely succeed. To choose a bright teacher when one does not know the crooked from the straight, one should divine a hexagram, and the false and true will be seen at once.

**《易林补遗·平生学艺章第二十三》原文** — 《易林补遗》第 688–689 行 · *Yilin Buyi (Supplement to the Forest of Changes), "Chapter 23: Learning a craft or art for one's whole life", base text; Yilin Buyi, lines 688–689*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 无鬼必无头无绪，游魂必游去游回。 | 没有官鬼，必定没有头绪；游魂，必定游去游回。 | With no Pressure there is surely no clue to the matter, and a Wandering Soul surely means going off and returning. |
| 世犯旬空，自有更张之意。 | 世爻犯旬空，自己有改弦更张的意思； | If the Self falls on the ten-day Void, one is inclined to change one's course; |
| 应遭月破，师无传授之心。 | 应爻遭月破，师父没有传授的心。 | if the Other meets Month-break, the teacher has no heart to pass on his teaching. |
| 蹇遇六冲，彼我无情难习业。 | 不幸遇到六冲，彼此无情，难以学业； | Meeting a six-clash is unlucky, for the two have no feeling for each other and it is hard to learn the trade; |
| 幸逢六合，师徒相得却成功。 | 幸而逢六合，师徒相得，反倒成功。 | luckily meeting a six-combine, master and pupil suit each other and succeed. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 690 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 690*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 一应徒师习业，不宜卦内无官鬼。 | 凡是徒弟向师父学业，不适宜卦内没有官鬼。 | For all pupils learning under masters, it is not fitting for the hexagram to have no Pressure line in it. |
| 若逢空，事无头绪，卦值游魂，往返不定。 | 如果逢空，事情没有头绪；卦值游魂，往返不定。 | If it meets Void, the matter has no clue; if the hexagram falls on a Wandering Soul, one goes back and forth and nothing is settled. |
| 又看世为己应为师，皆不可值旬空月破，又不宜卦值六冲。 | 又看世爻是自己、应爻是师父，都不可值旬空、月破，又不适宜卦值六冲。 | Look also at the Self as oneself and the Other as the teacher, neither of which may fall on the ten-day Void or Month-break, and it is also unfitting for the hexagram to be a six-clash. |
| 世应若不空亡，又遇爻逢六合，定主师徒相得事必成功。 | 世应如果不空亡，又遇到爻逢六合，一定主师徒相得、事必成功。 | If the Self and the Other are not Void and the lines also meet a six-combine, master and pupil will surely suit each other and the matter will surely succeed. |

**《易林补遗》诀下的解说** — 《易林补遗》第 691 行 · *Yilin Buyi, prose explanation under the verse; Yilin Buyi, line 691*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 欲择明师，未知曲直，当占易卦，便见虚真。 | 想选择明师，不知道曲直好坏，应当占一个易卦，就能看到真假。 | To choose a bright teacher when one does not know the crooked from the straight, one should divine a hexagram, and the false and true will be seen at once. |

> **按**:「游魂」是八宫卦里每宫的第七卦（见术语表）。「更张」是改变做法。「蹇」在这里是不幸、运气艰难（与下句的「幸」相对），不是蹇卦。「徒师习业」是徒弟跟师父学业；「明师」是高明的师父。这一章后半讲的是学手艺时的师徒关系，与前面几条（EL.140 起）合起来，是学艺从选行业、取用神到选师父的整个过程。
>
> **Note:** 'Wandering Soul' is the seventh hexagram of each of the eight palaces (see the glossary). 'Gengzhang' is changing one's way of doing things. 'Jian' here is ill luck and a hard fortune (set against the 'luckily' of the next line) and is not the Jian hexagram. 'Tushi xiye' is a pupil learning under a master, and 'mingshi' is a brilliant teacher. The second half of this chapter speaks of the relation of master and pupil in learning a trade, and together with the earlier entries (from EL.140) it is the whole course of learning a craft, from choosing a trade and the Subject to choosing a master.

相关:LY.CAREER.EL.143

## LY.CAREER.HDY.04 文书章·见贵求谋问立身：文书不动及时辰，财动文书空费力，子动伤官事不亨；事见两爻须再觅、官多宛转恐艰难，最要日辰生合出则知音处处得前程

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:拜见贵人、求谋、问立身：文书（父母）不动、又赶上时辰，可成；财动，文书白费力；子孙动，伤了官鬼，事情不亨通。事情现出两个爻，要再去找；官多，辗转曲折，恐怕艰难；最要紧的是日辰生合它、让它出来，那就处处遇到知音、得到前程。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** For calling on a noble, scheming for a post and asking about establishing oneself: with the documents (Resource) still and in step with the time it can be done; Wealth moving, the documents are spent in vain; Output moving injures Pressure and the matter does not go through. If the matter shows two lines, seek again; with many Pressure lines it winds and turns and may be hard; what matters most is that the Day generates or combines and brings it out, and then one finds those who know one everywhere and a future.

**《海底眼·文书》原文** — 《海底眼》第 636 行 · *Haidi Yan (The Eye at the Bottom of the Sea), "Documents", base text; Haidi Yan, line 636*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 见贵求谋问立身， | 拜见贵人、求谋、问立身处世， | To call on a noble, to scheme for a post, to ask how to establish oneself, |
| 文书不动及时辰， | 文书（父母）不动、又赶上时辰， | the documents (Resource) still and in step with the time, |
| 财动文书空费力， | 财动，文书白费力气； | Wealth moving, the documents are spent in vain; |
| 子动伤官事不亨。 | 子孙动，伤了官鬼，事情不亨通。 | Output moving injures Pressure and the matter does not go through. |

**《海底眼·文书》原文** — 《海底眼》第 638 行 · *Haidi Yan (The Eye at the Bottom of the Sea), "Documents", base text; Haidi Yan, line 638*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 又曰： | 它又说： | It says again: |
| 事见两爻须再觅， | 事情现出两个爻，要再去找； | if the matter shows two lines, seek again; |
| 官多宛转恐艰难， | 官多，辗转曲折，恐怕艰难； | with many Pressure lines it winds and turns and may be hard; |
| 最要日辰生合出， | 最要紧的是日辰生合它、让它出来， | what matters most is that the Day generates or combines and brings it out, |
| 知音处处得前程。 | 那就处处遇到知音、得到前程。 | and then one finds those who know one everywhere and a future. |

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《易林补遗》:同文 第 2185 行:财动文书空费力;同文 第 2185 行:子动伤官事不亨
- 《断易天机》:异文(相似 0.86) 第 2563 行:事见两爻须再;异文(相似 0.86) 第 2563 行:官多宛转恐难;异文(相似 0.86) 第 2563 行:最要日辰生合;同文 第 2563 行:知音处处得前程

## LY.CAREER.HDY.05 文书章：六爻中只一爻动最忌兄弟动；文书与贵人原无亦难成，出现有气亦怕空亡；求官与文书要旺相持世有成；应爻不克事体分明；乾兑坎宫谋事不一；见官用动其人多出

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:六爻中如果只有一爻动，最忌讳兄弟爻动，事情不实在、难成。如果文书和贵人在卦中原本没有，也难成；出现了、有气，也怕空亡——所说的动爻急得像火攻，急着落入空亡。求官和文书，要旺相、持世才有成。应爻不受克，事体就分明；乾、兑、坎宫，谋事不专一；见官员，用爻发动，那人多半出门，叫也是白叫。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If only one line moves among the six, what is most to be avoided is Peer moving: the matter is not real and is hard to accomplish. If the documents and the Noble are originally absent from the hexagram, it is also hard to accomplish; if they appear and have qi they still fear Void, as is said, a moving line is urgent like an attack by fire, hurrying into Void. For seeking office and documents, they must be strong and hold the Self to succeed. If the Other line is not controlled, the matter is clear; in the palaces Qian, Dui and Kan the planning is not single-minded; in seeing an official, if the Subject line moves, the man has mostly gone out, and calling for him is in vain.

**《海底眼·文书》原文** — 《海底眼》第 640 行 · *Haidi Yan (The Eye at the Bottom of the Sea), "Documents", base text; Haidi Yan, line 640*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 如六爻中， | 如果六爻之中， | If among the six lines |
| 只一爻动， | 只有一爻发动， | only one line moves, |
| 最忌兄弟动， | 最忌讳兄弟爻动， | what is most to be avoided is Peer moving, |
| 事不实而难成。 | 事情不实在，难以成功。 | the matter is not real and is hard to accomplish. |

**《海底眼·文书》原文** — 《海底眼》第 642 行 · *Haidi Yan (The Eye at the Bottom of the Sea), "Documents", base text; Haidi Yan, line 642*

| 亦难成。 | 也难成功。 | it is also hard to accomplish. |
| 出现有气， | 出现了、有气， | if they appear and have qi, |
| 亦怕空亡， | 也怕空亡， | they still fear Void, |
| 所谓动爻急如火攻， | 所说的动爻急得像火攻， | as is said, a moving line is urgent like an attack by fire, |
| 急落空亡。 | 急着落入空亡。 | hurrying into Void. |
| 求官与文书， | 求官和文书， | For seeking office and documents, |
| 要旺相持世有成。 | 要旺相、持世才有成。 | they must be strong and hold the Self to succeed. |

**《海底眼·文书》原文** — 《海底眼》第 644 行 · *Haidi Yan (The Eye at the Bottom of the Sea), "Documents", base text; Haidi Yan, line 644*
