## LY.CAREER.YM.85 营谋·谋事五忌：谋事在人营求不一皆物交物而成，故六冲则不交、合而冲则将交而散、应空散则彼心不附、世空散则我意先慵、官鬼失而谋无主；唯世应破绝犹得其半（破绝有形空散无迹）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:谋事在人，营求的办法不一，都是物与物相交才成。所以六冲就交不到一起；合处逢冲，就将要交合却又（此处疑脱一字，当是「散」）；应空、散，对方的心就不依附；世空、散，我自己的意思先懒了；只有世应破、绝，还能得到一半；官鬼失去，谋事就没有主。这是五种忌。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Scheming rests with men; the ways of seeking are not one, and all of them are accomplished by one thing meeting another. So with a six-clash they do not meet; with a combination clashed, they are about to join and then (a character seems lost here, presumably 'come apart'); with the Other Void or scattered, his heart does not attach to me; with the Self Void or scattered, my own will first turns slack; only with the Self and the Other broken or extinct is half still obtained; with Pressure lost, the scheme has no master. These are the five taboos.

**《易冒·营谋章第七十五》原文** — 《易冒》第 1640 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 75: Scheming and entrusting", base text; Yi Mao, line 1640*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 夫谋事在人，营求不一，皆物交物而成也。 | 谋事在人，营求的办法不一，都是物与物相交才成。 | Scheming rests with men; the ways of seeking are not one, and all of them are accomplished by one thing meeting another. |
| 故六冲则不交， | 所以六冲就交不到一起； | So with a six-clash they do not meet; |
| 合而冲，则将交而， | 合处逢冲，就将要交合却又（此处疑脱一字，当是「散」）； | with a combination clashed, they are about to join and then (a character seems lost here, presumably 'come apart'); |
| 应空散，则彼心不附， | 应空、散，对方的心就不依附； | with the Other Void or scattered, his heart does not attach to me; |
| 世空散，则我意先慵， | 世空、散，我自己的意思先懒了； | with the Self Void or scattered, my own will first turns slack; |
| 唯世应破绝，犹得其半， | 只有世应破、绝，还能得到一半； | only with the Self and the Other broken or extinct is half still obtained; |
| 官鬼失而谋无主，此五者之忌也。 | 官鬼失去，谋事就没有主，这是五种忌。 | with Pressure lost, the scheme has no master. These are the five taboos. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 1641 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 1641*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 破绝有形，空散无迹。 | 破、绝是有形的，空、散是没影的。 | Broken and extinct have a form; Void and scattered leave no trace. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 1639 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 1639*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 内附嘱托 | 本章里面附有嘱托一类的占问。 | This chapter has attached to it divinations about entrusting a matter to another. |

> **按**:「谋事在人」是谋事要靠人去做，「营求」是经营求取，「物交物」是事物和事物相交、两边凑到一起才成。「六冲则不交」是六冲（散）就凑不到一起；「合而冲」是合处逢冲（见 YM.07 注）。「则将交而」后面原文疑脱一字，当是「散」，按文意译作「将要交合却又散开」。「应空散则彼心不附」是应是对方，应空、散就是对方没有心；「世空散则我意先慵」是世是自己，世空、散就是自己先懒了。「世应破绝，犹得其半」：破、绝是有形的，力量残存，所以还能成一半；空、散是没影的，所以是忌。「官鬼失而谋无主」是官鬼是谋事的主，没有它事无主持（见 YM.88）。五忌就是：六冲、合冲、应空散、世空散、官鬼失。这一章是营谋（谋求一件事）与嘱托（托人办事），和易隐谋望占（YY.82–88）、易林补遗谋望成事（EL.103 起）取同一类，章首「内附嘱托」就是嘱托也在这里。
>
> **Note:** 'Moushi zai ren' is that scheming has to be done by men, 'yingqiu' is managing and seeking, and 'wu jiao wu' is one thing meeting another, so that the two sides come together and it succeeds. 'With a six-clash they do not meet' is that a six-clash (scattering) cannot bring them together; 'a combination clashed' is a combination meeting a clash (see the note to YM.07). Below 'ze jiang jiao er' the original seems to lack a character, presumably 'san', scatter, and by the sense it is translated 'about to join and then comes apart'. 'The Other Void or scattered, his heart does not attach to me' is that the Other is the other side, and the Other Void or scattered means he has no heart for it; 'the Self Void or scattered, my own will first turns slack' is that the Self is myself, and the Self Void or scattered means I am the first to go slack. 'The Self and the Other broken or extinct, half is still obtained': broken and extinct have a form, some force remains, so half can still be had; Void and scattered leave no trace, so they are taboo. 'Pressure lost, the scheme has no master' is that Pressure is the master of a scheme, and without it the matter has no one to run it (see YM.88). The five taboos are: a six-clash, a combination clashed, the Other Void or scattered, the Self Void or scattered, and Pressure lost. This chapter is about scheming (seeking something) and entrusting (asking someone to act for one), of a kind with the Yi Yin on scheming and hope (YY.82-88) and the Yilin Buyi on accomplishing a scheme (from EL.103); 'attached: entrusting' at the head of the chapter means entrusting is treated here too.

相关:LY.CAREER.YM.86、LY.CAREER.YM.88、LY.CAREER.YY.82、LY.CAREER.YY.84、LY.CAREER.EL.103、LY.CAREER.EL.104

---

## LY.CAREER.YM.86 营谋·成否与吉凶：自力可成莫观其应、因人成事莫观其世；止问成否但忌空冲、兼问吉凶并求用旺；谋文用父、谋利用财、释患用子（占己谋不论应、占彼谋不论世；问成占谋、问吉凶占用）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:所以靠自己的力量能办成的，不用看应；靠别人才能成的事，不用看世。只问成不成，只忌空、冲；兼问吉凶，就要同时求用神旺。谋文书（功名）用父，谋利用财，解除祸患用子。占自己谋的事不论应，占别人谋的事不论世；问成不成看谋（世应、冲空），问吉凶看用（用神）。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** So for what can be done by one's own strength, one need not look at the Other; for what is done through others, one need not look at the Self. If one asks only whether it comes off, one fears only Void and clash; if one also asks about good and ill, one must also seek the Subject thriving. For documents (a name) use the father line, for profit use Wealth, and for relieving trouble use Output. In divining a scheme of one's own, the Other is not discussed; in divining another's scheme, the Self is not discussed; asking whether it comes off one looks at the scheme (Self, Other, clash, Void), asking about good and ill one looks at the Subject.

**《易冒·营谋章第七十五》原文** — 《易冒》第 1642 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 75: Scheming and entrusting", base text; Yi Mao, line 1642*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 故自力可成，莫观其应， | 所以靠自己的力量能办成的，不用看应； | So for what can be done by one's own strength, one need not look at the Other; |
| 因人成事，莫观其世。 | 靠别人才能成的事，不用看世。 | for what is done through others, one need not look at the Self. |
| 止问成否，但忌空冲， | 只问成不成，只忌空、冲； | If one asks only whether it comes off, one fears only Void and clash; |
| 兼问吉凶，并求用旺， | 兼问吉凶，就要同时求用神旺。 | if one also asks about good and ill, one must also seek the Subject thriving. |
| 谋文用父，谋利用财，释患用子。 | 谋文书用父，谋利用财，解除祸患用子。 | For documents use the father line, for profit use Wealth, and for relieving trouble use Output. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 1643 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 1643*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 占己谋不论应，占彼谋不论世。 | 占自己谋的事不论应，占别人谋的事不论世； | In divining a scheme of one's own, the Other is not discussed; in divining another's scheme, the Self is not discussed; |
| 问成占谋，问吉凶占用。 | 问成不成看谋，问吉凶看用。 | asking whether it comes off one looks at the scheme, asking about good and ill one looks at the Subject. |

> **按**:「自力可成」是靠自己的力量能办成的事，不用看应；「因人成事」是靠别人才办成的事，不用看世。「止问成否」是只问成不成，「兼问吉凶」是还要问好坏。「谋文用父」是谋文书、功名用父母，「谋利用财」是谋利用妻财，「释患用子」是解除祸患用子孙（子孙克官鬼）。「用旺」是用神旺相。书里自注：占自己谋的事不论应、占别人谋的事不论世；问成不成看谋（世应、冲空），问吉凶看用（用神）。这一条把『成不成』和『好不好』分成两件事：前者看世应的空冲，后者看用神的旺衰。与易隐谋望占的可否成败（YY.83–YY.85）对着看。
>
> **Note:** 'Zili ke cheng' is what can be done by one's own strength, so one need not look at the Other; 'yin ren cheng shi' is what is done only through others, so one need not look at the Self. 'Zhi wen cheng fou' is asking only whether it comes off, and 'jian wen jixiong' is also asking about good and ill. 'For documents use the father line' is using Resource for documents and a name, 'for profit use Wealth' is using the Wealth line, and 'for relieving trouble use Output' is using Output (Output controls Pressure). 'Yong wang' is the Subject thriving. The book's own gloss: in divining a scheme of one's own the Other is not discussed, and in divining another's scheme the Self is not discussed; asking whether it comes off one looks at the scheme (the Self and the Other, clash and Void), and asking about good and ill one looks at the Subject. This entry separates 'whether it comes off' from 'whether it is good' as two things: the first is seen by the Void and clash of the Self and the Other, the second by the strength of the Subject. Read against the question of what can and cannot be done in the Yi Yin on scheming (YY.83-85).

相关:LY.CAREER.YM.85、LY.CAREER.YM.87、LY.CAREER.YY.83、LY.CAREER.YY.85、LY.CAREER.EL.103

---

## LY.CAREER.YM.89 营谋·嘱托的体与用：专问嘱托重体、专问所谋重用，问嘱托而兼求用神者体用两全事无不吉；嘱托体病虽用得位无成；本占为得不可贰

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:凡是专问嘱托的，重体；专问所谋的事的，重用；问嘱托而又兼求用神的，体用两全，事情没有不吉的。然而嘱托的体有病，即使用神得位也不成。所以以眼前这一占为得，不可有二心。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** When one asks only about the entrusting, the body counts for most; when one asks only about the matter schemed for, the use counts for most; when one asks about the entrusting and also seeks the Subject, with body and use both whole, there is no matter that is not auspicious. But if the body of the entrusting is sick, even if the Subject is well placed it does not come off. So the divination at hand is what is to be taken, and one must not be of two minds.

**《易冒·营谋章第七十五》原文** — 《易冒》第 1647 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 75: Scheming and entrusting", base text; Yi Mao, line 1647*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 盖凡专问嘱托则重体，专问所谋则重用， | 凡是专问嘱托的，重体；专问所谋的事的，重用； | When one asks only about the entrusting, the body counts for most; when one asks only about the matter schemed for, the use counts for most; |
| 问嘱托而兼求用神者，体用两全，事无不吉。 | 问嘱托而又兼求用神的，体用两全，事情没有不吉的。 | when one asks about the entrusting and also seeks the Subject, with body and use both whole, there is no matter that is not auspicious. |
| 然嘱托体病，虽用得位无成。 | 然而嘱托的体有病，即使用神得位也不成。 | But if the body of the entrusting is sick, even if the Subject is well placed it does not come off. |
| 故本占为得，不可贰也。 | 所以以眼前这一占为得，不可有二心。 | So the divination at hand is what is to be taken, and one must not be of two minds. |

> **按**:「体」是受托的这个人所对应的爻（他肯不肯出力），「用」是所谋的这件事的用神（谋文用父、谋利用财等，见 YM.86）。「专问嘱托则重体」是只问托人这件事，看托的那个人；「专问所谋则重用」是只问事情本身，看用神；两样都问，就体用两全才吉。「嘱托体病，虽用得位无成」是托的人有病（空、破、克世等），事情的用神再好也成不了：人靠不住，事就落空。「本占为得，不可贰」是以所占的这一件为准，不要同时拿两件事去凑。
>
> **Note:** The 'body' is the line that corresponds to the person entrusted (whether he is willing to exert himself), and the 'use' is the Subject of the matter schemed for (documents by the father line, profit by Wealth, and so on, see YM.86). 'Asking only about the entrusting counts the body' is asking only the matter of entrusting someone, looking at the person entrusted; 'asking only about the matter schemed for counts the use' is asking only about the matter itself, looking at the Subject; if both are asked, it is auspicious only when body and use are both whole. 'If the body of the entrusting is sick, even if the Subject is well placed it does not come off' is that if the person entrusted is ailing (Void, broken, controlling the Self and so on), however good the Subject of the matter, it cannot come off: if the man cannot be relied on, the matter comes to nothing. 'The divination at hand is what is to be taken, and one must not be of two minds' is to go by the one matter divined and not to piece two matters together at once.

相关:LY.CAREER.YM.86、LY.CAREER.YM.88、LY.CAREER.EL.137

---

## LY.CAREER.YM.92 道业·世空三冲的喜恶：出家而三冲世空为超然物外如孤鹤横空、求道而三冲世空为尘务撄心如困鱼失水；卜出世三冲解网世空无挂碍、卜入道则空无成

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:然而世空有喜与恶的分别，三冲有宜与忌的分别：出家而三冲、世空，是超然物外，像孤鹤横空；求道而三冲、世空，是尘世杂务缠心，像困鱼失水。占出世，三冲是解开（俗世的）网，世空是没有牵挂；若占入道，那么空就成不了。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Yet the Self Void has a distinction of welcome and unwelcome, and the three clashes a distinction of suitable and unsuitable: for one leaving home, the three clashes and the Self Void are being detached from the world, like a lone crane crossing the sky; for one seeking the Way, the three clashes and the Self Void are worldly business tangling the heart, like a trapped fish out of water. In divining about leaving the world, the three clashes are undoing the net and the Self Void is having nothing hanging on one's mind; if one divines about entering the Way, then Void means it does not come off.

**《易冒·道业章第九十》原文** — 《易冒》第 1961–1963 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 90: The monastic vocation", base text; Yi Mao, lines 1961–1963*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 然世空有喜恶之辨也，三冲有宜忌之分也， | 然而世空有喜与恶的分别，三冲有宜与忌的分别： | Yet the Self Void has a distinction of welcome and unwelcome, and the three clashes a distinction of suitable and unsuitable: |
| 出家而三冲世空，为超然物外，如孤鹤横空。 | 出家而三冲、世空，是超然物外，像孤鹤横空； | for one leaving home, the three clashes and the Self Void are being detached from the world, like a lone crane crossing the sky; |
| 求道而三冲世空为尘务撄心，如困鱼失水。 | 求道而三冲、世空，是尘世杂务缠心，像困鱼失水。 | for one seeking the Way, the three clashes and the Self Void are worldly business tangling the heart, like a trapped fish out of water. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 1964 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 1964*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 卜出世， | 占卜出世（出家）， | In divining about leaving the world, |
| 三冲谓解网，世空谓无挂碍， | 三冲是解开（俗世的）网，世空是没有牵挂； | the three clashes are undoing the net, and the Self Void is having nothing hanging on one's mind; |
| 若卜入道，则空无成也。 | 若占入道，那么空就成不了。 | if one divines about entering the Way, then Void means it does not come off. |

> **按**:「三冲」（见 YM.07 注）是冲散、断开；对出家（离开俗世）的人，冲散俗缘是好事，所以宜；对要修行的人（还在俗世里求道），冲散会把修行也断掉，所以忌。「世空」同样：离俗的人世空是无牵无挂，求道的人世空是心里空了、无从落脚。「撄心」是扰乱心神。「出世」是出家离俗，「入道」是进入修行。书里自注把这两种问法分开：卜出世，三冲是解网，世空是无挂碍；卜入道，空就无成。同一个卦象，问的是『出去』还是『进来』，喜恶相反。
>
> **Note:** The 'three clashes' (see the note to YM.07) are scattering and cutting apart; for one leaving the world (leaving the secular), scattering the worldly ties is a good thing and so suitable; for one who is to practise (still seeking the Way in the world), scattering will cut off the practice too and so is unsuitable. The 'Self Void' is the same: for one leaving the secular it is having nothing hanging on one's mind, and for one seeking the Way it is the heart gone empty with nowhere to rest. 'Yingxin' is disturbing the mind. 'Chushi' is leaving home and the secular, and 'rudao' is entering practice. The book's own note keeps the two ways of asking apart: divining about leaving the world, the three clashes are undoing the net and the Self Void is having nothing on one's mind; divining about entering the Way, Void means it does not come off. For the same image, whether one asks about 'going out' or 'coming in', welcome and unwelcome are opposite.

相关:LY.CAREER.YM.91、LY.CAREER.YM.93

---

## LY.CAREER.YM.94 道业·刻期取证、指日还丹：世空无成、六冲多阻、世病则九仞井弃、鬼发则一丈魔高，唯世旺福兴而丹成悟彻（止问求道，世空世病六冲鬼动为四戒）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:限定日期取证（证道）、指日还丹（炼丹成功）：世空就无成，六冲就多阻碍，世病就像掘井九仞而放弃，鬼发就是魔高一丈；只有世旺、福兴，才丹成悟彻。只问求道，世空、世病、六冲、鬼动，这是四戒。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** For fixing a date for attaining realisation and counting the days to the return of the elixir: the Self Void, and nothing comes of it; a six-clash, and there are many obstructions; a sick Self, and it is digging a well nine fathoms deep and giving it up; Pressure issuing, and the devil stands ten feet high; only with the Self thriving and Output rising is the elixir completed and the realisation thorough. In asking only about seeking the Way, the Self Void, the Self sick, a six-clash and Pressure moving are the four warnings.

**《易冒·道业章第九十》原文** — 《易冒》第 1967 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 90: The monastic vocation", base text; Yi Mao, line 1967*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 刻期取证，指日还丹， | 限定日期取证，指日还丹， | For fixing a date for attaining realisation and counting the days to the return of the elixir, |
| 世空则无成，六冲则多阻， | 世空就无成，六冲就多阻碍， | the Self Void, and nothing comes of it; a six-clash, and there are many obstructions; |
| 世病则九仞井弃，鬼发则一丈魔高。 | 世病就像掘井九仞而放弃，鬼发就是魔高一丈； | a sick Self, and it is digging a well nine fathoms deep and giving it up; Pressure issuing, and the devil stands ten feet high; |
| 唯世旺福兴，而丹成悟徹。 | 只有世旺、福兴，才丹成悟彻。 | only with the Self thriving and Output rising is the elixir completed and the realisation thorough. |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 1968 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 1968*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 止问求道， | 只问求道， | In asking only about seeking the Way, |
| 世空、世病、六冲、鬼动为四戒。 | 世空、世病、六冲、鬼动，这是四戒。 | the Self Void, the Self sick, a six-clash and Pressure moving are the four warnings. |

> **按**:「刻期」是限定日期，「取证」是修行证道（得到证悟），「指日还丹」是指着日子炼成还丹（道家内丹的成功）。「九仞井弃」出自「掘井九仞而不及泉，犹为弃井也」，是功亏一篑；「一丈魔高」是「道高一尺、魔高一丈」，修行越深障碍越大。「丹成悟彻」是丹炼成、悟透彻。书里自注：只问求道的时候，世空、世病、六冲、鬼动是四个要戒的。与 YM.91 的三戒对着看，这里多了一个六冲。
>
> **Note:** 'Keqi' is fixing a date, 'quzheng' is attaining realisation in practice, and 'zhiri huandan' is counting the days to the completion of the returned elixir (success in Daoist inner alchemy). 'Digging a well nine fathoms deep and giving it up' comes from 'digging a well nine fathoms and not reaching water is still an abandoned well', failing for want of the last basket of earth; 'the devil stands ten feet high' is 'the Way is a foot high, the devil is ten feet high', the deeper the practice the greater the obstacle. 'Dancheng wuche' is the elixir completed and the realisation thorough. The book's own note: when asking only about seeking the Way, the Self Void, the Self sick, a six-clash and Pressure moving are the four to be warned against. Read against the three warnings of YM.91; here a six-clash is added.

相关:LY.CAREER.YM.91、LY.CAREER.YM.93、LY.CAREER.YM.95

---

## LY.CAREER.YM.104 无鬼章·功名无鬼：功名无鬼则进取无成、升迁未遂（此言用鬼，鬼不宜无）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:功名没有官鬼（官鬼不上卦），进取就没有成就，升迁也不能如愿。这是说取官鬼为用的占问，官鬼不宜没有。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** In seeking a name and office with no Pressure line in the hexagram, advancement comes to nothing and promotion is not achieved. This speaks of divinations in which Pressure is taken as the Subject, and Pressure should not be absent.

**《易冒·无鬼章第三十二》原文** — 《易冒》第 556 行 · *Yi Mao, "Chapter 32: No Pressure line", base text; Yi Mao, line 556*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 功名无鬼， | 功名没有官鬼， | In seeking a name and office with no Pressure line, |
| 进取无成， | 进取就没有成就， | advancement comes to nothing |
| 升迁未遂； | 升迁也不能如愿； | and promotion is not achieved; |

**《易冒》括号小注(书里没有标注者)** — 《易冒》第 557 行 · *Bracketed small note in the Yi Mao (author not named in the book); Yi Mao, line 557*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 此言用鬼，鬼不宜无也。 | 这是说取官鬼为用的占问，官鬼不宜没有。 | This speaks of divinations in which Pressure is taken as the Subject, and Pressure should not be absent. |

> **按**:「功名无鬼」是占功名时官鬼不上卦（官鬼是功名的用神，见 YM.40）。「进取无成」是想进取没有成就，「升迁未遂」是想升迁不能如愿。书里自注：这是说取鬼（官鬼）为用的占问，鬼不宜没有。「无」要分真无和伏藏：同一章稍前（L550）说，卦中虽然没有、但伏在世爻或动爻之下，或者变出来临在日辰月建之上，就「无犹不无」，所以这里说的是官鬼既不上卦、又没有伏变的真无。同一句里还有身命、家宅、谋望无鬼的断法，分属别的问题域，这里只收功名的一半。和 YM.42 的「毋谓官伏，得助亦升」对着看。
>
> **Note:** 'Gongming wu gui' is Pressure being absent from the hexagram when divining about a name and office (Pressure is the Subject of fame and office, see YM.40). 'Jinqu wu cheng' is that advancement comes to nothing, and 'shengqian wei sui' is that promotion is not achieved. The book's own gloss: this speaks of divinations in which Pressure is taken as the Subject, and Pressure should not be absent. 'Absent' must be told apart as truly absent and hidden: slightly earlier in the same chapter (L550) it says that though it is not in the hexagram, if it is hidden under the Self line or a moving line, or if it changes out and sits on the Day or Month branch, then 'absent is as if not absent', so what is meant here is Pressure truly absent, neither in the hexagram nor hidden or changed. The same sentence also has judgments for no Pressure in a person's fate, the household and scheming, which belong to other domains, and only the half about fame and office is taken here. Read against 'do not say Pressure hidden means no promotion: with help it is promoted too' in YM.42.

相关:LY.CAREER.YM.42、LY.CAREER.YM.40、LY.CAREER.HJC.06

---

## LY.CAREER.YY.07 官禄占·候选（一）：官临身世与财爻独发，或世官加书贵带龙雀动，或岁月带官印生合世身者得选；世下伏官伏驿马印绶迟选；卦无官印、身世空绝、子孙持世独发不得选；鬼化鬼反覆，鬼动财空选司无力；身世随鬼入墓选不成

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:候选（等待选官）——能选上：官星临在身爻、世爻上，财爻单独发动；或者世爻、官星加文书、贵人，带青龙或朱雀发动；或者太岁、月建带着官星、印星来生合世身。选得迟：官星伏在世爻下，或伏着驿马、印绶。选在下等：世爻持官星、贵人遇升爻，但休囚受克。选不上：卦里没有官星、印星，或它们空绝，或身爻世爻空绝，或子孙持世旺动、子孙单独发动；官鬼变官鬼，事情多反复；官鬼发动而财空绝，选官的衙门没有力量；官星入绝但逢生，事虽有阻挠，终得贵人之力而成；身爻世爻随官鬼入墓，选不成，成了也不如意；遇丁未、戊戌二杀之墓，即使选了官、到了任，也要带着亲人回乡；官鬼带亡神劫杀大杀克世身，选了官也不得赴任；有官星而无父母，或父母空死墓绝胎，没有任所。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Awaiting selection. Selected: the star of office sits on the Body or the Self and the Wealth line alone moves; or the Self and the star of office carry the document and the Noble and move with the Azure Dragon or the Vermilion Bird; or Tai Sui and the Month carry the star of office and seal and generate or combine with the Self and Body. Late selection: the star of office or the Post Horse or seal lies hidden under the Self. A low selection: the Self holds the star of office and the Noble on a promotion line but is resting or trapped and controlled. Not selected: the hexagram has no star of office or seal, or they are Void or extinct, or the Body and Self are Void or extinct, or Output holds the Self and is thriving and moving, or Output alone moves. Pressure turning into Pressure: the matter goes back and forth; Pressure moves while Wealth is Void or extinct: the selecting office has no power; the star of office extinct but meeting generation: the matter meets obstruction but is in the end achieved through a patron's help; the Body and Self following Pressure into the tomb: the selection fails, and if it succeeds it is not as wished; meeting the tombs of the two baleful spirits Dingwei and Wuxu: though selected and arrived at the post, he travels home with his kin; Pressure carrying the Lost Spirit, Robbery and great baleful spirits and controlling the Self and Body: though selected he cannot go to his post; the star of office present but no Resource, or Resource Void, dead, in tomb, extinct or in the womb stage: there is no post.

**《易隐·官禄占》原文** — 《易隐》第 2617 行 · *Yi Yin (What Lies Hidden in the Changes), "Divining office and salary", base text; Yi Yin, line 2617*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 官临身世，与财爻独发，或世官加书贵，带龙雀动，或岁月带官印生合世身者，选也。 | 官星临在身爻、世爻上，与财爻单独发动，或者世爻、官星加上文书、贵人，带着青龙或朱雀发动，或者太岁、月建带着官星、印星来生合世身的，能被选上。 | If the star of office sits on the Body or the Self and the Wealth line alone moves, or the Self and the star of office carry the document and the Noble and move with the Azure Dragon or the Vermilion Bird, or Tai Sui and the Month carry the star of office and seal and generate or combine with the Self and Body, one will be selected. |
| 世下伏官，或伏驿马印绶者，迟选。 | 世爻下面伏着官星，或者伏着驿马、印绶的，选得迟。 | If the star of office lies hidden under the Self, or the Post Horse or seal lies hidden there, the selection is late. |
| 世持官贵值升爻，而休囚受克者，下选。 | 世爻持官星、贵人，遇到升爻，但休囚又受克的，选在下等。 | If the Self holds the star of office and the Noble on a promotion line but is resting or trapped and controlled, the selection is a low one. |
| 世加夜贵带龙雀动者，方催选陪点也。 | 世爻加夜贵人、带青龙或朱雀发动的，正是催着选官、陪着点名的时候。 | If the Self carries the night Noble and moves with the Dragon or the Bird, it is just the time when the selection is being pressed and one is called in company. |
| 卦无官印，与官印空绝，或身世空绝，或子持世旺动，或子孙独发者，俱不得选也。 | 卦里没有官星、印星，或者官星、印星落空入绝，或者身爻、世爻落空入绝，或者子孙持世旺相发动，或者子孙单独发动的，都选不上。 | If the hexagram has no star of office or seal, or they are Void or extinct, or the Body and Self are Void or extinct, or Output holds the Self and is thriving and moving, or Output alone moves, none of these is selected. |
| 鬼化鬼者，事多反覆也。 | 官鬼变官鬼的，事情多有反复。 | If Pressure turns into Pressure, the matter goes back and forth. |
| 鬼动而财空绝者，选司无力也。 | 官鬼发动而财落空入绝的，负责选官的衙门没有力量。 | If Pressure moves while Wealth is Void or extinct, the selecting office has no power. |
| 官绝逢生者，事虽有沮，终得贵人力而成也。 | 官星入绝但逢生的，事情虽然有阻挠，最终得到贵人的力量而成。 | If the star of office is extinct but meets generation, the matter meets obstruction but is in the end achieved through the help of a patron. |
| 身世随鬼入墓者选不成，成亦不如意也。 | 身爻、世爻随官鬼入墓的，选不成，即使成了也不如意。 | If the Body and Self follow Pressure into the tomb, the selection fails, and if it succeeds it is not as wished. |
| 若值丁未戊戌二杀墓者，虽选官到任，而旅亲回乡也。 | 如果遇到丁未、戊戌两个杀的墓，虽然选了官、到了任，也要带着亲人回乡。 | If one meets the tombs of the two baleful spirits Dingwei and Wuxu, then though he is selected and reaches his post, he travels home with his kin. |
| 鬼带亡劫大杀克世身者，虽选官而不得赴任也。 | 官鬼带亡神、劫杀等大杀去克世身的，虽然选了官，也不能赴任。 | If Pressure carries the Lost Spirit, Robbery and great baleful spirits and controls the Self and Body, then though he is selected he cannot go to his post. |
| 卦有官无父，或父值空死墓绝胎者，无在所也。 | 卦里有官星而没有父母，或者父母遇空、死、墓、绝、胎的，没有任所。 | If the hexagram has the star of office but no Resource, or Resource meets Void, death, tomb, extinction or the womb stage, there is no post to go to. |

**书中卦例** — 《易隐》第 2668 行 · *Case from the book; Yi Yin, line 2668*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 己丑年壬申月甲子旬乙丑日，占求官，得震之豫。 | 己丑年壬申月，甲子旬乙丑日，占求官，得震卦变豫卦。 | In the Jichou year, Renshen month, Jiazi decade, on a Yichou day, a divination on seeking office gave Zhen changing into Yu. |
| 此卦己事不成，代占则成也。 | 这个卦，自己的事不成，别人代占就成。 | In this hexagram one's own business fails, but when divined on one's behalf by another it succeeds. |
| 但费心力多用财耳，盖子水动化未土，文书受伤，又应财亦克文书，所云克我者，他来就我易，我克者，我去就他难也。 | 但是要多费心力、多花钱财，因为子水发动化出未土，文书受伤，又有应爻的财也克文书；所谓「克我的，他来就我容易；我克的，我去就他难」，就是这个道理。 | But it will take much effort and money, because Zi Water moves and turns into Wei Earth and the document is injured, and the Wealth of the Other line also controls the document; as the saying goes, 'what controls me, he comes to me easily; what I control, I go to him with difficulty'. |
| 所喜日辰丑土，冲未合子，子水又长生于月建申中，故只损心神，行财贿，终必成也。 | 幸好日辰丑土，冲未土、合子水，子水又在月建申中长生，所以只是耗费心神、行贿花钱，最终一定成。 | Fortunately the Day, Chou Earth, clashes with Wei Earth and combines with Zi Water, and Zi Water is also born in the Month Shen, so it only costs mental effort and money in bribes, and in the end it will surely succeed. |
| 若夫世爻旬空，丑日又刑之，己事必不可成也。 | 至于自己的事，世爻落旬空，丑日又刑它，必定不能成。 | As for one's own business, the Self falls in Void and the Chou day also punishes it, so it surely cannot succeed. |

**书中卦例** — 《易隐》第 2670–2672 行 · *Case from the book; Yi Yin, lines 2670–2672*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 己丑年丙子月甲子旬丁卯日，占求官，得睽之归妹。 | 己丑年丙子月，甲子旬丁卯日，占求官，得睽卦变归妹卦。 | In the Jichou year, Bingzi month, Jiazi decade, on a Dingmao day, a divination on seeking office gave Kui changing into Guimei. |
| 本官寅宫，动合世下戌兄，所托贵人得力，父母属火，文书难立时空，得日辰生扶，必可成也，但本宫午火自刑，求仕文书，字眼必有踈驳处。 | 本宫寅木官星发动，合住世爻下面的戌土兄弟，所托的贵人得力；父母爻属火，文书难以立住，时值落空，得到日辰生扶，必定能成；但是本宫的午火自刑，求官的文书，字眼上必有疏漏被驳的地方。 | The Yin Wood star of office of the home palace moves and combines with the Xu Earth Peer line under the Self, so the patron entrusted has real strength; the Resource line is Fire and the document is hard to establish because it is Void at the time, but with generation and support from the Day it can surely succeed; but the home palace's Wu Fire punishes itself, so the document seeking office will surely have rough spots in its wording that get queried. |
| 得旁爻卯官相生，又得官府主张成就，本宫寅官，又伏傍爻巳火文书下，必另有贵人保举，或别经勘验，方得完备，乃经两三衙门之事也。 | 得到旁边的卯木官星相生，又得到官府主张成全；本宫的寅木官星，又伏在旁边巳火文书下面，必定另有贵人保举，或者另外经过勘验，才能完备，这是要经过两三个衙门的事。 | With the side Mao Wood star of office generating it and the authorities also pushing it through, and the home palace's Yin Wood star of office lying hidden under the side Si Fire document, there will surely be another patron to recommend him, or another inspection, before it is complete; it is a matter that goes through two or three offices. |
| 毕竟火空无气，须待立春后官现，方准。 | 毕竟火落空无气，必须等立春以后官星出现，才批准。 | In the end Fire is Void and without breath, and one must wait until after the start of spring when the star of office appears before it is approved. |
| 立夏后文书旺，方完备。 | 立夏以后文书旺相，才算完备。 | After the start of summer the document thrives and then it is complete. |
| 官动六爻，地方远，外离化震，必选东方也。 | 官星在六爻发动，地方远；外卦离化震，必选在东方。 | The star of office moves in the sixth line, so the place is far; the outer trigram Li turns into Zhen, so it will surely be selected in the east. |

**书中卦例** — 《易隐》第 2674 行 · *Case from the book; Yi Yin, line 2674*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 庚寅年辛巳月甲申旬壬辰日，占求官，得需卦安静。 | 庚寅年辛巳月，甲申旬壬辰日，占求官，得需卦，没有动爻。 | In the Gengyin year, Xinsi month, Jiashen decade, on a Renchen day, a divination on seeking office gave Xu with no moving line. |
| 本宫巳火文书，临月建，伏在官鬼之下，又巳火生世丁丑土，求官必得。 | 本宫巳火文书，临在月建上，伏在官鬼下面，又巳火生世爻丁丑土，求官必定得到。 | The Si Fire document of the home palace sits on the Month and lies hidden under Pressure, and Si Fire also generates the Self, Dingchou Earth, so the office will surely be had. |
| 所嫌旁爻申金持世，与月建六合来克官，日辰又生之，主暂时阻节，终是夏金逢时空，不能为祸。 | 所嫌的是旁边的申金持世，与月建六合、来克官星，日辰又生它，主暂时有阻碍；但终究是夏天的金遇到时令空，不能成祸。 | What is disliked is that the side Shen Metal holds the Self and combines with the Month to control the star of office, and the Day also generates it, so there is a temporary obstruction; but in the end it is summer Metal meeting an empty season and cannot make misfortune. |
| 本宫卯木官，伏辰土兄弟下，日辰又是兄弟，为劫财之神，无旁爻寅鬼与本宫巳父相刑，乃所掌官吏，有求索之意，若与之贿，事必谐矣。 | 本宫的卯木官星，伏在辰土兄弟下面，日辰又是兄弟，是劫财之神；没有旁边的寅鬼与本宫的巳父相刑；这是主管的官吏，有索求的意思，如果给他送贿赂，事情必定顺利。 | The Mao Wood star of office of the home palace lies hidden under Chen Earth Peer, and the Day is also Peer, a spirit that robs wealth; there is no side Yin Pressure line to punish the home palace's Si Resource; this is the official in charge, who has the sense of demanding something, and if he is given a bribe, the matter will surely go smoothly. |

> **按**:几处字面可疑，译文只按字面意思译：「旅亲回乡」疑是「旅榇回乡」（扶柩回乡）；「丁未戊戌二杀墓」书里没有解释是哪两个杀；「方催选陪点」按字面译。「选」是明清的铨选，按资格抽签选授官缺。三个附断例都是《易隐》官禄占后面的附断例，这里放在最相关的一条下。
>
> **Note:** A few places are doubtful and are translated only by the letter: 'travelling home with kin' is presumably 'travelling home with the coffin' (escorting a coffin home); the book does not say which two baleful spirits the 'tombs of the two killers Dingwei and Wuxu' belong to; and 'just urging the selection and accompanying the roll-call' is rendered literally. 'Xuan' is the Ming and Qing selection by which vacant posts were allotted by lot according to qualification. The three examples are from the examples appended to the end of this chapter of the Yi Yin and are placed here under the entry they illustrate most.

相关:LY.CAREER.YY.08、LY.CAREER.YY.09、LY.CAREER.ZS.01、LY.CAREER.ZS.02

## LY.CAREER.YY.10 官禄占·领凭：以文书为用，旺则墓日可领、衰则生旺日可领、伏藏则临值之日领；卦无父或父临空死墓绝胎候久而不得；父化父文凭不实

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:领凭证：以文书（父母）为用神。文书旺相，到它入墓的日子可以领；衰弱，到它生旺的日子可以领；伏藏，到它当值的日子领。卦里没有父母，或者父母临空、死、墓、绝、胎，等很久也领不到；父母变出父母，文凭不实在。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Collecting the credentials: the document line is the Subject. If it is thriving, the credentials can be collected on the day it enters the tomb; if weak, on a day of its birth or strength; if hidden, on the day in which it rules. If the hexagram has no Resource line, or Resource sits on Void, death, tomb, extinction or the womb stage, one waits long and does not get them; if Resource turns into Resource, the credentials are not genuine.

**《易隐·官禄占》原文** — 《易隐》第 2621 行 · *Yi Yin (What Lies Hidden in the Changes), "Divining office and salary", base text; Yi Yin, line 2621*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 以文书为用。 | 以文书（父母）为用神。 | The document line is the Subject. |
| 旺则墓日可领，衰则生旺日可领，伏藏则临值之日领也。 | 旺相的，到它入墓的日子可以领；衰弱的，到它生旺的日子可以领；伏藏的，到它当值的日子领。 | If it is thriving, the credentials can be collected on the day it enters the tomb; if weak, on a day of its birth or strength; if hidden, on the day in which it rules. |
| 卦无父，与父临空死墓绝胎者，候久而不得也。 | 卦里没有父母，或者父母临在空、死、墓、绝、胎上的，等很久也得不到。 | If the hexagram has no Resource line, or Resource sits on Void, death, tomb, extinction or the womb stage, one waits long and does not get them. |
| 又父化父者，文凭不实也。 | 又，父母变出父母的，文凭不实在。 | Again, if Resource turns into Resource, the credentials are not genuine. |

相关:LY.CAREER.YY.09、LY.CAREER.YY.11、LY.CAREER.HJC.05

## LY.CAREER.YY.29 官禄占·官职退复（二）：官遇三传生扶必起复；世下伏官伏印马必官印临值之年月日起官；官父持身世旺静必冲动官印之年月日起官；旁爻带贵马旺动生官印得贵人提拔；旁爻兄弟带亡劫隔神刑冲官印必有匪人相隔

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:起复，重新起用：官星遇到三传（太岁、月建、日辰）生扶的，必定起复；世爻下伏着官星，或伏着印星、驿马的，必定在官星、印星当值的年月日起复；官星、印星持身爻、世爻，旺相安静的，必定在冲动官星、印星的年月日起复；旁边的爻带贵人、驿马，旺相发动，来生身爻、世爻上的官星、印星的，必得贵人提拔，在相生的年月日起复；旁边的兄弟爻旺相发动，带亡神、劫杀、隔神来刑、冲、克、害身爻、世爻上的官星、印星的，必有小人从中阻隔、坏事。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Being recalled to office. If the star of office meets generation and support from the three rulers, he is surely recalled; if the star of office lies hidden under the Self, or the seal and the Horse lie hidden, he is recalled in the year, month or day when the star of office and seal rule; if the star of office and seal hold the Body and Self, thriving and still, he is recalled in the year, month or day that clashes them into motion; if a side line carrying the Noble and the Horse is thriving and moving and generates the star of office and seal on the Body and Self, he gets a patron's promotion and is recalled in the year, month or day of generation; if a side Peer line is thriving and moving, carrying the Lost Spirit, Robbery and the obstructing spirit, and punishes, clashes, controls or harms the star of office and seal on the Body and Self, there will surely be bad men who stand between and spoil the matter.

**《易隐·官禄占》原文** — 《易隐》第 2659 行 · *Yi Yin (What Lies Hidden in the Changes), "Divining office and salary", base text; Yi Yin, line 2659*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 又官遇三传生扶者，必起复也。 | 又，官星遇到三传（太岁、月建、日辰）生扶的，必定起复。 | Again, if the star of office meets generation and support from the three rulers, he is surely recalled to office. |
| 世下伏官，与伏印马者，必官印临值之年月日起官也。 | 世爻下伏着官星，以及伏着印星、驿马的，必定在官星、印星当值的年月日起复做官。 | If the star of office lies hidden under the Self, or the seal and the Horse lie hidden, he is surely recalled to office in the year, month or day when the star of office and seal rule. |
| 官父持身世旺静者，必冲动官印之年月日起官也。 | 官星、印星持身爻、世爻，旺相安静的，必定在冲动官星、印星的年月日起复做官。 | If the star of office and the seal hold the Body and Self and are thriving and still, he is surely recalled in the year, month or day that clashes the star of office and seal into motion. |
| 旁爻带贵马旺动来生身世上官印者，必得贵人提拔。 | 旁边的爻带着贵人、驿马，旺相发动，来生身爻、世爻上的官星、印星的，必定得到贵人提拔。 | If a side line carrying the Noble and the Horse is thriving and moving and generates the star of office and seal on the Body and Self, he surely gets a patron's promotion. |
| 以相生之年月日起官也。 | 以相生的年月日起复做官。 | He is recalled to office in the year, month or day of generation. |
| 若旁爻兄弟旺动，带亡劫隔神来刑冲克害身世上官印者，必有匪人相隔坏事也。 | 如果旁边的兄弟爻旺相发动，带着亡神、劫杀、隔神，来刑、冲、克、害身爻、世爻上的官星、印星，必有小人从中阻隔、坏事。 | But if a side Peer line is thriving and moving, carrying the Lost Spirit, Robbery and the obstructing spirit, and comes to punish, clash, control or harm the star of office and seal on the Body and Self, there will surely be bad men who stand between and spoil the matter. |

**书中卦例** — 《易隐》第 2677 行 · *Case from the book; Yi Yin, line 2677*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 是月是日有占起官者，得剥之晋。 | 这个月这一天有人占起复做官，得剥卦变晋卦。 | In that month and on that day someone divined on being recalled to office and got Bo changing into Jin. |
| 世临子水，伏申金，金生水为印绶。 | 世爻临子水，伏着申金，金生水，是印绶。 | The Self sits on Zi Water, with Shen Metal hidden, and Metal generates Water, so it is the seal and ribbon. |
| 又寅年午月戌日，马聚于申，世下伏印马，后必起官。 | 又是寅年、午月、戌日，驿马聚在申，世爻下面伏着印星和驿马，后来必定起复做官。 | It is also a Yin year, Wu month and Xu day, and the Horse gathers at Shen, and the seal and the Horse lie hidden under the Self, so he will surely be recalled to office later. |
| 但嫌本宫午官伏戌墓下，须待壬辰年冲开戌墓，透出午官，又辰年与世爻飞伏申子三合，故其年上寅月，可用财求谋，官生在寅也。 | 但是嫌本宫的午火官星伏在戌墓下面，必须等到壬辰年冲开戌墓，透出午火官星；而且辰年与世爻的飞神子水、伏神申金三合，所以那一年到寅月，可以用钱财去谋求，官星生在寅。 | But what is disliked is that the Wu Fire star of office of the home palace lies hidden under the Xu tomb; he must wait for the Renchen year, when the Xu tomb is clashed open and the Wu Fire star of office comes out; moreover the Chen year forms a three-harmony with the flying Zi Water and the hidden Shen Metal of the Self line, so in that year by the Yin month he can use money to seek it, since the star of office is born in Yin. |
| 丙午月得官，官旺于午也。 | 丙午月得到官职，官星在午旺。 | He gets the office in the Bingwu month, since the star of office is thriving in Wu. |
| 近则未月到任，远则戌月到任也。 | 近的话是未月到任，远的话是戌月到任。 | Nearer, he takes up his post in the Wei month; further off, in the Xu month. |
| 土为印绶，未与午官六合，戌与午官三合也。 | 土是印绶，未与午官星六合，戌与午官星三合。 | Earth is the seal and ribbon, and Wei forms a six-combination with the Wu star of office, and Xu forms a three-harmony with it. |

> **按**:附断例是《易隐》官禄占末尾的一例，放在最相关的这一条下；书里没有说占的是哪一年的什么日子，只说「是月是日」，接的是前一例庚寅年壬午月甲戌日。「隔神」是神煞的一种，书里没有解释。第四、五句里「相生之年月日」承上一句，是旁边的贵人、驿马生到官星、印星的年月日。
>
> **Note:** The example is one of those appended at the end of this chapter of the Yi Yin, and is placed under the entry it illustrates most; the book does not give the year or day of the divination, saying only 'that month, that day', following on the previous example of the Gengyin year, Renwu month, Jiaxu day. 'Geshen' is a kind of spirit-star that the book does not explain. In the fourth and fifth sentences 'the year, month or day of generation' follows on from the previous sentence, and is the year, month or day in which the side Noble and Post Horse generate the star of office and seal.

相关:LY.CAREER.YY.28、LY.CAREER.YY.30、LY.CAREER.HJC.06、LY.CAREER.ZS.47

## LY.CAREER.YY.71 文书占·公私之辨与成否（游南子）：虽俱以父母为用，公占以官旺为助，私占以子旺为助；三传刑害克破父母不成；卦无官父或官父临空绝化空绝非不发则遗失；父值墓胎、化墓胎、加勾土旺动必阻滞；在官占文凭诰敕公文差札身世坐禄马贵官印或三传值官印动生合身世必得；商占盐引钞文私居占契券田贴父旺动生合世身而财子旺静者有；应空绝墓胎、世持财加武动者无

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:文书的占问，有公事和私事的分别：虽然都以父母爻为用神，但公事占以官星旺相为助，私事占以子孙旺相为助。三传（太岁、月建、日辰）刑、害、克、破父母爻的，不成；卦里没有官星、父爻，或官星、父爻临空、绝，以及化成空、绝的，不是发不下来，就是遗失；父爻值墓、胎，以及化成墓、胎，或者加勾陈、土爻而旺相发动的，必定被阻滞耽搁拖延月日。在官的人占文凭、诰敕、公文、差委的札付，只要身爻、世爻坐着禄、驿马、贵人、官星、印绶，或者三传值官星、印星发动来生合身爻、世爻的，必定可以得到。为经商占盐引、钞文，以及居家占契券、田地凭帖，只要父母爻旺相发动生合世爻、身爻，而财爻、子孙旺相安静的，有；应爻空、绝、墓、胎，以及世爻持财爻加玄武发动的，没有（财爻克文书，玄武克朱雀）。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Documents differ between public and private matters: though Resource is the Subject in both, in a public matter the star of office being thriving helps, and in a private matter Output being thriving helps. If the three rulers punish, harm, control or break Resource, it does not come about; if the hexagram has no star of office or seal line, or they sit on Void or extinction or turn into Void or extinction, it is either not issued or lost; if the seal line meets tomb or womb, or turns into tomb or womb, or carries Hook Earth and an Earth line and is thriving and moving, it will surely be blocked and delayed for months and days. For one in office divining credentials, imperial edicts, official letters and orders of appointment, if the Body and Self sit on Emolument, the Horse, the Noble, the star of office and the seal and ribbon, or the three rulers on the star of office and seal move to generate or combine with the Body and Self, they can surely be had. For a merchant divining salt vouchers and notes of credit, or a householder divining contracts, deeds and land certificates, if the Resource line is thriving and moving and generates or combines with the Self and Body while Wealth and Output are thriving and still, there will be one; if the Other is Void, extinct, in tomb or in womb, or the Self holds Wealth with the Dark Tortoise added and moving, there will be none (Wealth controls the document and the Dark Tortoise controls the Vermilion Bird).

**游南子(《易隐》引)** — 《易隐》第 2681 行 · *Master Younan (quoted in the Yi Yin); Yi Yin, line 2681*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 游南子曰，文书之占，有公私之辨焉。 | 游南子说：占文书，有公事和私事的分别。 | Master Younan says: in divining documents there is a distinction between public and private. |
| 虽俱以父母为用，然公占则以官旺为助，私占则以子旺为助也。 | 虽然都以父母爻为用神，但公事占以官星旺相为助，私事占以子孙旺相为助。 | Though in both Resource is taken as the Subject, for a public matter the star of office being thriving helps, and for a private matter Output being thriving helps. |
| 三传刑害克破父母者，不成。 | 三传（太岁、月建、日辰）刑、害、克、破父母爻的，不成。 | If the three rulers punish, harm, control or break Resource, it does not come about. |
| 卦无官父或官父临空绝，与化空绝者，非不发，则遗失。 | 卦里没有官星、父爻，或者官星、父爻临空、绝，以及化成空、绝的，不是发不下来，就是遗失。 | If the hexagram has no star of office or seal line, or they sit on Void or extinction, or turn into Void or extinction, it is either not issued or lost. |
| 父值墓胎，与化墓胎，或加勾土旺动者，必阻滞耽隔逗留月日也。 | 父爻值墓、胎，以及化成墓、胎，或者加勾陈、土爻而旺相发动的，必定被阻滞、耽搁、拖延月日。 | If the seal line meets tomb or womb, or turns into tomb or womb, or carries Hook Earth and an Earth line and is thriving and moving, it will surely be blocked, delayed and held up for months and days. |
| 若乃在官而占，文凭诰勅，与夫公文差劄者，但身世坐禄马贵官印绶，或三传值官印动来生合身世者，必可得也。 | 如果是在官的人占文凭、诰敕，以及公文、差委的札付，只要身爻、世爻坐着禄、驿马、贵人、官星、印绶，或者三传值官星、印星发动来生合身爻、世爻的，必定可以得到。 | For one in office divining credentials, imperial edicts, official letters and orders of appointment, if the Body and Self sit on Emolument, the Horse, the Noble, the star of office and the seal and ribbon, or the three rulers on the star of office and seal move to generate or combine with the Body and Self, they can surely be had. |
| 为商而占盐引钞文，与私居而占契券田贴者，但父母旺动生合世身，而财子旺静者，有也。 | 为经商占盐引、钞文，和居家占契约、田地凭帖的，只要父母爻旺相发动生合世爻、身爻，而财爻、子孙旺相安静的，有。 | For a merchant divining salt vouchers and notes of credit, or a householder divining contracts, deeds and land certificates, if the Resource line is thriving and moving and generates or combines with the Self and Body while Wealth and Output are thriving and still, there will be one. |
| 应爻空绝墓胎，与世持财加武动者，无也。 | 应爻空、绝、墓、胎，以及世爻持财爻加玄武发动的，没有。 | If the Other is Void, extinct, in tomb or in womb, or the Self holds Wealth with the Dark Tortoise added and moving, there will be none. |
| (财克文书，武克朱雀)。 | （财爻克文书，玄武克朱雀。） | (Wealth controls the document, and the Dark Tortoise controls the Vermilion Bird.) |

**书中卦例** — 《易隐》第 2685–2687 行 · *Case from the book; Yi Yin, lines 2685–2687*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 庚寅年戊寅月乙卯日，代占求文书，得剥之观。 | 庚寅年戊寅月乙卯日，替人占求文书，得剥卦变观卦。 | In the Gengyin year, Wuyin month, on a Yimao day, someone divined for another on seeking a document and got Bo changing into Guan. |
| 傍爻巳官临应，受岁月日生，人多云吉，终是被岁月刑巳，又世上旁爻子动伤官，本宫辰戌文书，皆值时空，三传克之，且世应相克，人事不和，决主徒劳无成也。 | 旁边的巳火官星临在应爻上，受太岁、月建、日辰相生，人们多说吉，但终究是巳被太岁、月建刑；又世爻上旁边的子水发动，伤官星；本宫的辰土、戌土文书，都值当时的空，被三传克它；而且世爻、应爻相克，人事不和，断定白费力气、没有结果。 | The side Si Fire star of office sits on the Other and is generated by the Year, Month and Day, and people mostly called it auspicious, but in the end Si is punished by the Year and Month; also the side Zi Water line on the Self moves and injures the star of office; the Chen and Xu Earth documents of the home palace both fall in the Void of the time and are controlled by the three rulers; and the Self and Other control each other and the human side is not in harmony, so it was judged to be wasted effort without result. |

**书中卦例** — 《易隐》第 2688 行 · *Case from the book; Yi Yin, line 2688*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 庚寅年庚辰月己巳日，占托人求文书，得丰之复。 | 庚寅年庚辰月己巳日，占托人求文书，得丰卦变复卦。 | In the Gengyin year, Gengchen month, on a Jisi day, a divination on entrusting someone to seek a document got Feng changing into Fu. |
| 主文书却在，其人必死也。 | 主文书倒还在，但受托的那个人必死。 | It meant that the document would still be there, but the man entrusted would surely die. |
| 缘申金文书，与月辰巳火六合，而动爻亥水兄弟，化入辰墓，又犯月破，况病于岁建寅，申墓于月建辰，申绝于日建巳中，故知其必死也。 | 因为申金文书，和月建辰、巳火六合，而发动的亥水兄弟，变入辰墓，又犯月破，何况病在太岁寅，申（水）墓在月建辰，申（水）绝在日建巳中，所以知道他必死。 | Because the Shen Metal document forms a six-combination with the Month Chen and Si Fire, while the moving Hai Water Peer line turns into its tomb at Chen and also suffers Month-break, and moreover it is ill at the Year Yin, Shen (Water) is in tomb at the Month Chen and extinct in the Day Si, so it was known that he would surely die. |

> **按**:两个例子都是《易隐》文书占末尾附的断例，放在最相关的这一条下。第二例里有几处字面对不上通行的说法，译文都照字面：「月辰巳火六合」按字面是月建辰和巳火，而申的六合只有巳，辰与申是申子辰的半三合；「又犯月破」按通行的月破（月建所冲的支）庚辰月破的是戌，不是亥，这里大概是说亥水被月建辰土克破；「病于岁建寅，申墓于月建辰，申绝于日建巳」说的是亥水兄弟（受托的人）：水病在寅、墓在辰、绝在巳，「申」是借申来指水长生之位。「三传」是太岁、月建、日辰（见术语表）。
>
> **Note:** Both examples are appended at the end of this chapter on documents in the Yi Yin, and are placed under the entry they illustrate most. In the second example several wordings do not match the usual way of speaking, and the translation follows the letter: 'six-combination with the Month Chen and Si Fire' is by the letter the Month Chen and Si Fire, whereas Shen's six-combination is only Si, and Chen and Shen are a half three-harmony of Shen, Zi and Chen; 'also suffers Month-break' by the usual Month-break (the branch the Month clashes) would be Xu in a Gengchen month and not Hai, and here it presumably means that Hai Water is controlled and broken by the Month Chen Earth; 'ill at the Year Yin, Shen in tomb at the Month Chen, Shen extinct in the Day Si' speaks of the Hai Water Peer line (the man entrusted): Water is ill at Yin, in tomb at Chen and extinct at Si, and 'Shen' is used for the position where Water is born. The 'three rulers' are Tai Sui, the Month and the Day (see the glossary).

相关:LY.CAREER.YY.72、LY.CAREER.YY.10、LY.CAREER.HJC.05

---

## LY.CAREER.YY.83 谋望占·可否成败（一）：以世应为主，内卦为谋事之人；内克外、世克应、外应生内世、内外世应俱旺相比和，事可图易就；月日合世应心虽不欲亦允成；间动鬼动生合世应得人劝成；谋官官旺生合世身而子衰静成、谋财财旺而兄衰静成、谋文书父旺而财衰静成；凡事嫌兄动，并贵禄生世则为吹嘘之人；凡事喜福动，求名忌之

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:谋事能不能成，以世爻、应爻为主，内卦是谋事的人。内卦克外卦、世爻克应爻，外卦、应爻生内卦、世爻，或者内外卦、世应都旺相比和的，事情可以谋，谋也容易成；月建、日辰合世、应的，对方心里虽然不愿意，也会答应成全；间爻、官鬼发动生合世、应的，有人劝说成全。谋官，逢官星旺相生合世身、子孙衰弱安静的，成；谋财，逢财爻旺相生合世身、兄弟衰弱安静的，成；谋文书，逢父爻旺相生合世身、财爻衰弱安静的，成。凡事都嫌兄弟发动，但如果兄弟带着贵人、禄一起生世爻，就是替你吹嘘推荐的人；凡事都喜欢福神发动，求名（功名）却忌讳它。这是可谋而成的占。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Whether a scheme can succeed takes the Self and Other as the chief, and the inner trigram is the man who schemes. If the inner controls the outer, the Self controls the Other, the outer and the Other generate the inner and Self, or the inner and outer and the Self and Other are all thriving and in harmony, the matter can be planned and is easily done; if the Month and Day combine with the Self and Other, though the other side is unwilling at heart he will agree and see it through; if a line between or Pressure moves to generate or combine with the Self and Other, someone persuades him to see it through. Scheming for office, if the star of office is thriving and generates or combines with the Self and Body while Output is weak and still, it succeeds; scheming for wealth, if Wealth is thriving and generates or combines with the Self and Body while Peer is weak and still, it succeeds; scheming for a document, if Resource is thriving and generates or combines with the Self and Body while Wealth is weak and still, it succeeds. In everything a moving Peer line is disliked, but if it carries the Noble and Emolument and generates the Self, it is one who talks you up and recommends you; in everything the fortune spirit moving is liked, but in seeking fame it is avoided. This is the pattern of a scheme that can be planned and will succeed.

**《易隐·谋望占》原文** — 《易隐》第 2840–2842 行 · *Yi Yin (What Lies Hidden in the Changes), "Divining a scheme", base text; Yi Yin, lines 2840–2842*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 以世应为主。 | 以世爻、应爻为主。 | Take the Self and Other as the chief. |
| 内卦为谋事之人。 | 内卦是谋事的人。 | The inner trigram is the man who schemes. |
| 内克外，世克应，外应生内世者，内外世应俱旺相比和者，事可图，图亦易就。 | 内卦克外卦，世爻克应爻，外卦、应爻生内卦、世爻的，内外卦、世应都旺相比和的，事情可以谋，谋也容易成。 | If the inner controls the outer, the Self controls the Other, the outer and the Other generate the inner and Self, or the inner and outer and the Self and Other are all thriving and in harmony, the matter can be planned and is easily done. |
| 月日合世应，心虽不欲，亦得允成。 | 月建、日辰合世、应的，对方心里虽然不愿意，也会答应成全。 | If the Month and Day combine with the Self and Other, though the other side is unwilling at heart he will agree and see it through. |
| 间动鬼动生合世应，得人劝成。 | 间爻、官鬼发动生合世、应的，有人劝说成全。 | If a line between or Pressure moves to generate or combine with the Self and Other, someone persuades him to see it through. |
| 谋官逢官旺生合世身，而子衰静者成。 | 谋官，逢官星旺相生合世身、子孙衰弱安静的，成。 | Scheming for office, if the star of office is thriving and generates or combines with the Self and Body while Output is weak and still, it succeeds. |
| 谋财逢财旺生合世身，而兄衰静者成。 | 谋财，逢财爻旺相生合世身、兄弟衰弱安静的，成。 | Scheming for wealth, if Wealth is thriving and generates or combines with the Self and Body while Peer is weak and still, it succeeds. |
| 谋文书，逢父旺生合世身，而财衰静者成。 | 谋文书，逢父爻旺相生合世身、财爻衰弱安静的，成。 | Scheming for a document, if Resource is thriving and generates or combines with the Self and Body while Wealth is weak and still, it succeeds. |
| 凡事皆嫌兄动，若并贵禄生世，又为吹嘘之人。 | 凡事都嫌兄弟发动；但如果兄弟带着贵人、禄一起生世爻，就是替你吹嘘推荐的人。 | In everything a moving Peer line is disliked; but if it carries the Noble and Emolument and generates the Self, it is one who talks you up and recommends you. |
| 凡事皆喜福动，求名则忌之，此可谋而成之占也。 | 凡事都喜欢福神发动，求名（功名）却忌讳它；这是可谋而成的占。 | In everything the fortune spirit moving is liked, but in seeking fame it is avoided; this is the pattern of a scheme that can be planned and will succeed. |

> **按**:「世身」是世爻和身爻，「内外世应」按字面是内卦、外卦、世爻、应爻。「吹嘘」是替人说好话、推荐。「福动」是子孙（福神）发动；求名忌子孙是因为子孙克官（见 HJC.03）。
>
> **Note:** 'Shishen' is the Self and the Body line; 'inner, outer, Self, Other' is by the letter the inner trigram, the outer trigram, the Self and the Other. 'Chuixu' is speaking well of someone, recommending him. 'The fortune spirit moves' is Output moving; Output is avoided in seeking fame because Output controls the star of office (see HJC.03).

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《易林补遗》:同文 第 885 行:内卦为谋事之人

相关:LY.CAREER.YY.82、LY.CAREER.YY.84、LY.CAREER.HJC.03、LY.CAREER.HJC.04

---

## LY.CAREER.YY.84 谋望占·可否成败（二）：内外世应身爻逢空死墓绝胎被刑害克破、卦六冲、世应俱动、外克内应克世、鬼兄临间爻带隔神退神退悔杀动，俱不可谋；合中犯刑害克破恩中变怨成而后毁；用神不上卦或空绝（求官无官爻、求财求文书无财印爻）谋不成；卦中无官及官空绝诸事不成；卦中无鬼伏谋事父动费力成

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:不可谋、难成的占：内外卦、世爻、应爻、身爻，逢空、死、墓、绝、胎，被刑、害、克、破的，或者卦是六冲，或者世、应都发动，或者外卦克内卦、应爻克世爻，或者官鬼、兄弟临在间爻、带隔神、退神、退悔杀发动的，都不可谋，谋也不成。又，合中犯了刑、害、克、破的，事情多半恩中生怨，成了之后又毁，虽然是自己的事，也被旁人阻隔。又，用神不上卦的（比如求官而卦里没有官爻、官爻空绝；求财、求文书而没有财爻、印爻，或财爻、印爻空绝），都谋不成。又凡是卦里没有官星，以及官星逢空、绝的，诸事不成。所以经书说：卦中没有官鬼、伏着的谋事，父爻发动，终究要费力才成。这是不可谋而难成的占。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The pattern of a scheme that cannot be planned and is hard to complete. If the inner and outer trigrams, the Self, Other and Body meet Void, death, tomb, extinction or womb and are punished, harmed, controlled or broken, or the hexagram is a six-clash, or the Self and Other both move, or the outer controls the inner and the Other controls the Self, or Pressure or Peer sits on a line between carrying the obstructing spirit, the Retreating spirit or the regret baleful spirit and moves, none can be planned, and if planned will not succeed. Again, if within a combination there is punishment, harm, control or breaking, the matter often turns from favour to resentment, and is spoiled after succeeding, and though it is one's own matter it is blocked by others. Again, if the Subject does not appear on the board (as when seeking office there is no Pressure line, or Pressure is Void or extinct; seeking wealth or a document, there is no Wealth or seal line, or they are Void or extinct), the scheme will not succeed. Again, when the hexagram has no star of office, or the star of office is Void or extinct, nothing succeeds. So the classic says: if there is no Pressure in the hexagram and it lies hidden, in a scheme, with the Resource line moving, it is in the end completed only with effort. This is the pattern of a scheme that cannot be planned and is hard to complete.

**《易隐·谋望占》原文** — 《易隐》第 2842 行 · *Yi Yin (What Lies Hidden in the Changes), "Divining a scheme", base text; Yi Yin, line 2842*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 若内外世应身爻，逢空死墓绝胎，被刑害克破者，或卦六冲者，或世应俱动者，或外克内，应克世者，或鬼兄临间爻带隔神退神退悔杀动者，俱不可谋，谋亦不成。 | 如果内外卦、世爻、应爻、身爻，逢空、死、墓、绝、胎，被刑、害、克、破的，或者卦是六冲，或者世、应都发动，或者外卦克内卦、应爻克世爻的，或者官鬼、兄弟临在间爻、带隔神、退神、退悔杀发动的，都不可谋，谋也不成。 | If the inner and outer trigrams, the Self, Other and Body meet Void, death, tomb, extinction or womb and are punished, harmed, controlled or broken, or the hexagram is a six-clash, or the Self and Other both move, or the outer controls the inner and the Other controls the Self, or Pressure or Peer sits on a line between carrying the obstructing spirit, the Retreating spirit or the regret baleful spirit and moves, none can be planned, and if planned will not succeed. |
| 又合中犯刑害克破者，事多恩中变怨，成而后毁，虽是已事，亦被旁人阻隔。 | 又，合中犯了刑、害、克、破的，事情多半恩中生怨，成了之后又毁，虽然是自己的事，也被旁人阻隔。 | Again, if within a combination there is punishment, harm, control or breaking, the matter often turns from favour to resentment, and is spoiled after succeeding, and though it is one's own matter it is blocked by others. |
| 又用神不上卦者，如求官无官爻，或官爻空绝。 | 又，用神不上卦的，比如求官而卦里没有官爻，或官爻空、绝； | Again, if the Subject does not appear on the board, as when seeking office there is no Pressure line, or the Pressure line is Void or extinct; |
| 求财，求文书，无财印爻，或财印爻空绝，俱谋不成也。 | 求财、求文书，没有财爻、印爻，或财爻、印爻空、绝，都谋不成。 | seeking wealth or a document, there is no Wealth or seal line, or they are Void or extinct, the scheme will not succeed. |
| 又凡卦中无官，与官逢空绝者，诸事不成。 | 又凡是卦里没有官星，以及官星逢空、绝的，诸事不成。 | Again, when the hexagram has no star of office, or the star of office meets Void or extinction, nothing succeeds. |
| 故经曰，卦中无鬼伏谋事，父动终当费力成也。 | 所以经书说：卦中没有官鬼、伏着的谋事，父爻发动，终究要费力才成。 | So the classic says: if there is no Pressure in the hexagram and it lies hidden, in a scheme, with the Resource line moving, it is in the end completed only with effort. |
| 此不可谋而难成之占也。 | 这是不可谋而难成的占。 | This is the pattern of a scheme that cannot be planned and is hard to complete. |

> **按**:「隔神」「退悔杀」是神煞，书里没有解释，译文照字面。「经曰」引的是另一部经书，书里没有说是哪一部，所以这句译文照字面，不指认出处；「卦中无鬼伏谋事」这句字面不太通，译文按「卦中没有官鬼，而官鬼伏藏时谋事」译。「用神」是占问时被拿来看这件事成败的那个爻。
>
> **Note:** 'Geshen' (the obstructing spirit) and 'tuihui sha' (the regret baleful spirit) are spirit-stars that the book does not explain, and the translation follows the letter. 'The classic says' quotes another classic, and the book does not say which, so the translation follows the letter and does not identify the source; the line 'gua zhong wu gui fu mou shi' does not run smoothly by the letter, and the translation reads it as 'when there is no Pressure in the hexagram and it lies hidden, in a scheme'. The 'Subject' is the line taken as the matter asked about.

相关:LY.CAREER.YY.83、LY.CAREER.YY.85、LY.CAREER.HJC.06、LY.CAREER.ZS.02

---

## LY.CAREER.YY.85 谋望占·可否成败（三）：内外纯阳用爻旺相出现宜公不宜私，内外纯阴用爻休囚伏藏宜私不宜公；内生外世生应我去求人，外生内应生世人来就我；主卦冲变卦合、动克世变生世先难后易，主卦合变卦冲、动合世变刑世先易后难

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:谋事的几种格局：内外卦纯阳、用爻旺相出现的，适宜公事，不适宜私事；内外卦纯阴、用爻休囚伏藏的，适宜私事，不适宜公事。内卦生外卦、世爻生应爻，是我去求人；外卦生内卦、应爻生世爻，是人来就我。本卦冲而变卦合、发动克世爻而变出来生世爻的，先难后易；本卦合而变卦冲、发动合世爻而变出来刑世爻的，先易后难。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Some patterns in a scheme. If the inner and outer trigrams are pure yang and the Subject line is thriving and appears, it is fit for a public matter and not a private one; if the inner and outer are pure yin and the Subject line is resting or trapped and hidden, it is fit for a private matter and not a public one. If the inner generates the outer and the Self generates the Other, I go to ask others; if the outer generates the inner and the Other generates the Self, others come to me. If the original hexagram clashes and the changed hexagram combines, or a moving line controls the Self and changes into one that generates the Self, it is hard at first and easy after; if the original hexagram combines and the changed hexagram clashes, or a moving line combines with the Self and changes into one that punishes the Self, it is easy at first and hard after.

**《易隐·谋望占》原文** — 《易隐》第 2842 行 · *Yi Yin (What Lies Hidden in the Changes), "Divining a scheme", base text; Yi Yin, line 2842*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 然有宜公而不宜私者，内外纯阳，用爻旺相出现也。 | 然而有适宜公事、不适宜私事的：内外卦纯阳，用爻旺相出现。 | Yet there is a pattern fit for a public matter and not a private one: the inner and outer trigrams are pure yang, and the Subject line is thriving and appears. |
| 有宜私而不宜公者，内外纯阴，用爻休囚伏藏也。 | 有适宜私事、不适宜公事的：内外卦纯阴，用爻休囚伏藏。 | There is a pattern fit for a private matter and not a public one: the inner and outer trigrams are pure yin, and the Subject line is resting or trapped and hidden. |
| 内生外，世生应，我去求人也。 | 内卦生外卦、世爻生应爻，是我去求人。 | If the inner generates the outer and the Self generates the Other, I go to ask others. |
| 外生内，应生世，人来就我也。 | 外卦生内卦、应爻生世爻，是人来就我。 | If the outer generates the inner and the Other generates the Self, others come to me. |
| 主卦冲而变卦合，动克世而变生世，先难后易也。 | 本卦冲而变卦合，发动克世爻而变出来生世爻的，先难后易。 | If the original hexagram clashes and the changed hexagram combines, or a moving line controls the Self and changes into one that generates the Self, it is hard at first and easy after. |
| 主卦合而变卦冲，动合世而变刑世，先易后难也。 | 本卦合而变卦冲，发动合世爻而变出来刑世爻的，先易后难。 | If the original hexagram combines and the changed hexagram clashes, or a moving line combines with the Self and changes into one that punishes the Self, it is easy at first and hard after. |

> **按**:「宜公不宜私」「宜私不宜公」是看这件事该走公家的路还是私人的路：纯阳、用爻出现，是公开、光明的事；纯阴、用爻伏藏，是私下、隐秘的事。这一句书里没有解释理由，译文只照字面译。
>
> **Note:** 'Fit for the public and not the private' and 'fit for the private and not the public' ask whether the matter should take an official road or a private one: pure yang with the Subject line appearing is an open and bright matter, and pure yin with the Subject line hidden is a private and hidden one. The book gives no reason for this sentence, and the translation follows only the letter.

相关:LY.CAREER.YY.84、LY.CAREER.YY.86

## LY.CAREER.YY.86 谋望占·可否成败（四）：成败于何人以六亲定——本宫内卦至亲、本宫外卦远亲、他宫内卦邻里、他宫外卦远方人，鬼加贵缙绅否则牙人保人媒人，八卦定其方；托人图事外人看应、家亲看用爻，应爻用爻犯日破月破旬空及空死墓绝胎无能赞襄，生旺化空死墓绝胎有始无终，冲克刑害世必怀欺，六冲终反目，虎损害蛇心变勾愚钝武诈欺雀多诳语，惟旺相带龙喜成神生合世身者赖其扶持

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:想知道事情成败在哪个人身上，就用六亲来定：本宫的内卦，是至亲；本宫的外卦，是远亲；他宫的内卦，是邻里；他宫的外卦，是远方的人；官鬼加贵人，是缙绅，否则是牙人、保人、媒人；想知道是哪个方向的人，用八卦来定。又有托人办事的，外人看应爻，亲人看用爻。凡应爻、用爻犯日破、月破、旬空的，都主受托的人没有能力帮忙，空、死、墓、绝、胎也一样；生旺而变成空、死、墓、绝、胎的，必有始无终；冲、克、刑、害世爻的，受托的人必定怀着欺骗，难以托付；六冲的卦，终究反目成仇。白虎发动的，损害；螣蛇发动的，变心；勾陈发动的，愚钝；玄武发动的，欺诈；朱雀旺相落空而发动的，多说谎话。只有旺相、带青龙、喜神、成神，发动来生合世爻、身爻的，才靠他扶持把事办成。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** To know through whom the matter succeeds or fails, fix it by the six kin: the inner trigram of the home palace is the nearest kin; the outer trigram of the home palace, distant kin; the inner trigram of another palace, neighbours; the outer trigram of another palace, men from afar; Pressure with the Noble is gentry, otherwise a broker, a guarantor or a go-between; the direction is fixed by the eight trigrams. If one entrusts someone with the matter, outsiders are seen through the Other and kin through the Subject line. Whenever the Other or the Subject line suffers Day-break, Month-break or decade Void, the man entrusted has no ability to help, and so too for Void, death, tomb, extinction and womb; if thriving and turning into Void, death, tomb, extinction or womb, there is a beginning without an end; if the Self is clashed, controlled, punished or harmed, the man entrusted surely harbours deceit and cannot be relied on; a six-clash hexagram ends in a falling out. If the Tiger moves, he does harm; the Snake, his heart changes; Hook Earth, he is dull; the Dark Tortoise, he cheats; the Bird thriving and Void and moving, he tells many lies. Only if he is thriving and carries the Dragon, Joy and the Completion spirit and moves to generate or combine with the Self and Body does one rely on his support to get the matter done.

**《易隐·谋望占》原文** — 《易隐》第 2842 行 · *Yi Yin (What Lies Hidden in the Changes), "Divining a scheme", base text; Yi Yin, line 2842*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 欲知事成败于何人，则以六亲定之。 | 想知道事情成败在哪个人身上，就用六亲来定。 | To know through whom the matter succeeds or fails, fix it by the six kin. |
| 本宫内卦，至亲也。 | 本宫的内卦，是至亲。 | The inner trigram of the home palace is the nearest kin. |
| 本宫外卦，远亲也。 | 本宫的外卦，是远亲。 | The outer trigram of the home palace is distant kin. |
| 他宫内卦，邻里也。 | 他宫的内卦，是邻里。 | The inner trigram of another palace is neighbours. |
| 他宫外卦，远方人也。 | 他宫的外卦，是远方的人。 | The outer trigram of another palace is men from afar. |
| 鬼加贵，缙绅也，否则牙人保人媒人也。 | 官鬼加贵人，是缙绅，否则是牙人、保人、媒人。 | Pressure with the Noble is gentry, otherwise a broker, a guarantor or a go-between. |
| 欲知何方人，以八卦定之。 | 想知道是哪个方向的人，用八卦来定。 | To know from which direction the man comes, fix it by the eight trigrams. |
| 又有托人图事者，外人看应，家亲看用爻。 | 又有托人办事的，外人看应爻，亲人看用爻。 | Again, if one entrusts someone with the matter, outsiders are seen through the Other and kin through the Subject line. |
| 凡应爻用爻犯日破月破旬空者，皆主无能赞勷，空死墓绝胎亦然。 | 凡应爻、用爻犯日破、月破、旬空的，都主受托的人没有能力帮忙，空、死、墓、绝、胎也一样。 | Whenever the Other or the Subject line suffers Day-break, Month-break or decade Void, the man entrusted has no ability to help, and so too for Void, death, tomb, extinction and womb. |
| 若生旺化空死墓绝胎，必有始无终。 | 生旺而变成空、死、墓、绝、胎的，必有始无终。 | If thriving and turning into Void, death, tomb, extinction or womb, there is a beginning without an end. |
| 冲克刑害世者，必怀欺难托。 | 冲、克、刑、害世爻的，受托的人必定怀着欺骗，难以托付。 | If the Self is clashed, controlled, punished or harmed, the man entrusted surely harbours deceit and cannot be relied on. |
| 六冲之卦，终成反目。 | 六冲的卦，终究反目成仇。 | A six-clash hexagram ends in a falling out. |
| 虎动者损害，蛇动者心变，勾动者愚钝，武动者，诈欺，雀旺空动者，多诳语。 | 白虎发动的，损害；螣蛇发动的，变心；勾陈发动的，愚钝；玄武发动的，欺诈；朱雀旺相落空而发动的，多说谎话。 | If the Tiger moves, he does harm; the Snake, his heart changes; Hook Earth, he is dull; the Dark Tortoise, he cheats; the Bird thriving and Void and moving, he tells many lies. |
| 惟旺相带龙喜成神，动来生合世身者，则赖其扶持以成事也。 | 只有旺相、带青龙、喜神、成神，发动来生合世爻、身爻的，才靠他扶持把事办成。 | Only if he is thriving and carries the Dragon, Joy and the Completion spirit and moves to generate or combine with the Self and Body does one rely on his support to get the matter done. |

> **按**:「本宫」「他宫」指占得的这一卦所属的宫和别的宫（本宫是用爻或世爻所在的宫，书里没有展开，译文照字面）。「缙绅」是做官的人和乡绅；「牙人」是经纪，「保人」是担保的人，「媒人」是中间说合的人。「赞襄」是帮助。日破是被日辰冲的爻。「成神」书里没有解释，是神煞。「雀旺空动」按字面是朱雀旺相、落空、而发动。
>
> **Note:** 'Home palace' and 'another palace' mean the palace to which the hexagram cast belongs and other palaces (the book does not spell out whose palace the home palace is, and the translation follows the letter). 'Jinshen' are officials and country gentry; 'yaren' are brokers, 'baoren' guarantors, and 'meiren' go-betweens. 'Zanxiang' is helping. Day-break is a line clashed by the Day. 'Chengshen' (the Completion spirit) is a spirit-star that the book does not explain. 'Que wang kong dong' is by the letter the Vermilion Bird thriving, Void, and moving.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《易林补遗》:同文 第 1633 行:六冲之卦，终成反目

相关:LY.CAREER.YY.85、LY.CAREER.YY.87、LY.CAREER.HJC.36

## LY.CAREER.ZS.02 升选候补·子孙持世，休望荣取；官位破空，勿思升选（子孙持世或动于卦中，升选无期；官临空绝墓破，及动而变凶，补升尚早）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:子孙持世，或者在卦中发动，升迁选官没有指望；官星落在空、绝、墓、破上，或者发动变出凶爻，补缺升迁还早得很。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If Output holds the Self or moves in the hexagram, promotion and selection have no hope; if the star of office falls on Void, extinction, tomb or break, or moves and turns into a bad line, it is still early for an appointment or promotion.

**《增删卜易·升选候补章》原文** — 《增删卜易》第 10301–10302 行 · *Zengshan Buyi (Divination, Pruned and Supplemented), "Chapter on promotion, selection and waiting for a post", base text; Zengshan Buyi, lines 10301–10302*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 子孙持世，休望荣取； | 子孙持世，别指望得到荣耀的官职； | If Output holds the Self, do not hope for an honourable post; |
| 官位破空，勿思升选。 | 官位被破、落空，不要想升迁选官。 | if the office position is broken or Void, do not think of promotion or selection. |
| 子孙持世，或动于卦中，升选无期； | 子孙持世，或者在卦中发动，升迁选官没有日期； | if Output holds the Self, or moves in the hexagram, promotion and selection have no date; |
| 官临空绝墓破，及动而变凶，补升尚早。 | 官星临在空、绝、墓、破上，以及发动变出凶爻，补缺升迁还早得很。 | if the star of office falls on Void, extinction, tomb or break, or moves and turns into a bad line, it is still early for an appointment or promotion. |

相关:LY.CAREER.ZS.01、LY.CAREER.ZS.05、LY.CAREER.HJC.03

## LY.CAREER.ZS.03 升选候补·雨露承恩，名中逢合（世与官星三合官局、财局，或局生世、世在局中，或官星持世与日月动爻相合，得六合卦及动变六合，皆主承恩之日；书中例：官局生应不生世，此缺不得）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:世爻与官星三合成官局或财局，或者这个局生世爻，或者世爻在局中，或者官星持世、又与日月动爻相合，得到六合卦，以及发动变成六合卦的，都是得到皇恩的日子。但如果官局生的是应爻、不是世爻，这叫「出现无情」，这个缺得不到。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If the Self and the star of office form a three-harmony Pressure frame or Wealth frame, or the frame generates the Self, or the Self is within the frame, or the star of office holds the Self and combines with the Day, the Month or a moving line, or one gets a Six-Combine hexagram or a hexagram that moves into Six-Combine, it is the day of receiving the ruler's favour. But if the Pressure frame generates the Other and not the Self, this is called 'appearing but without feeling', and the post cannot be had.

**《增删卜易·升选候补章》原文** — 《增删卜易》第 10303–10304 行 · *Zengshan Buyi (Divination, Pruned and Supplemented), "Chapter on promotion, selection and waiting for a post", base text; Zengshan Buyi, lines 10303–10304*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 雨露承恩，名中逢合。 | 像雨露一样承受皇恩，要在功名里逢到合。 | To receive favour like rain and dew, one must meet a combination in the matter of rank. |
| 世与官星三合官局、财局，或局生世、或世在局中，或官星持世与日月动爻相合，得逢六合卦，及动变六合者，皆主承恩之日。 | 世爻与官星三合成官局、财局，或者这个局生世爻，或者世爻在局中，或者官星持世、与日月动爻相合，得到六合卦，以及发动变成六合卦的，都主得到皇恩的日子。 | If the Self and the star of office form a three-harmony Pressure frame or Wealth frame, or the frame generates the Self, or the Self is within the frame, or the star of office holds the Self and combines with the Day, the Month or a moving line, or one gets a Six-Combine hexagram or a hexagram that moves into Six-Combine, all mean the day of receiving favour. |

**书中卦例** — 《增删卜易》第 10305 行 · *Case from the book; Zengshan Buyi, line 10305*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 如：申月乙亥日(旬空：辰巳)，占缺得否？ | 例如：申月乙亥日（旬空：辰、巳），占这个缺能不能得到？ | For example: in the Shen month on a Yihai day (void: Chen and Si), a divination on whether he would get a vacant post; |
| 得“井之节” | 得到井卦变节卦。 | the hexagram was Jing changing into Jie. |

**书中卦例** — 《增删卜易》第 10350 行 · *Case from the book; Zengshan Buyi, line 10350*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 断曰：内卦巳酉丑合成官局，不生世而生应，正所谓“出现无情”也，此缺不得。 | 断语说：内卦巳、酉、丑合成官局，不生世爻而生应爻，正是所谓「出现无情」，这个缺得不到。 | The judgment said: the inner trigram Si, You and Chou combines into a Pressure frame, which does not generate the Self but generates the Other, which is just what is meant by 'appearing but without feeling', and this post cannot be had. |
| 彼问：如何不得？ | 他问：为什么得不到？ | He asked: why can it not be had? |
| 余曰：官生应，如何能得？ | 我说：官星生的是应爻，怎么能得到？ | I said: the star of office generates the Other, so how could he get it? |
| 后果另选别人。 | 后来果然另选了别人。 | In the end someone else was indeed chosen. |

> **按**:「出现无情」书里只用在这个例子上，没有另外解释；按断语的意思，是官局出现了，却不生世、去生了应爻。
>
> **Note:** The book uses 'appearing but without feeling' only in this example and does not explain it elsewhere; from the judgment, it means that the Pressure frame appears but does not generate the Self and instead generates the Other.

相关:LY.CAREER.ZS.01、LY.CAREER.ZS.04

## LY.CAREER.ZS.04 升选候补·风云未际，冲上逢冲（世官不旺又得六冲，或官被日月冲，或六冲变冲，元神衰静、泄气爻摇，问升选无期；世动官化进神，升迁在即）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:时机未到的征兆：世爻、官星都不旺，又得六冲卦，或者官星被日月相冲，或者六冲变六冲；元神衰弱安静，或者泄元神之气的爻发动，问升选就没有日期。反过来，世爻发动、官星变成进神，升迁就在眼前；世爻发动或变吉，或禄、马、贵人临世，或官星发动变吉、生合世爻，都可以收拾行装等着。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Signs that the moment has not come: the Self and the star of office are not thriving and the hexagram is Six-Clash, or the star of office is clashed by the Day or Month, or a Six-Clash hexagram turns into Six-Clash; or the Support is weak and still, or the line that drains it moves, and then promotion has no date. The reverse: if the Self moves and the star of office turns into an Advancing line, promotion is at hand; if the Self moves, or turns auspicious, or Emolument, the Horse and a Noble sit on the Self, or the star of office moves, turns auspicious and generates or combines with the Self, one may pack one's bags and wait.

**《增删卜易·升选候补章》原文** — 《增删卜易》第 10351–10352 行 · *Zengshan Buyi (Divination, Pruned and Supplemented), "Chapter on promotion, selection and waiting for a post", base text; Zengshan Buyi, lines 10351–10352*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 风云未际，冲上逢冲。 | 风云际会的时机还没到，是冲上又逢冲。 | The moment of wind and cloud has not yet come: a clash upon a clash. |
| 世官不旺，又得六冲，或官被日月相冲，或六冲变冲，乃风云未际之时。 | 世爻和官星都不旺，又得到六冲卦，或者官星被日月相冲，或者六冲卦变六冲卦，就是风云际会的时机还没到的时候。 | If the Self and the star of office are not thriving and one gets a Six-Clash hexagram, or the star of office is clashed by the Day or Month, or a Six-Clash hexagram turns into Six-Clash, it is a time when the moment of wind and cloud has not yet come. |
| 元神衰静，泄气爻摇，问升选则无期。 | 元神衰弱安静，或者泄气的爻在动，问升选就没有日期。 | If the Support is weak and still, or the line that drains it is moving, then for promotion and selection there is no date. |
| 如官爻属木，水爻衰而不动，火爻发动是也。 | 比如官爻属木，水爻衰弱而不动，火爻发动，就是这种情形。 | For example, the Pressure line is Wood, the Water line is weak and does not move, and the Fire line moves: that is the case. |
| 余以类推。 | 其余的依此类推。 | The rest follow by analogy. |
| 世爻发动，官化进神，望荣升而在即。 | 世爻发动，官星变成进神，盼望的荣升就在眼前。 | If the Self moves and the star of office turns into an Advancing line, the longed-for promotion is at hand. |
| 世爻发动，或动而化吉，或禄马贵人临世，及官动化吉生合世爻，皆可束装以待。 | 世爻发动，或者发动变吉，或者禄、马、贵人临世，以及官星发动变吉、生合世爻，都可以收拾行装等着。 | If the Self moves, or moves and turns auspicious, or Emolument, the Horse or a Noble sits on the Self, or the star of office moves, turns auspicious and generates or combines with the Self, one may pack one's bags and wait. |

相关:LY.CAREER.ZS.03、LY.CAREER.ZS.05、LY.CAREER.HJC.06

## LY.CAREER.ZS.05 升选候补·世官破而休望，世福空而亦升（世临月破纵官星持世亦不能升；子孙持世值旬空亦不当升，但占两三日之内者许必得，因子孙未出空、不克官；书中例三个）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:世爻或官星被破就不要指望，世爻上的子孙落空却也能升。世爻落在月破上，即使官星持世也升不了，还要防别的变故；子孙持世又遇旬空，也不该升。但如果占的是两三天之内的事，可以答应一定得到，因为子孙还没有出空、没有克官星。作者占过三个人，都得了官，得官后随即死了。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If the Self or the star of office is broken, give up hope; if the Self sits on an Output line that is Void, one is promoted all the same. If the Self is Month-broken, one cannot be promoted even when the star of office holds the Self, and one must guard against other changes; if Output holds the Self and meets Void, one should not be promoted either. But if the question concerns the next two or three days, one may grant that it will surely be had, since Output has not yet left the Void and does not yet control the star of office. The author divined for three men; all got office, and all died soon after getting it.

**《增删卜易·升选候补章》原文** — 《增删卜易》第 10354 行 · *Zengshan Buyi (Divination, Pruned and Supplemented), "Chapter on promotion, selection and waiting for a post", base text; Zengshan Buyi, line 10354*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 世官破而休望，世福空而亦升。 | 世爻和官星被破就不要指望，世爻上的福神落空却也能升。 | If the Self and the star of office are broken, give up hope; if the Self sits on an Output line that is Void, one is promoted all the same. |
| 世临月破，纵官星持世，亦不能升，而犹防他变。 | 世爻落在月破上，即使官星持世，也不能升迁，而且还要防备别的变故。 | If the Self sits in Month-break, even if the star of office holds the Self one cannot be promoted, and one must still guard against other changes. |
| 子孙持世值旬空，亦不当升。 | 子孙持世而遇旬空，也不该升。 | If Output holds the Self and meets Void, one should not be promoted either. |
| 若占两三日之内者，许之必得，其故由子孙尚未出空，不克官也。 | 如果占的是两三天之内的事，可以答应他一定得到，原因是子孙还没有出空，没有克官星。 | If the question is about the next two or three days, one may grant that he will surely get it, because Output has not yet left the Void and so does not control the star of office. |
| 余占过三人，俱皆得官，得后随死。 | 我占过三个人，都得了官，得官之后随即死了。 | I have divined for three men, all of whom got office and all of whom died soon after getting it. |

**书中卦例** — 《增删卜易》第 10355 行 · *Case from the book; Zengshan Buyi, line 10355*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 如：申月戊寅日(旬空：申酉)，占得差否？ | 例如：申月戊寅日（旬空：申、酉），占能不能得到差事？ | For example: in the Shen month on a Wuyin day (void: Shen and You), a divination on whether he would get a commission; |
| 得“泽天夬” | 得到夬卦。 | the hexagram was Guai. |

**书中卦例** — 《增删卜易》第 10386 行 · *Case from the book; Zengshan Buyi, line 10386*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 子孙持世，此差不得； | 子孙持世，这个差事得不到； | Output holds the Self, so this commission cannot be had; |
| 世空，亦不能得。 | 世爻落空，也得不到。 | the Self is Void, so it cannot be had either. |
| 岂知不出三日得差，行至中途而死。 | 哪知道不出三天就得了差事，走到半路上死了。 | Who would have thought that within three days he got the commission, and died on the road halfway there. |
| 寻其故，盖三日之内而得差者，子孙未出空也； | 追究它的缘故，三天之内得到差事，是因为子孙还没有出空； | Looking for the reason: getting the commission within three days was because Output had not yet left the Void; |
| 中途而死者，世爻空也。 | 半路上死，是因为世爻落空。 | dying on the road was because the Self was Void. |

**书中卦例** — 《增删卜易》第 10387 行 · *Case from the book; Zengshan Buyi, line 10387*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 又：寅月庚辰日(旬空：申酉)，占得缺否？ | 又例：寅月庚辰日（旬空：申、酉），占能不能得缺？ | Another: in the Yin month on a Gengchen day (void: Shen and You), a divination on whether he would get a post; |
| 得“水天需” | 得到需卦。 | the hexagram was Xu. |

**书中卦例** — 《增删卜易》第 10394 行 · *Case from the book; Zengshan Buyi, line 10394*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 子孙持世遇旬空，与前卦同，亦是只在三两日命下之事。 | 子孙持世、遇旬空，和前一个卦相同，也是只在三两天内命令就下来的事。 | Output holds the Self and meets Void, the same as the previous hexagram, so again the order will come down within two or three days. |
| 又悟前占卦之差，余竟许之，果得缺，不出两月而亡。 | 又因为看出了前一次占断的差错，我就答应他能得，结果果然得了缺，不到两个月就死了。 | Having also seen where the earlier judgment went wrong, I granted it, and he did get the post, but died in less than two months. |

**书中卦例** — 《增删卜易》第 10395 行 · *Case from the book; Zengshan Buyi, line 10395*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 又：辰月丁丑日(旬空：申酉)，占起用，已列名矣，得“渐之观” | 又例：辰月丁丑日（旬空：申、酉），占起用，已经列了名，得到渐卦变观卦。 | Another: in the Chen month on a Dingchou day (void: Shen and You), a divination on reinstatement, his name already being listed, gave Jian changing into Guan. |

**书中卦例** — 《增删卜易》第 10422 行 · *Case from the book; Zengshan Buyi, line 10422*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 此与前二卦大同小异，前二卦子孙持世遇旬空，未曾发动，此卦动而变官，况亦系两三日内事，子孙尚未出空，竟许必得。 | 这个卦和前面两个卦大同小异：前两个卦子孙持世、遇旬空，没有发动，这个卦是发动后变出官星，何况也是两三天之内的事，子孙还没有出空，我就答应他一定得到。 | This hexagram is much the same as the two before, with small differences: in the previous two Output held the Self and met Void without moving, while in this one it moves and turns into the star of office; moreover it too concerned the next two or three days, and Output had not yet left the Void, so I granted that he would surely get it. |
| 果得起用，行六日而家内子死，抵任八阅月而疾终。 | 结果果然得到起用，上路六天后家里的儿子死了，到任八个月后他自己病故。 | He did get reinstated; six days after setting out his son at home died, and eight months after he took up his post he himself died of illness. |
| 身死子亡者，乃是世与子孙同变鬼也。 | 自己死、儿子也死，是因为世爻和子孙一起变成了官鬼。 | That he and his son both died was because the Self and Output both turned into Pressure. |
| 余于后二卦之得验者，实因前卦点差，时刻留心故耳！ | 我在后面两个卦能断准，实在是因为前一个卦让我发现了差错，时刻留心的缘故！ | That I judged the latter two hexagrams correctly was really because the former hexagram showed me my error and I kept it constantly in mind! |
| 今人占过即忘，何由精奥。 | 现在的人占过就忘，哪能精通其中的奥妙。 | People today forget as soon as they have divined, so how could they master its depths? |

> **按**:「世福空而亦升」按注文的意思是：世爻持子孙又落旬空，一般不该升，只有占两三天之内的事才许得，因为子孙没出空就克不了官。「前卦点差」「又悟前占卦之差」是说第一例让他看出了自己断错的地方，后两例才断准。
>
> **Note:** 'The Self on a Void Output line is promoted all the same' means, by the note, that when the Self holds Output and it falls in Void one is generally not due for promotion, and may be granted it only for a question of two or three days, since Output cannot control the star of office while it has not left the Void. 'The former hexagram pointed out the error' and 'also realized the error of the earlier divination' mean that the first example showed him where his own judgment had been wrong, and he judged the next two correctly.

相关:LY.CAREER.ZS.02、LY.CAREER.ZS.04、LY.CAREER.ZS.06、LY.CAREER.HJC.08、LY.CAREER.HJC.09

## LY.CAREER.ZS.22 援例·财旺官空，且自堆金于白屋（财旺世旺而官临月破旬空，或被日月冲克、动而变凶，官如朽木枯枝，有旺财也生不起，终是白屋富翁，难食王家天禄）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:用钱买官（援例）：妻财旺相、世爻旺相，但官星落在月破、旬空上，或被日月冲克，以及发动变凶，官星像朽木枯枝，有再旺相的财也生不起它，到头来只是个有钱的平民，吃不上国家的俸禄。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Buying office by precedent: if Wealth and the Self are thriving but the star of office falls in Month-break or Void, or is clashed or controlled by the Day or Month, or moves and turns bad, the star of office is like a rotten log and a dry branch: though Wealth is thriving it cannot raise it, and in the end one is only a rich man in a commoner's house who cannot eat the heavenly salary of the royal house.

**《增删卜易·援例章》原文** — 《增删卜易》第 10610 行 · *Zengshan Buyi (Divination, Pruned and Supplemented), "Chapter on buying office by precedent", base text; Zengshan Buyi, line 10610*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 财旺官空，且自堆金于白屋。 | 财旺相而官星落空，只能自己在平民的白屋里堆金子。 | With Wealth thriving but the star of office Void, one can only pile up gold in a white-roofed house (a commoner's home). |
| 财旺世旺，而官临月破旬空，或被日月冲克，及动而变凶，官如朽木枯枝，虽有旺相之财，生之不起，终属白屋之富翁，难食王家之天禄。 | 财旺相、世爻旺相，但官星临在月破、旬空上，或者被日月冲克，以及发动变凶，官星就像朽木枯枝，虽然有旺相的妻财，也生不起来，终究只是平民之家的富翁，难以享用王家的俸禄。 | If Wealth and the Self are thriving but the star of office falls in Month-break or Void, or is clashed or controlled by the Day or Month, or moves and turns bad, the star of office is like a rotten log and a dry branch: though there is thriving Wealth it cannot raise it, and in the end one is only a rich man in a commoner's house, who cannot eat the heavenly salary of the royal house. |

> **按**:「援例」是按成例出钱捐官（捐纳）；书里没有另外解释，这里是按标题「凡用财图名者同此」和内容读的。
>
> **Note:** 'Yuanli' is buying office or rank with money under established precedent; the book does not explain it separately, and this is how it reads from the heading 'the same for all who use money to seek a name' and from the content.

相关:LY.CAREER.ZS.23、LY.CAREER.HJC.01
