## LY.CAREER.ZS.23 援例·妻摇鬼旺，定然执王拜丹墀（世、官星、财星三者不可一陷：财官旺而世陷，得官无福安享；官世旺而财陷，有例无财；书中例：豫之否，财动生官世临月破，名成而身丧；困之兑，世休囚动变鬼，次年得噎疾而终）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:援例捐官，世爻、官星、财星这三样不能有一样失陷：妻财、官星都旺而世爻失陷，终究得了官也没有福气安享；官星、世爻都旺而财星失陷，是有例可援却没有钱。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** In buying office by precedent, of the Self, the star of office and the star of Wealth, not one may be lost: if Wealth and the star of office are both thriving but the Self is lost, one may in the end get office but has no fortune to enjoy it in peace; if the star of office and the Self are both thriving but the star of Wealth is lost, there is a precedent to appeal to but no money.

**《增删卜易·援例章》原文** — 《增删卜易》第 10611–10612 行 · *Zengshan Buyi (Divination, Pruned and Supplemented), "Chapter on buying office by precedent", base text; Zengshan Buyi, lines 10611–10612*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 妻摇鬼旺，定然执王拜丹墀。 | 妻财摇动、官鬼旺相，必定能上殿朝拜、得官。 | When Wealth sways and Pressure thrives, one will surely bow at the vermilion steps of the court. |
| 世与官星、财星，三者不可一陷，若财官两旺，而世爻失陷者，终使得官，无福安享； | 世爻、官星、财星这三样不能有一样失陷；如果财、官都旺，而世爻失陷，终究得了官，也没有福气安享； | Of the Self, the star of office and the star of Wealth, not one may be lost: if Wealth and the star of office are both thriving but the Self is lost, one may in the end get office but has no fortune to enjoy it in peace; |
| 若官世两旺，财星失陷者，乃有例无财。 | 如果官星、世爻都旺，而财星失陷，就是有例可援而没有钱。 | if the star of office and the Self are both thriving but the star of Wealth is lost, there is a precedent to appeal to but no money. |

**书中卦例** — 《增删卜易》第 10613 行 · *Case from the book; Zengshan Buyi, line 10613*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 如：丑月乙卯日(旬空：子丑)，占援例，得“豫之否” | 例如：丑月乙卯日（旬空：子、丑），占援例，得到豫卦变否卦。 | For example: in the Chou month on a Yimao day (void: Zi and Chou), a divination on buying office by precedent gave Yu changing into Pi. |

**书中卦例** — 《增删卜易》第 10656 行 · *Case from the book; Zengshan Buyi, line 10656*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 财动生官，不宜世临月破，破而被克，有官不能享也。 | 财爻发动生官星，不宜世爻临月破，被破又被克，有了官也不能享用。 | Wealth moves to generate the star of office, but it is not good for the Self to sit in Month-break; broken and also controlled, one has the office but cannot enjoy it. |
| 后竟纳之，名成而身丧矣。 | 后来他竟然还是捐纳了，功名成了，人却死了。 | Afterwards he went ahead and made the payment; the rank was gained but he himself died. |

**书中卦例** — 《增删卜易》第 10656 行 · *Case from the book; Zengshan Buyi, line 10656*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 又如：戌月癸丑日(旬空：寅卯)，占捐复，得“困之兑” | 又例：戌月癸丑日（旬空：寅、卯），占捐复，得到困卦变兑卦。 | Another: in the Xu month on a Guichou day (void: Yin and Mao), a divination on buying reinstatement gave Kun changing into Dui. |

**书中卦例** — 《增删卜易》第 10682 行 · *Case from the book; Zengshan Buyi, line 10682*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 世爻休囚，动而变鬼，不可行也。 | 世爻休囚，发动变出官鬼，不可以去办。 | The Self is resting or trapped and moves into Pressure, so it should not be done. |
| 不听。 | 他不听。 | He would not listen. |
| 余曰：明年秋令甚低，名成亦不能出仕。 | 我说：明年秋天时令很低，功名成了也不能出来做官。 | I said: next year's autumn is very low, so even if the rank is gained you will not be able to take up office. |
| 果捐复，至次年正月得噎疾，七月而终。 | 他果然捐复，到第二年正月得了噎病，七月就去世了。 | He did buy the reinstatement; in the first month of the next year he fell ill with a choking disease and died in the seventh month. |

> **按**:「执王拜丹墀」原文如此，意思是上殿朝拜、得官；「王」字可能是「玉」（手执玉圭）的误字，译文只按意思译。「捐复」是出钱捐纳恢复官职。
>
> **Note:** 'Holding the king and bowing at the vermilion steps' is how the text reads and means bowing at court and gaining office; the character 'king' may be a slip for 'jade' (holding the jade tablet), and the translation follows the sense only. 'Juanfu' is buying reinstatement in office with money.

相关:LY.CAREER.ZS.22、LY.CAREER.ZS.24

## LY.CAREER.ZS.24 援例·财静子飞空用意（子孙持世，财爻动不动皆不可行；财临空破亦不可行；子财官同动又可行，因子动生财、财动生官）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:援例捐官，子孙持世，不管财爻动不动，都不可以去办；财爻落空落破，也不可以去办；但子孙、财、官一起发动又可以去办，因为子孙动生财、财动生官。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** In buying office by precedent, if Output holds the Self, whether or not the Wealth line moves it should not be done; if Wealth falls in Void or break, it should not be done either; but if Output, Wealth and the star of office all move together, it may be done, because Output moves to generate Wealth and Wealth moves to generate the star of office.

**《增删卜易·援例章》原文** — 《增删卜易》第 10683 行 · *Zengshan Buyi (Divination, Pruned and Supplemented), "Chapter on buying office by precedent", base text; Zengshan Buyi, line 10683*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 财静子飞空用意。 | 财爻安静而子孙飞动，用意落空。 | If Wealth is still while Output flies, the intent is spent in vain. |
| 子孙持世，财爻动与不动，皆不可行； | 子孙持世，财爻动不动，都不可以去办； | If Output holds the Self, whether or not the Wealth line moves it should not be done; |
| 财临空破，亦不可行。 | 财爻临空、临破，也不可以去办。 | if Wealth falls in Void or break, it should not be done either. |
| 子财官同动者，又可行之，其故以子动生财，财动以生官也。 | 子孙、财、官一起发动的，又可以去办，原因是子孙动生财、财动生官。 | If Output, Wealth and the star of office all move together, it may be done, because Output moves to generate Wealth and Wealth moves to generate the star of office. |

相关:LY.CAREER.ZS.23、LY.CAREER.ZS.25

---

## LY.CAREER.ZS.25 援例·兄兴财动枉劳心（兄弟持世或兄发动，破耗之神，枉费其财名终不就，与官同动则可行；野鹤：官星不动而兄持世、兄父发动、财持世化兄，百无一成）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:援例捐官，兄弟爻持世，或者兄爻发动，是破耗的神，白花钱财，功名终究不成；如果和官星一起发动，又可以去办，因为官鬼能制住兄弟。野鹤说：卦中官星不动，只要遇到兄弟持世、兄爻和父母爻发动、以及财爻持世变出兄弟，百件里没有一件成，即使成了也必有别的缘故，到头来没有地方吃俸禄。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** In buying office by precedent, if the Peer line holds the Self or a Peer line moves, it is a spirit of waste and loss: the money is spent for nothing and the rank is never gained; if it moves together with the star of office it may be done, since Pressure restrains Peer. The Old Man of Yehe says: if the star of office does not move in the hexagram, and Peer holds the Self, or Peer and Resource lines move, or the Wealth line holds the Self and turns into Peer, not one in a hundred succeeds, and even if it succeeds there will surely be some other cause, and in the end one has no place to eat a salary.

**《增删卜易·援例章》原文** — 《增删卜易》第 10684 行 · *Zengshan Buyi (Divination, Pruned and Supplemented), "Chapter on buying office by precedent", base text; Zengshan Buyi, line 10684*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 兄兴财动枉劳心。 | 兄弟兴旺、财爻发动，白白劳心。 | With Peer flourishing and Wealth moving, one wears out one's heart for nothing. |
| 兄弟爻持世，或兄爻发动，乃是破耗之神，枉费其财，名终不就。 | 兄弟爻持世，或者兄爻发动，是破耗的神，白白花了钱财，功名终究不成。 | If the Peer line holds the Self, or a Peer line moves, it is a spirit of waste and loss: the money is spent for nothing, and the rank is never gained. |
| 若与官同动者，又可行之，以鬼而制兄也。 | 如果和官星一起发动，又可以去办，因为官鬼可以制住兄弟。 | If it moves together with the star of office, it may be done, since Pressure restrains Peer. |

**野鹤老人(《增删卜易》)** — 《增删卜易》第 10685 行 · *The Old Man of Yehe (Zengshan Buyi); Zengshan Buyi, line 10685*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 野鹤曰：卦中官星不动，但遇兄弟持世、兄父发动，及财爻持世化兄，百无一成，即使成后，必有他故，终食禄无地也。 | 野鹤说：卦中官星不动，只要遇到兄弟持世、兄爻和父母爻发动，以及财爻持世变出兄弟，百件里没有一件成；即使成了之后，也必有别的缘故，终究没有地方吃俸禄。 | The Old Man of Yehe says: if the star of office does not move in the hexagram, and Peer holds the Self, or Peer and Resource lines move, or the Wealth line holds the Self and turns into Peer, not one in a hundred succeeds, and even if it succeeds there will surely be some other cause, and one will in the end have no place to eat a salary. |

相关:LY.CAREER.ZS.24、LY.CAREER.HJC.04

## LY.CAREER.ZS.28 上书·旺父持世，宜日月岁五以维持（父母为文书奏章，持世合世奏必见准，再得岁五日月动爻生合，见用无疑）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:上书奏事，父母爻就是文书、奏章：旺相的父母爻持世、合世，奏章必定被批准；再得到太岁、五爻和日月、动爻来生合的，被采用无疑。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** In submitting a memorial the Resource line is the document and the memorial: if a thriving Resource line holds or combines with the Self, the memorial will surely be approved, and if Tai Sui, the fifth line, the Day, the Month or a moving line also generates or combines with it, it will be adopted without doubt.

**《增删卜易·面圣、上书、叩阍、献策、条陈、劾奏章》原文** — 《增删卜易》第 10712 行 · *Zengshan Buyi (Divination, Pruned and Supplemented), "Chapter on audiences with the emperor, memorials, petitions, proposals and impeachments", base text; Zengshan Buyi, line 10712*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 旺父持世，宜日月岁五以维持。 | 旺相的父母爻持世，适合有日月、太岁、五爻来扶持。 | A thriving Resource line holding the Self does well with the Day, the Month, Tai Sui or the fifth line to sustain it. |
| 父母为文书、奏章，持世、合世，奏必见准，再得岁五及日月动爻生合者，见用无疑。 | 父母爻是文书、奏章，持世、合世，奏章必定被批准；再得到太岁、五爻和日月、动爻来生合的，被采用无疑。 | The Resource line is documents and memorials: if it holds or combines with the Self, the memorial will surely be approved, and if Tai Sui, the fifth line, the Day, the Month or a moving line also generates or combines with it, it will be adopted without doubt. |

相关:LY.CAREER.ZS.29、LY.CAREER.ZS.30

## LY.CAREER.ZS.29 上书·兄弟临身，喜父母化吉而拱合（兄弟持世，日月生之，或世、父母动而化吉，或世化父以生世，皆得喜动龙颜、因文见用）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:兄弟临身时，喜欢父母爻变吉并来拱合：兄弟持世，有日月生它，或者世爻、父母爻发动变吉，或者世爻变出父母爻来生世，都能使皇上龙颜大悦，因为奏章被采用。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** If Peer sits on the Body, it is good that the Resource line turns auspicious and holds up and combines: if Peer holds the Self and the Day and Month generate it, or the Self or the Resource line moves and turns auspicious, or the Self turns into Resource to generate the Self, all of these please the dragon countenance, and one is employed because of the document.

**《增删卜易·面圣、上书、叩阍、献策、条陈、劾奏章》原文** — 《增删卜易》第 10713–10715 行 · *Zengshan Buyi (Divination, Pruned and Supplemented), "Chapter on audiences with the emperor, memorials, petitions, proposals and impeachments", base text; Zengshan Buyi, lines 10713–10715*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 兄弟临身，喜父母化吉而拱合。 | 兄弟临身，喜欢父母爻变吉并来拱合。 | If Peer sits on the Body, it is good that Resource turns auspicious and holds up and combines. |
| 兄弟持世，日月生之，或世爻、父母爻动而化吉，或世化父以生世，以上皆得喜动龙颜，因文见用。 | 兄弟持世，有日月生它，或者世爻、父母爻发动变吉，或者世爻变出父母爻来生世，以上都能使皇上龙颜大悦，因为奏章被采用。 | If Peer holds the Self and the Day and Month generate it, or the Self or the Resource line moves and turns auspicious, or the Self turns into Resource to generate the Self, all of these please the dragon countenance, and one is employed because of the document. |

相关:LY.CAREER.ZS.28、LY.CAREER.ZS.30

## LY.CAREER.ZS.33 上书·子动临身，力能折槛（野鹤：有职之官陈奏忌子动，但条陈将相、诤谏君非这类事则以子孙为福神，反喜子孙持世，面折庭诤可保无祸；古以子发乾宫为吉，非也；书中例：旅卦，劾奏权奸，果蒙准行）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:条陈将相、诤谏君过、弹劾奸臣这类事，和一般的奏事不一样：这里子孙要当福神用，所以反而喜欢子孙持世，即使当面诤谏也可以保证没有祸。但有官职的人如果只是为了保全功名，子孙发动时千万不要去做。古人以子孙在乾宫发动为吉，是不对的，子孙是制住官鬼的福神，没有听说只在乾宫才是福神。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** For proposals about the generals and chancellors, remonstrance against the ruler's faults and the impeachment of a treacherous minister, the rule differs from ordinary memorials: here Output is used as the lucky spirit, so Output holding the Self is welcome instead, and even if one contends to the ruler's face one can be sure of no misfortune. But an official who only wants to preserve his rank must not do it when Output moves. The ancients took Output moving in the Qian palace as auspicious, which is wrong: Output is the lucky spirit that restrains Pressure, and it has never been heard that it is so only in the Qian palace.

**《增删卜易·面圣、上书、叩阍、献策、条陈、劾奏章》原文** — 《增删卜易》第 10719 行 · *Zengshan Buyi (Divination, Pruned and Supplemented), "Chapter on audiences with the emperor, memorials, petitions, proposals and impeachments", base text; Zengshan Buyi, line 10719*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 子动临身，力能折槛。 | 子孙发动临身，有力量像朱云那样折断殿槛、直言进谏。 | With Output moving on the Body, one has the strength to break the railing. |

**野鹤老人(《增删卜易》)** — 《增删卜易》第 10719–10720 行 · *The Old Man of Yehe (Zengshan Buyi); Zengshan Buyi, lines 10719–10720*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 野鹤曰：或疑前说子动克官，不宜见之，此喜子孙持世者，何也？ | 野鹤说：有人怀疑，前面说子孙发动克官星，不宜见到它，这里却喜欢子孙持世，是为什么？ | The Old Man of Yehe says: someone may doubt it: earlier it was said that a moving Output controls the star of office and is not welcome, yet here Output holding the Self is welcome; why? |
| 殊不知须看事之大小耳。 | 殊不知要看事情的大小。 | It is not understood that one must look at the size of the matter. |
| 有职之官，行此事者，预料此事，设不准行，定然革职。 | 有官职的人做这件事，事先就料到，如果不批准施行，必定革职。 | An official with a post who does this foresees that if it is not approved he will certainly be dismissed. |
| 子孙者，即为克官之神也。 | 子孙，就是克官星的神。 | Output is the spirit that controls the star of office. |
| 欲保全功名，子孙发动，切勿行之。 | 想要保全功名，子孙发动的时候，千万不要去做。 | If one wants to preserve one's rank, when Output moves one must not do it. |
| 倘若条陈将相，诤谏君非，若得准行，名垂青史； | 如果是向将相条陈、直言劝谏君主的过失，若得到批准施行，名垂青史； | But if one submits a proposal about the generals and chancellors, or remonstrates against the ruler's faults, then if it is approved and carried out, one's name goes down in history; |
| 如不见准，祸及其身。 | 如果不被批准，祸患落到自己身上。 | if it is not approved, misfortune falls on oneself. |
| 此处则以子孙为福神矣，所以反喜子孙持世，即使面折庭诤，可保无祸。 | 这里就以子孙为福神了，所以反而喜欢子孙持世，即使当面折服、在朝廷上直言争谏，也可以保证没有祸。 | Here Output is taken as the lucky spirit, so Output holding the Self is welcome instead, and even if one confronts the ruler to his face and contends in court, one can be sure of no misfortune. |
| 古以子发乾宫为吉，非也。 | 古人以子孙在乾宫发动为吉，是不对的。 | The ancients took Output moving in the Qian palace as auspicious, which is wrong. |
| 夫子孙为制鬼之福神，未闻独在乾宫而为福神也。 | 子孙是制住官鬼的福神，没有听说只在乾宫才是福神。 | Output is the lucky spirit that restrains Pressure; it has never been heard that it is the lucky spirit only in the Qian palace. |

**书中卦例** — 《增删卜易》第 10721 行 · *Case from the book; Zengshan Buyi, line 10721*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 如：巳年巳月丁卯日(旬空：戌亥)，占劾奏，得“旅卦” | 例如：巳年巳月丁卯日（旬空：戌、亥），占弹劾上奏，得到旅卦。 | For example: in a Si year, Si month, on a Dingmao day (void: Xu and Hai), a divination on impeaching a minister gave Lü. |

**书中卦例** — 《增删卜易》第 10728 行 · *Case from the book; Zengshan Buyi, line 10728*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 占者问曰：我欲劾奏权奸，恐其根固，反受其害，尔详此卦何如？ | 来占的人问：我想弹劾权奸，担心他根基牢固，反而被他所害，请你详细看看这个卦怎么样？ | The man who came to divine asked: I wish to impeach a powerful and treacherous minister, but fear his roots are firm and I shall be harmed instead; please examine this hexagram carefully, how is it? |
| 余曰：彼虽根深蒂固，今已坏矣。 | 我说：他虽然根深蒂固，现在已经坏了。 | I said: though his roots are deep and firm, they are now spoiled. |
| 问：何以知之。 | 问：凭什么知道？ | He asked: how do you know? |
| 余曰：应爻酉金，长生于巳年巳月，岂非根固耶？ | 我说：应爻是酉金，在巳年巳月长生，难道不是根基牢固吗？ | I said: the Other line is You Metal, which is born in a Si year and Si month; is that not firm roots? |
| 今被岁月克之、卯日冲之，有伤无救，所以知其权势自此衰矣。 | 现在被太岁、月建克它，又被卯日冲它，只有伤害没有救助，所以知道他的权势从此衰落了。 | But now the year and the month control it and the Mao day clashes it, with injury and no rescue, so I know his power will decline from now on. |
| 又问：我有害否？ | 又问：我有没有害处？ | He asked again: will I be harmed? |
| 余曰：子孙持世，何害之有？ | 我说：子孙持世，有什么害处？ | I said: Output holds the Self, so what harm could there be? |
| 果蒙准行，奸势大败。 | 结果果然被批准施行，奸臣的势力大败。 | And indeed it was approved and carried out, and the treacherous faction was utterly defeated. |

> **按**:「折槛」是汉朝朱云当面直谏、攀折殿槛的典故，书里没有讲，译文是按通行的意思注的。前一条（ZS.31）说有官职的人上奏忌见子孙，这一条说子孙持世反吉，野鹤自己的解释是：要看事情的大小。
>
> **Note:** 'Breaking the railing' alludes to Zhu Yun of Han, who remonstrated to the emperor's face and clung to and broke the palace railing; the book does not tell it, and the translation gives the usual sense. The previous entry (ZS.31) says an official with a post should not see Output in a memorial and this one says Output holding the Self is good instead; the Old Man's own explanation is that it depends on the size of the matter.

相关:LY.CAREER.ZS.31、LY.CAREER.ZS.34

---

## LY.CAREER.ZS.36 上书·父旺官生，叨蒙赠爵；旺官持世，平步青云（旺父持世官动生之，旺官持世日月生之：官则官上加官，士则身登台阁，平人立见平步登云；野鹤：断卦最重来人之一念，借上书作阶梯求名者，官旺文不旺亦可许封官，因文乃敲门瓦）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:上书求赠爵封官：父母旺相、官星生它，或者旺相的官星持世，或者旺相的父母持世、官星发动生它，或者旺官持世、有日月生它，做官的就官上加官，读书人就位至台阁，平民得到这个卦，立刻平步青云。野鹤说：断卦最看重的是来占的人心里的那个念头；借上书当阶梯来求功名的人，官星旺相而文书不旺，也可以答应他得到赠官封职，因为他本来的念头是为了得官，文书只是敲门砖，所以看重官星而不看重父母。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** On submitting a memorial to win a bestowed rank or office: if Resource is thriving and the star of office generates it, or a thriving star of office holds the Self, or a thriving Resource holds the Self and the star of office moves to generate it, or a thriving star of office holds the Self and the Day and Month generate it, an official gets office added to office, a scholar reaches the high ministries, and a commoner who gets it at once climbs to the clouds. The Old Man of Yehe says: the most important thing in judging a hexagram is the one thought of the person who comes; for one who uses a memorial as a ladder to seek a name, if the star of office is thriving though the document is not, one may still grant that he will be given a post and title, because his first thought was to gain office and the document is only the tile to knock at the door, so the star of office is weighed and not Resource.

**《增删卜易·面圣、上书、叩阍、献策、条陈、劾奏章》原文** — 《增删卜易》第 10739–10740 行 · *Zengshan Buyi (Divination, Pruned and Supplemented), "Chapter on audiences with the emperor, memorials, petitions, proposals and impeachments", base text; Zengshan Buyi, lines 10739–10740*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 父旺官生，叨蒙赠爵； | 父母旺相、官星生它，能蒙受赠予爵位； | With Resource thriving and the star of office generating it, one is favoured with a bestowed rank; |
| 旺官持世，平步青云。 | 旺相的官星持世，平步青云。 | a thriving star of office holding the Self means a smooth climb to the clouds. |
| 旺父持世，官动生之； | 旺相的父母持世，官星发动去生它； | A thriving Resource line holds the Self and the star of office moves to generate it; |
| 旺官持世，日月生之； | 旺相的官星持世，有日月生它； | a thriving star of office holds the Self and the Day and Month generate it; |
| 官则官上加官； | 做官的就官上加官； | for an official it is office added to office; |
| 士则身登台阁； | 读书人就位至台阁； | for a scholar it is to reach the high ministries; |
| 平人得者，立见平步登云。 | 平民得到的，立刻就能平步登云。 | for a commoner who gets it, he immediately climbs to the clouds. |

**野鹤老人(《增删卜易》)** — 《增删卜易》第 10741 行 · *The Old Man of Yehe (Zengshan Buyi); Zengshan Buyi, line 10741*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 野鹤曰：断卦者，最重来人之一念。 | 野鹤说：断卦的人，最看重的是来占的人心里的那一个念头。 | The Old Man of Yehe says: for one who judges hexagrams, the most important thing is the single thought of the person who comes. |
| 此行有为人为己者； | 这件事有为别人也为自己的； | In this undertaking some act for others and for themselves; |
| 有为父母自身而雪怨者； | 有为父母、为自己洗雪冤屈的； | some to clear a grievance for their parents or themselves; |
| 有为保全人己之功名者； | 有为保全别人和自己功名的； | some to preserve their own and others' rank; |
| 有为财利而行者； | 有为财利而做的； | some for gain; |
| 有为免自己之赔累，或辨下属之赔累者； | 有为了免除自己的赔累，或为下属辩明赔累的； | some to be spared their own liabilities or to explain their subordinates' liabilities; |
| 为类固多，在人通变。 | 这类事本来很多，要靠人灵活变通。 | There are many such kinds, and it is for the man to adapt. |
| 夫士民上书，虽则亦有忠心为国者，然亦有借作阶梯而求名者，倘得官旺而文不旺，亦可许之赠职封官，其故盖因彼之初念原为得官，文乃敲门之瓦耳，所以重官而不重父。 | 士人百姓上书，虽然也有忠心为国的，但也有借此作为阶梯来求功名的；如果得到官星旺相而文书不旺，也可以答应他得到赠官封职，原因是他最初的念头本来是为了得官，文书只是敲门的砖瓦，所以看重官星而不看重父母爻。 | Scholars and commoners who submit memorials include some who are loyal to the state, but also some who use it as a ladder to seek a name; if the star of office is thriving though the document is not thriving, one may grant that he will be given a post and title, because his first thought was to gain office and the document was only the tile to knock at the door, so the star of office is weighed and not Resource. |

相关:LY.CAREER.ZS.35、LY.CAREER.ZS.37

## LY.CAREER.ZS.51 营造公务防患·六冲不久，六合坚牢（工程以父爻为用神，用神不旺或受冲克又得六冲卦必不坚久，化冲亦然；世休囚被克将来遭害；父旺得六合卦永远坚牢）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:看工程牢不牢：工程都以父爻为用神；用神不旺，或者受冲克，又得六冲卦的，必定不坚固长久，变冲的也一样；还要看世爻旺不旺，世爻如果休囚被克，将来终究要遭它的害；只有父爻旺相、又得六合卦的，才永远坚固牢靠。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** To tell whether a work will be firm: in construction the Resource line is always the Subject; if the Subject is not thriving, or is clashed or controlled, and the hexagram is also Six-Clash, the work will surely not be firm and lasting, and so also when it turns into a clash; one must also see whether the Self is thriving, for if the Self is resting or trapped and controlled one will in the end suffer harm from it; only when the Resource line is thriving and the hexagram is also Six-Combine will it be firm and secure for ever.

**《增删卜易·修陵修河，一切营造公务防患章》原文** — 《增删卜易》第 10909 行 · *Zengshan Buyi (Divination, Pruned and Supplemented), "Chapter on repairing tombs and rivers and guarding against trouble in all public works", base text; Zengshan Buyi, line 10909*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 六冲不久，六合坚牢。 | 六冲卦不长久，六合卦坚固牢靠。 | A Six-Clash hexagram does not last; a Six-Combine hexagram is firm and secure. |
| 工程皆以父爻为用神，用神不旺，或受冲克，又得六冲卦者，必不坚久，化冲者亦然。 | 工程都以父爻为用神，用神不旺，或者受冲克，又得六冲卦的，必定不坚固长久，变冲的也一样。 | In construction works the Resource line is always the Subject; if the Subject is not thriving, or is clashed or controlled, and the hexagram is also Six-Clash, the work will surely not be firm and lasting, and so also when it turns into a clash. |
| 又看世爻旺否，世若休囚被克，将来终遭其害，惟喜父爻旺相，又得六合卦者，永远坚牢。 | 还要看世爻旺不旺，世爻如果休囚被克，将来终究要遭它的害；只喜欢父爻旺相，又得六合卦的，永远坚固牢靠。 | One must also see whether the Self is thriving: if the Self is resting or trapped and controlled, one will in the end suffer harm from it; only when the Resource line is thriving and the hexagram is also Six-Combine will it be firm and secure for ever. |

> **按**:这一条看的是工程本身牢不牢（父爻为用神），上面几条看的是办这件公务的人自己吉凶。
>
> **Note:** This entry judges whether the work itself is sound (the Resource line as the Subject); the entries above judge the fortune of the man who handles the public business.

相关:LY.CAREER.ZS.50、LY.CAREER.ZS.52

---

## LY.CAREER.ZS.55 子占父功名·父旺官动相生皆许成名，财克父、子动伤官、父官变凶、日月克官克父、父官衰墓破绝皆主无益（书中例：涣之夬，寅年起用、终于未年；观之涣，父在京候补，未土父持世巳官生之，补四川）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:儿子占父亲的功名：父母旺相、官星发动相生，或日月作官星来生父母，或父母旺相变出官星，或父母、官星发动变吉，都可以答应成名；财克父母、子孙发动伤官星、父母和官星发动变凶、日月克官星克父母，或者父爻、官爻衰弱入墓被破入绝，都主没有益处。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** A son divining about his father's career: if Resource is thriving and the star of office moves to generate it, or the Day or Month acts as the star of office and generates Resource, or a thriving Resource turns into the star of office, or Resource and the star of office move and turn auspicious, he may be granted a name; if Wealth controls Resource, Output moves to injure the star of office, Resource and the star of office move and turn bad, the Day or Month controls the star of office and Resource, or the Resource line or the Pressure line is weak, in tomb, broken or extinct, all mean no gain.

**《增删卜易·子占父功名章》原文** — 《增删卜易》第 11055–11056 行 · *Zengshan Buyi (Divination, Pruned and Supplemented), "Chapter on a son divining about his father's career", base text; Zengshan Buyi, lines 11055–11056*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 父旺官动相生，日月作官星而生父母，父旺化官，父官动而化吉，皆许成名。 | 父母旺相、官星发动相生，日月作官星来生父母，父母旺相变出官星，父母和官星发动变吉，都可以答应成名。 | If Resource is thriving and the star of office moves to generate it, or the Day or Month acts as the star of office and generates Resource, or a thriving Resource turns into the star of office, or Resource and the star of office move and turn auspicious, all mean he may be granted a name. |
| 财克父、子动伤官、父官动而变凶、日月克官克父，或父爻、官爻衰墓破绝，皆主无益。 | 财克父母、子孙发动伤官星、父母和官星发动变凶、日月克官星克父母，或者父爻、官爻衰弱入墓被破入绝，都主没有益处。 | If Wealth controls Resource, Output moves to injure the star of office, Resource and the star of office move and turn bad, the Day or Month controls the star of office and Resource, or the Resource line or the Pressure line is weak, in tomb, broken or extinct, all mean no gain. |

**书中卦例** — 《增删卜易》第 11057 行 · *Case from the book; Zengshan Buyi, line 11057*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 如：卯月壬辰日(旬空：午未)，占父何时起用，得“涣之夬” | 例如：卯月壬辰日（旬空：午、未），占父亲什么时候被起用，得到涣卦变夬卦。 | For example: in the Mao month on a Renchen day (void: Wu and Wei), a divination on when his father would be reinstated gave Huan changing into Guai. |

**书中卦例** — 《增删卜易》第 11064 行 · *Case from the book; Zengshan Buyi, line 11064*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 内卦寅木父母旺而化出子水之官，许寅年起用。 | 内卦的寅木父母爻旺相，并且变出子水的官星，答应他寅年被起用。 | The Yin Wood Resource line in the inner trigram is thriving and turns into the Zi Water star of office, so I granted that he would be reinstated in a Yin year. |
| 果应于寅年，仍以原品起用，后终于未年。 | 果然应在寅年，仍然按原来的品级起用，后来在未年去世。 | It came true in the Yin year, and he was reinstated at his former grade, and later died in a Wei year. |
| 又以此卦悟出初爻寅木父母，寅年而起用，上爻卯木父母动化未墓，墓于未年。 | 又从这个卦悟出：初爻寅木父母爻，在寅年起用；上爻卯木父母爻发动变入未土墓，墓在未年。 | From this hexagram I also understood: the Yin Wood Resource line in the first place is reinstated in a Yin year, while the Mao Wood Resource line in the top place moves and turns into the tomb of Wei Earth, so the tomb falls in a Wei year. |
| 占时未墓爻空，谓之“空墓以待”，所以终于实墓之年。 | 占的时候未土墓爻落空，这叫「空墓以待」，所以终于填实墓的那一年。 | At the time of the cast the Wei Earth tomb line was Void, which is called 'the Void tomb waiting', so it ended in the year the tomb was filled. |
| 又因用神重叠，逢墓库以收藏也。 | 又因为用神重叠，遇到墓库就被收藏了。 | Also, because the Subject was doubled, meeting the tomb it was stored away. |

**书中卦例** — 《增删卜易》第 11066 行 · *Case from the book; Zengshan Buyi, line 11066*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 又如：辰月戊申日(旬空：寅卯)，占父在京候补，得“观之涣” | 又例：辰月戊申日（旬空：寅、卯），占父亲在京城候补的事，得到观卦变涣卦。 | Another: in the Chen month on a Wushen day (void: Yin and Mao), a divination on his father waiting in the capital for a post gave Guan changing into Huan. |

**书中卦例** — 《增删卜易》第 11072 行 · *Case from the book; Zengshan Buyi, line 11072*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 未土父母持世，巳火官动生之，许即得缺。 | 未土父母持世，巳火官星发动去生它，答应他立刻得缺。 | Wei Earth Resource holds the Self and the Si Fire star of office moves to generate it, so I granted that he would get a post at once. |
| 果补于四川。 | 果然补在四川。 | He was indeed given a post in Sichuan. |
| 巳月归里赴任，巳火生世之故耳。 | 巳月回乡赴任，是巳火生世的缘故。 | He returned to his home district and took up his post in the Si month, because Si Fire generates the Self. |

> **按**:「起用」是被重新任用；「候补」是等候补缺。原文这一章在两个例子之间有一段今人的评语（「居士评」），不收。
>
> **Note:** 'Qiyong' is being given a post again and 'houbu' is waiting for a vacancy. Between the two examples this chapter has a modern comment ('the lay scholar's comment'), which is not quoted.

相关:LY.CAREER.ZS.56、LY.CAREER.ZS.06

## LY.CAREER.ZSZ.01 八卦断例·求官吉：姤「求官官荣」、观「求官为上」、渐「求官渐升」、鼎「求官十全」、家人「谒贵求官获福无量」

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:占求官时，遇到姤卦（求官，官位荣耀）、观卦（求官最好）、渐卦（求官渐渐升迁）、鼎卦（求官十全）、家人卦（谒见贵人、求官，获福无量），都是求官有利。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** In seeking office, meeting the hexagram Gou ('seeking office: the office is glorious'), Guan ('seeking office: the best'), Jian ('seeking office: gradual promotion'), Ding ('seeking office: complete in all ten parts') or Jiaren ('calling on nobles and seeking office, one wins blessing beyond measure') is favourable to seeking office.

**《周易尚占·八卦断例章》原文** — 《周易尚占》第 884 行 · *Zhouyi Shangzhan (Divination First in the Zhou Changes), "Chapter of judgments by example for the eight palaces", base text; Zhouyi Shangzhan, line 884*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 媾者遇也 | 媾（姤），就是相遇。 | Gou (Meeting) means to meet. |
| 谋必有遇 | 谋事一定会有所遇。 | Any plan is sure to meet with an encounter. |
| 求官官荣 | 求官，官位荣耀。 | Seeking office: the office is glorious. |
| 求财财遂 | 求财，财能如愿。 | Seeking wealth: the wealth comes as wished. |
| 病者难安 | 病人难得平安。 | The sick will find it hard to be at peace. |
| 婚姻休娶 | 婚姻，不要娶。 | Marriage: do not take the bride. |
| 卜筮行人 | 占问出门在外的人， | As for divining about a traveller, |
| 端的在路 | 的确还在路上。 | he is indeed on the road. |

**《周易尚占·八卦断例章》原文** — 《周易尚占》第 890 行 · *Zhouyi Shangzhan (Divination First in the Zhou Changes), "Chapter of judgments by example for the eight palaces", base text; Zhouyi Shangzhan, line 890*

| 凡事有望 | 凡事都有指望。 | All matters hold hope. |
| 田蚕进益 | 田蚕有进益。 | Fields and silkworms bring gain. |
| 家宅兴旺 | 家宅兴旺。 | The household flourishes. |
| 占婚最宜 | 占婚事，最合适。 | Asking about marriage: most fitting. |
| 求官为上 | 求官，最好。 | Seeking office: the best. |
| 财聚病痊 | 钱财聚拢，病痊愈。 | Wealth gathers and the illness is cured. |
| 获福无量 | 得福无量。 | Blessing is won beyond measure. |

**《周易尚占·八卦断例章》原文** — 《周易尚占》第 1012 行 · *Zhouyi Shangzhan (Divination First in the Zhou Changes), "Chapter of judgments by example for the eight palaces", base text; Zhouyi Shangzhan, line 1012*

| 田蚕进益 | 田蚕有进益。 | Fields and silkworms bring gain. |
| 求官渐升 | 求官，慢慢升。 | Seeking office: gradual promotion. |

## LY.CAREER.ZSZ.04 八卦断例·吉凶参半：屯「见贵求官先难后易」、师「求官虽吉、见贵遭嗔」、井「求官谒贵（不宜妄为、藏器待时）」

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:遇到屯卦，见贵人、求官先难后易；遇到师卦，求官虽吉，见贵人却会遭到嗔怪；遇到井卦，不宜妄动，求官谒贵要藏好器具等待时机。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Meeting the hexagram Zhun, in seeing a noble and seeking office it is first hard, then easy; meeting Shi, seeking office is auspicious but seeing a noble draws his anger; meeting Jing, rash action does not suit, and in seeking office and calling on nobles one should keep the tools hidden and wait for the time.

**《周易尚占·八卦断例章》原文** — 《周易尚占》第 944 行 · *Zhouyi Shangzhan (Divination First in the Zhou Changes), "Chapter of judgments by example for the eight palaces", base text; Zhouyi Shangzhan, line 944*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 屯者难也 | 屯，就是艰难。 | Zhun (Difficulty) means hardship. |
| 元有亨义 | 本来有亨通的意思。 | At root it carries the sense of success. |
| 目下未伸 | 眼下还伸展不开。 | For now it cannot yet unfold. |
| 到头必遂 | 到头来一定能如愿。 | In the end it will surely be achieved. |
| 一切经营 | 一切经营谋划， | All undertakings |
| 悉皆迟滞 | 全都迟缓阻滞。 | are slow and held up. |
| 见贵求官 | 拜见贵人、求官， | Seeing a noble, seeking office: |
| 先难后易 | 先难后易。 | first hard, then easy. |

**《周易尚占·八卦断例章》原文** — 《周易尚占》第 954 行 · *Zhouyi Shangzhan (Divination First in the Zhou Changes), "Chapter of judgments by example for the eight palaces", base text; Zhouyi Shangzhan, line 954*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 师者众也 | 师，就是众。 | Shi (the Army) means the multitude. |
| 不利占身 | 占自身不利。 | Divining about oneself is unfavourable. |
| 求官虽吉 | 求官虽然吉， | Seeking office is auspicious, |
| 见贵遭嗔 | 但见贵人会遭嗔怪。 | but seeing a noble draws his anger. |
| 休占财帛 | 不要占财帛。 | Do not divine about wealth. |
| 莫问行人 | 不要问行人。 | Do not ask about the traveller. |
| 病人迟瘥 | 病人痊愈得迟。 | The sick recover late. |
| 家宅灾迍 | 家宅有灾祸困顿。 | The household has calamity and hardship. |

**《周易尚占·八卦断例章》原文** — 《周易尚占》第 1066 行 · *Zhouyi Shangzhan (Divination First in the Zhou Changes), "Chapter of judgments by example for the eight palaces", base text; Zhouyi Shangzhan, line 1066*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 井者静也 | 井，就是静。 | Jing (the Well) means stillness. |
| 不宜妄为 | 不宜乱作为。 | Rash action does not suit. |
| 求官谒贵 | 求官、拜见贵人， | Seeking office, calling on nobles: |
| 问病稽迟 | 问病，拖延迟缓。 | Asking about illness: delay and slowness. |
| 田蚕半得 | 田蚕得一半。 | Fields and silkworms yield half. |
| 行人未归 | 在外的人没有回来。 | The traveller has not returned. |
| 占婚阻滞 | 占婚事，阻滞。 | Asking about marriage: obstruction and delay. |
| 藏器待时 | 把本事藏起来，等待时机。 | Keep the tools hidden and wait for the time. |

> **按**:井卦那一行里「求官谒贵」后面没有断语，同一行的前后是「不宜妄为」「藏器待时」，这里按整行的口气读成要等待时机，不确定。
>
> **Note:** In the line for Jing nothing follows 'seeking office, calling on nobles', and the same line has 'rash action does not suit' before it and 'keep the tools hidden and wait for the time' after it, so it is read here as meaning to wait for the time, which is uncertain.

## LY.CAREER.ZSZ.06 六亲断例·持世：官鬼持世必得官（官化官为禄、求官宜疾速、化子必伤官）；子孙持世谒贵求官反不亨；父母持世旺相文书喜信通（化鬼身迁举）

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:官鬼持世，必定得官，文书、印信两样一起看；官化官是得禄，求官宜赶快；化财，占病凶；化父，文书相随；化子，必伤官；化兄，家里不和。子孙持世是福神，事成忧散，占胎问病重重吉利，谒见贵人、求官反而不亨通。父母持世及身宫，旺相，文书、喜信通达；父母化父母，文书一定应许；化子添人口；化鬼，自身得迁升荐举；化财，住宅长忧；兄弟，要从本身去取。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** With Pressure holding the Self one is sure to get office, and the documents and the seal are looked at together; Pressure turning into Pressure is gaining emolument, and in seeking office one should be swift; turning into Wealth, in illness it is bad; turning into Resource, the documents follow one after another; turning into Output, it surely injures office; turning into Peer, the household is not harmonious. Output holding the Self is the spirit of blessing: matters succeed and worries scatter, and in pregnancy and illness it is good upon good, but in calling on a noble or seeking office it does not go through. With Resource holding the Self and the palace of the Body, strong, documents and glad news go through; Resource turning into Resource, the documents are sure to be granted; turning into Output, people are added; turning into Pressure, one is promoted and recommended; turning into Wealth, the dwelling long worries; for Peer, take it from oneself.

**《周易尚占·六亲断例》原文** — 《周易尚占》第 477 行 · *Zhouyi Shangzhan (Divination First in the Zhou Changes), "Judgments by example for the six relations", base text; Zhouyi Shangzhan, line 477*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 官鬼持世必得官， | 官鬼持世，必定得官； | Pressure holding the Self, one is sure to get office; |
| 文书印信两相看， | 文书、印信两样一起看； | look at the documents and the seal together; |
| 占婚问病俱凶犯， | 占婚姻、问疾病，都会犯凶； | for marriage and illness, both run into ill; |
| 破宅伤财身不荣。 | 破宅、伤财，自身不荣。 | the dwelling is broken, wealth hurt and the self not flourishing. |
| 官化官为禄， | 官化官，是得禄； | Pressure turning into Pressure is gaining emolument; |
| 求官宜疾速， | 求官宜赶快； | in seeking office, be swift; |
| 化财占病凶， | 化成财，占病凶； | turning into Wealth, in illness it is bad; |
| 化父文书逐， | 化成父，文书相随而来； | turning into Resource, the documents follow one after another; |
| 化子必伤官， | 化成子，必伤官； | turning into Output, it surely injures office; |
| 化兄家不睦。 | 化成兄，家里不和睦。 | turning into Peer, the household is not harmonious. |

**《周易尚占·六亲断例》原文** — 《周易尚占》第 476 行 · *Zhouyi Shangzhan (Divination First in the Zhou Changes), "Judgments by example for the six relations", base text; Zhouyi Shangzhan, line 476*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 子孙持世为福神， | 子孙持世，是福神； | Output holding the Self is the spirit of blessing; |
| 事成忧散谷财荣， | 事成，忧愁散去，谷和财荣盛； | matters succeed, worries scatter, grain and wealth flourish; |
| 占胎问病重重吉， | 占胎、问病，重重吉利； | for pregnancy and illness, good upon good; |
| 谒贵求官反不亨。 | 谒见贵人、求官，反而不亨通。 | for calling on a noble or seeking office, on the contrary it does not go through. |
| 子孙化子孙， | 子孙化子孙， | Output turning into Output, |
| 人情两称情， | 人情两边都称心； | human feeling pleases both sides; |
| 化父田蚕旺， | 化成父，田蚕旺； | turning into Resource, fields and silkworms thrive; |
| 化财加倍荣， | 化成财，加倍荣盛； | turning into Wealth, doubly flourishing; |
| 化鬼忧产病， | 化成鬼，担忧生产、疾病； | turning into Pressure, worry for childbirth and illness; |
| 兄弟必相争。 | 兄弟，必定相争。 | Peer, the brothers, will surely quarrel. |

**《周易尚占·六亲断例》原文** — 《周易尚占》第 475 行 · *Zhouyi Shangzhan (Divination First in the Zhou Changes), "Judgments by example for the six relations", base text; Zhouyi Shangzhan, line 475*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 父母持世及身宫， | 父母持世以及身宫， | Resource holding the Self and the palace of the Body, |
| 旺相文书喜信通， | 旺相，文书、喜信通达； | strong, documents and glad news go through; |
| 田宅禾苗皆遂意， | 田宅、禾苗都遂意； | fields, houses and crops all as one wishes; |
| 占身问病却成凶。 | 占自身、问疾病，却成凶。 | but for the self and for illness it turns ill. |
| 父母化父母， | 父母化父母， | Resource turning into Resource, |
| 文书定相许， | 文书一定应许； | the documents are sure to be granted; |
| 化子进人丁， | 化成子，添进人口； | turning into Output, people are added; |
| 化鬼身迁举， | 化成鬼，自身得迁升荐举； | turning into Pressure, one is promoted and recommended; |
| 化财宅长忧， | 化成财，住宅长久忧愁； | turning into Wealth, the dwelling long worries; |
| 兄弟本身取。 | 兄弟，要从本身去取。 | Peer: take it from oneself. |

> **按**:「化父文书逐」的「逐」没有解释，这里读作「文书相随而来」，不确定；「兄弟本身取」也没有解释，按字面译。这三行各问题通用，这里按次序把官鬼持世、子孙持世、父母持世三行都收了。
>
> **Note:** 'Turning into Resource, the documents chase' does not explain the 'chase', and it is read here as 'the documents follow one after another', which is uncertain; 'Peer, take it from oneself' is not explained either and is translated literally. These three lines serve every kind of question, and the lines for Pressure, Output and Resource holding the Self are all taken here in order.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮大全》:同文 第 3179 行:官鬼持世必得官;同文 第 3179 行:文书印信两相看;异文(相似 0.86) 第 3179 行:占婚问病俱凶;异文(相似 0.86) 第 3179 行:破宅伤财身不
- 《火珠林》:同文 第 948 行:官鬼持世必得官;同文 第 948 行:文书印信两相看;异文(相似 0.86) 第 948 行:占婚问病俱凶;异文(相似 0.86) 第 948 行:破宅伤财身不
- 《卜筮大全》:同文 第 3177 行:子孙持世为福神;异文(相似 0.86) 第 3177 行:事成忧散谷财;异文(相似 0.86) 第 3177 行:占胎问病重重;异文(相似 0.86) 第 3177 行:遏贵求官反不亨
- 《火珠林》:同文 第 948 行:子孙持世为福神;异文(相似 0.86) 第 948 行:事成忧散谷财;异文(相似 0.86) 第 948 行:占胎问病重重;异文(相似 0.86) 第 948 行:遏贵求官反不亨

## LY.CAREER.ZSZ.07 六神断例·青龙发动万事通进财进禄；朱雀交重文印旺、杀神相并徒劳功；白虎交重惊怪事、求官临鬼反丰隆

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:青龙发动，万事通达，进财进禄，临在凶上、遇杀都无妨碍，只忌落在金上和落空；朱雀交重，文书印绶旺，和杀神并在一起就白劳功夫，是非口舌都由此而来，持水、临空反而有利于公事；白虎交重，是惊恐怪异之事，持金、世上带杀妨碍人口，求官时临在官鬼上，反而丰盛隆盛。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** With the Azure Dragon moving, all matters go through and wealth and emolument come in, and standing on ill or meeting a baleful spirit it meets no obstruction, only avoid its standing on Metal or falling void; with the Vermilion Bird moving heavily the documents and the seal and ribbon are strong, joined with a baleful spirit the labour is spent in vain, quarrels and gossip all come from this, and holding Water or standing in the void it is on the contrary favourable to public business; with the White Tiger moving heavily it is fright and strange things, holding Metal with a baleful spirit on the Self harms the household, and in seeking office, standing on Pressure, it brings abundance and prosperity on the contrary.

**《周易尚占·六神断例》原文** — 《周易尚占》第 483 行 · *Zhouyi Shangzhan (Divination First in the Zhou Changes), "Judgments by example for the six spirits", base text; Zhouyi Shangzhan, line 483*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 发动青龙万事通， | 青龙发动，万事通达； | With the Azure Dragon moving, all matters go through; |
| 进财进禄福无穷， | 进财进禄，福气无穷； | wealth and emolument come in and blessing has no end; |
| 临凶遇杀都无碍， | 临在凶上、遇杀，都没有妨碍； | standing on ill or meeting a baleful spirit, there is no obstruction; |
| 惟忌临金与落空。 | 只忌讳落在金上和落空。 | only avoid its standing on Metal and falling void. |

**《周易尚占·六神断例》原文** — 《周易尚占》第 484 行 · *Zhouyi Shangzhan (Divination First in the Zhou Changes), "Judgments by example for the six spirits", base text; Zhouyi Shangzhan, line 484*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 朱雀交重文印旺， | 朱雀交重，文书印绶旺； | With the Vermilion Bird moving heavily, the documents and the seal and ribbon are strong; |
| 杀神相并谩劳功， | 和杀神并在一起，白劳功夫； | joined with a baleful spirit, labour is spent in vain; |
| 是非口舌皆因此， | 是非口舌都因此而起； | quarrels and gossip all come from this; |
| 持水临空却利公。 | 持水、临空，反而有利于公事。 | holding Water or standing in the void, it is on the contrary favourable to public business. |

**《周易尚占·六神断例》原文** — 《周易尚占》第 487 行 · *Zhouyi Shangzhan (Divination First in the Zhou Changes), "Judgments by example for the six spirits", base text; Zhouyi Shangzhan, line 487*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 白虎交重惊怪事， | 白虎交重，是惊恐怪异之事； | With the White Tiger moving heavily, it is fright and strange things; |
| 求官临鬼反丰隆， | 求官时临在官鬼上，反而丰盛隆盛； | in seeking office, standing on Pressure, it brings abundance and prosperity on the contrary; |
| 持金世杀妨人口， | 持金、世上带杀，妨碍人口； | holding Metal with a baleful spirit on the Self harms the household; |
| 遇火加空却不同。 | 遇火再加空，就不一样了。 | meeting Fire and Void, it is otherwise. |

> **按**:这三首和海底眼「六神爻动」里的青龙、朱雀、白虎三首是同一首诗，字句基本相同（海底眼多出每句后面的小注）。白虎一般主凶，这里说求官临鬼反而「丰隆」，照原文录。
>
> **Note:** These three are the same poems as the Azure Dragon, Vermilion Bird and White Tiger poems in the Haidi Yan's 'The six spirits moving', nearly word for word (the Haidi Yan adds a small note after each line). The White Tiger generally stands for ill, yet here it is said that in seeking office, on Pressure, it brings abundance on the contrary, as the text has it.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮大全》:同文 第 3221 行:发动青龙万事通;同文 第 3221 行:进财进禄福无穷;同文 第 3221 行:临凶遇杀都无碍;同文 第 3221 行:惟忌临金与落空
- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 270 行:进财进禄福无穷
- 《海底眼》:异文(相似 0.86) 第 346 行:进财旺禄福无穷;同文 第 346 行:临凶遇杀都无碍;同文 第 346 行:惟忌临金与落空
- 《火珠林》:同文 第 1017 行:发动青龙万事通;同文 第 1017 行:进财进禄福无穷;同文 第 1017 行:临凶遇杀都无碍;同文 第 1017 行:惟忌临金与落空
- 《卜筮大全》:同文 第 3223 行:朱雀交重文印旺;同文 第 3223 行:杀神相并谩劳功;同文 第 3223 行:是非口舌皆因此;同文 第 3223 行:持水临空却利公
- 《卜筮正宗》:同文 第 270 行:朱雀交重文印旺;同文 第 270 行:是非口舌皆因此
- 《海底眼》:同文 第 350 行:朱雀交重文印旺;同文 第 350 行:杀神相并谩劳功;同文 第 350 行:是非口舌皆因此;同文 第 350 行:持水临空却利公
- 《火珠林》:同文 第 1017 行:朱雀交重文印旺;同文 第 1017 行:杀神相并谩劳功;同文 第 1017 行:是非口舌皆因此;同文 第 1017 行:持水临空却利公
- 《海底眼》:同文 第 362 行:白虎交重惊怪事;同文 第 362 行:求官临鬼反丰隆;同文 第 362 行:持金世杀妨人口;异文(相似 0.86) 第 362 行:遇火如空却不同

相关:LY.CAREER.HDY.11、LY.CAREER.YM.52

## LY.CAREER.ZSZ.09 六亲章·玄武当官奸与盗、两重官鬼必忧丧；若占官府反为吉，福厚官高名誉扬

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:玄武落在官鬼上，是奸和盗；两重官鬼，必有忧愁丧事；但如果占的是官府（的事），反而是吉：福气厚、官位高、名誉远扬。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** The Dark Tortoise on Pressure means treachery and theft, and two layers of Pressure bring worry and mourning; but if one divines about officialdom it is on the contrary auspicious: blessing thick, office high and fame spread.

**《周易尚占·六亲章》原文** — 《周易尚占》第 636 行 · *Zhouyi Shangzhan (Divination First in the Zhou Changes), "Chapter on the six relations", base text; Zhouyi Shangzhan, line 636*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 玄武当官奸与盗， | 玄武当了官（落在官鬼上），是奸和盗； | the Dark Tortoise on Pressure means treachery and theft; |
| 两重官鬼必忧丧， | 两重官鬼，必有忧愁丧事； | two layers of Pressure bring worry and mourning; |
| 若占官府反为吉， | 如果占的是官府（的事），反而是吉； | but if one divines about officialdom it is on the contrary auspicious; |
| 福厚官高名誉扬。 | 福气厚、官位高、名誉远扬。 | blessing thick, office high and fame spread. |

> **按**:这一句在「六亲章」里，前后讲的是家人、婚姻、讼事；「占官府」在这里指占做官、官场的事，不是占官司。
>
> **Note:** This line stands in the chapter on the six relations among lines about family, marriage and lawsuits; 'divining about officialdom' here means divining a career in office and not a lawsuit.

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮大全》:同文 第 3641 行:玄武当官奸与盗;同文 第 3641 行:两重官鬼必忧丧;同文 第 3641 行:若占官府反为吉;同文 第 3641 行:福厚官高名誉扬
- 《火珠林》:同文 第 1171 行:玄武当官奸与盗;同文 第 1171 行:两重官鬼必忧丧;同文 第 1171 行:若占官府反为吉;同文 第 1171 行:福厚官高名誉扬

相关:LY.CAREER.HDY.11、LY.CAREER.CA.12

## LY.CAREER.ZSZ.12 僧道章（周易尚占）·子动妻交还俗汉；世空身旺地行仙、身空不动头头道、应旺无魔处处禅；应偶世持生又合神清身静寿绵绵；负结财身动填还俗债；世应不和身妄动待驴年；本身太岁临身命即日逢神得正传；太岁火神加父母天边恩命定来宣

**规则(整理,不是原文)**:子孙发动、妻财交动，是要还俗的汉子；世空、身旺，是地上的行仙；身空而不动，事事都是道；应旺而没有魔，处处都是禅；应落偶位、世持住，又生又合，精神清明、身体安静，寿命绵长；忽然有负债纠结、财和身一起发动，免不了去偿还俗世的债和冤；世、应不和、身又乱动，想了身达命要等到驴年（永远等不到）；本身、太岁落在身和命上，当天就遇上神人、得到真传；太岁、火神加在父母上，天边的恩命一定会来宣布。

**Rule (our summary, not the source text):** Output moving with Wealth also moving: a man who will return to lay life; the Self void and the Body strong makes an earth-wandering immortal; the Body void and still, every single thing is the Way; the Other strong with no demon, Chan is everywhere; the Other on an even place and the Self holding, generating and combining, the spirit clear and the body at peace, life is long; if suddenly there is a debt-knot and Wealth and the Body move, one cannot avoid paying back worldly debts and grievances; with Self and Other at odds and the Body moving wildly, to finish with the body and reach one's destiny one must wait for the Year of the Donkey; the person himself and Tai Sui on the Body and one's fate, on that very day one meets a divine person and receives the true transmission; Tai Sui and the Fire spirit added to Resource, a favour commanded from the edge of heaven will surely be proclaimed.

**《周易尚占·僧道章》原文** — 《周易尚占》第 680 行 · *Zhouyi Shangzhan (Divination First in the Zhou Changes), "Chapter on monks and Daoists", base text; Zhouyi Shangzhan, line 680*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 子动妻交还俗汉， | 子孙发动、妻财交动，是要还俗的汉子； | Output moving and Wealth also moving: a man who will return to lay life; |
| 世空身旺地行仙， | 世空、身旺，是地上的行仙； | the Self void and the Body strong, an earth-wandering immortal; |
| 身空不动头头道， | 身空而不动，事事都是道； | the Body void and still, every single thing is the Way; |
| 应旺无魔处处禅。 | 应旺而没有魔，处处都是禅； | the Other strong with no demon, Chan is everywhere; |

**《周易尚占·僧道章》原文** — 《周易尚占》第 681 行 · *Zhouyi Shangzhan (Divination First in the Zhou Changes), "Chapter on monks and Daoists", base text; Zhouyi Shangzhan, line 681*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 应偶世持生又合， | 应落偶位、世持住，又生又合， | the Other on an even place and the Self holding, generating and combining, |
| 神清身静寿绵绵， | 精神清明、身体安静，寿命绵长。 | the spirit clear and the body at peace, life is long. |
| 忽然负结财身动， | 忽然有负债纠结、财和身一起发动， | If suddenly there is a debt-knot and Wealth and the Body move, |
| 未免填还俗债冤。 | 免不了要偿还俗世的债和冤。 | one cannot avoid paying back worldly debts and grievances. |

**《周易尚占·僧道章》原文** — 《周易尚占》第 682 行 · *Zhouyi Shangzhan (Divination First in the Zhou Changes), "Chapter on monks and Daoists", base text; Zhouyi Shangzhan, line 682*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 世应不和身妄动， | 世、应不和，身又乱动， | Self and Other at odds and the Body moving wildly, |
| 了身达命待驴年， | 想了身达命，要等到驴年。 | to finish with the body and reach one's destiny, wait for the Year of the Donkey. |
| 本身太岁临身命， | 本身、太岁落在身和命上， | the person himself and Tai Sui on the Body and one's fate, |
| 即日逢神得正传。 | 当天就遇上神人，得到真传。 | on that very day one meets a divine person and receives the true transmission. |

**《周易尚占·僧道章》原文** — 《周易尚占》第 683 行 · *Zhouyi Shangzhan (Divination First in the Zhou Changes), "Chapter on monks and Daoists", base text; Zhouyi Shangzhan, line 683*

| 原文 | 译文 | English |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 太岁火神加父母， | 太岁、火神加在父母上， | Tai Sui and the Fire spirit added to Resource, |
| 天边恩命定来宣， | 天边的恩命一定会来宣布。 | a favour commanded from the edge of heaven will surely be proclaimed. |
| 问予末后天机事， | 问我最后这件天机之事， | If you ask me about this last matter of heaven's secret, |
| 拈起笔来画个圈。 | 拈起笔来，画一个圈。 | I take up my brush and draw a circle. |

> **按**:「应偶世持生又合」卜筮大全作「应偶世奇生又合」（一个「持」一个「奇」）；「本身太岁临身命」卜筮大全作「木神太岁临身命」；末句「拈起笔来画个圈」卜筮大全作「拈笔书将一个缘」。「负结」「驴年」的说明同阐奥歌章（CA.31）。
>
> **Note:** 'The Other on an even place and the Self holding' reads in the Bushi Daquan 'the Other on an even place and the Self on an odd one' (a 'hold' for an 'odd'); 'the person himself and Tai Sui on the Body and one's fate' reads there 'the Wood spirit and Tai Sui on the Body and one's fate'; and the last line 'I take up my brush and draw a circle' reads there 'I take up my brush and write down a single yuan'. The remarks on 'debt-knot' and 'the Year of the Donkey' are as for the Chan'ao Ge (CA.31).

他本同文、异文(程序比对,只比本文):

- 《卜筮大全》:同文 第 3839 行:子动妻交还俗汉;同文 第 3839 行:世空身旺地行仙;同文 第 3839 行:身空不动头头道;同文 第 3839 行:应旺无魔处处禅
- 《火珠林》:同文 第 1278 行:子动妻交还俗汉;同文 第 1278 行:世空身旺地行仙;同文 第 1278 行:身空不动头头道;同文 第 1278 行:应旺无魔处处禅
- 《卜筮大全》:异文(相似 0.86) 第 3839 行:应偶世奇生又合;同文 第 3839 行:神清身静寿绵绵;同文 第 3839 行:忽然负结财身动;同文 第 3839 行:未免填还俗债冤
- 《火珠林》:异文(相似 0.86) 第 1278 行:应偶世奇生又合;同文 第 1278 行:神清身静寿绵绵;同文 第 1278 行:忽然负结财身动;异文(相似 0.86) 第 1278 行:未免填还俗债
- 《卜筮大全》:同文 第 3839 行:世应不和身妄动;同文 第 3839 行:了身达命待驴年;同文 第 3839 行:即日逢神得正传
- 《易林补遗》:同文 第 2559 行:世应不和身妄动
- 《火珠林》:同文 第 1278 行:世应不和身妄动;同文 第 1278 行:了身达命待驴年;同文 第 1278 行:即日逢神得正传
- 《卜筮大全》:同文 第 3839 行:太岁火神加父母;同文 第 3839 行:天边恩命定来宣;同文 第 3839 行:问予末后天机事
- 《火珠林》:同文 第 1278 行:太岁火神加父母;同文 第 1278 行:天边恩命定来宣;同文 第 1278 行:问予末后天机事

相关:LY.CAREER.CA.31、LY.CAREER.CA.32、LY.CAREER.CA.33
